```
### Added  - Cloud sync: sync your data between computers, end-to-end encrypted (E2EE): it’s encrypted before it leaves this computer, so remote storage holds only ciphertext, and only devices with the password can read it. - When the tabs don’t fit, scroll the tab bar sideways with the mouse wheel.  ### Fixed  - In a terminal, Home and End didn’t move to the start and end of the line when zsh on the server was set up with oh-my-zsh and the like. 
```

## [6.8.0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.7.0...rea-agents-6.8.0) (2026-10-25)  ### Highlights  - Build local agent environments from network captures, browser scenarios, or a prompt, then serve derived reads and evidence-gated writes and compare them with fresh application captures. - Inspect more shipped formats, including GPU code objects, BEAM, Python bytecode, Unity IL2CPP metadata, Godot PCK, PyInstaller, Node.js SEA, devicetree, and additional PE/ELF tables. - Keep large investigations focused with retained summaries, selected fact queries, configurable recovery limits, and row-oriented CLI exports. Complete Evidence remains available.  ### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES  - **Function analysis:** `analyze_function` now defaults to a retained summary. Set `detail: "complete"` for the previous inline dossier; the CLI retains its complete default and requires `--evidence_output` when selecting a summary. [#5660](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/5660). Thanks @CryptoJones, @gensokyo-dev, @RishTheFish21. - **Accessibility:** Accept JSON numbers and booleans in node `value`, alongside strings and null. Update consumers that previously assumed text-only values. [cc90771](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/cc90771af14f045538651615147d8bf86b576549), [#6759](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/6759), [#6467](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/6467). Thanks @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @mvanhorn. - **JSON Lines:** `--format jsonl` now emits object-collection rows followed by a `$rea: "jsonl/v1"`, `type: "metadata"` record; scalar selections and unsupported reports are refused. Parse control records separately, select a single supported collection when necessary, and use `--format json` for the previous whole-document output. See [JSON Lines framing](https://github.com/morluto/rea/blob/rea-agents-6.8.0/docs/cli.md#json-lines-record-output). [#5629](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/5629), [#5487](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/5487). Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @jayykernel, @vijaykiran06. - **Interface Builder:** Keyed-archive graph identities use decimal table UIDs instead of authored `objectID`/`object-id`. Treat authored IDs as attributes and update persisted graph lookups; ibtool and native NIBArchive identities keep their source semantics. [6fbe67d](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/6fbe67d66e3d716db69929503a3a232a7ada0860). - **Frida:** `instrument-with-frida` JSON returns the canonical Evidence directly. Read `normalized_result.cleanup_error` instead of the former top-level wrapper field. [48f700e](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/48f700ee35e0988ed973e5486dc9db33f2a5d944). - **Reconstruction proof:** ID-array proof records no longer establish verified or ready ledgers. Supply implementation/revision bindings and individual claim outcomes; handle inapplicable, unavailable, and failed proof diagnostics. [f8b65e7](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/f8b65e78723b1e2169f94dd1226198ea3139707e). - **Mach-O imports:** Handle a null import `name` when identity is ambiguous; use `raw_name` and `name_status` to distinguish observed expressions from exact symbols. [5d0d243](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/5d0d243a45c9132e1aff75d454ab53d86a74cc31), [169512a](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/169512a35bb015ff209168fb8bc1c9b31a2c678b). - **Node.js:** Use a runtime satisfying `^22.23.4 || ^24.22.1 || >=26.12.1`; Node 23 and 25 are unsupported. Upgrade Node before installing or updating REA; the installer does not upgrade it. [#6315](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/6315), [#6283](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/6283). Thanks @GamerDubz, @gensokyo-dev, @ShikamaruNaaraa. - **Browser storage:** Historical hashed storage remains readable, but comparisons stay unknown until both captures are recaptured in the new representation. [#6341](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/6341), [#6289](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/6289). Thanks @coygeek, @gongfpp. - **Environment serving:** `environment-serve` serves derived state by default, including write tools whose effects are confirmed by recorded evidence. Use `--replay-only` for stateless replay of manifest tools. Read stop-summary `state` instead of `state_evidence_id`. [#5351](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/5351), [#6158](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/6158). Thanks @earakely-scale, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **MCP schemas:** Input advertisements default to compact. Set `REA_MCP_INPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=full` for complete advertised guidance and nested constraints. [#5975](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/5975). Thanks @gensokyo-dev. - **Browser console arguments:** Accept null `argument_types` and `text_capture.status: "unavailable"` when the protocol argument inventory is omitted or malformed; preserve that uncertainty. [#6016](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/6016), [#5934](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/5934). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Reconstruction summaries:** `summary.by_evidence_authority` is keyed by Evidence authority; read the former source-state counts from required `summary.by_source_state`. Regenerate ledgers whose closure digests depended on the old projection. [#5875](https://github.com/morluto/rea/pull/5875), [#5838](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/5838). Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji. - **Swift bindings:** Conformance `type_name` and `protocol_name` may be null; consume typed `type_binding`/`protocol_binding` evidence. Replace the former Objective-C-specific import-binding type/schema with `MachoImportBinding`. [d0067ba](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/d0067ba34a39ea47dd7e11bb2e6169bc0938cc3f). - **WebSocket frames:** Accept null opcodes and preserve unknown frame facts; numeric opcodes are restricted to the four-bit protocol range. [0bc2b42](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/0bc2b42001f684beffcf47b70e507443b1a5d320). - **Resource-only inspection:** Evidence subjects and executable digests may be null. Use `resource_root` and each resource's reported provenance instead of requiring an executable target. [6a6adf7](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/6a6adf703fa44100643947d20fbc670f86260b1b). - **Browser timestamps:** Console and session-timeline timestamps may be null when the protocol supplies no finite/comparable time. Keep unknown time explicit in sorting and comparisons. [690fc51](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/690fc518540bae982038a74f47ef7781201785aa), [2a0410f](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/2a0410f1717b3126e8a95063d77dea66a597010a). - **Reference sources:** Read failed root enumeration from `rootFailure` rather than a synthetic failed child at `"."`; preserve the top-level source-graph limitation. [6296fdf](https://github.com/morluto/rea/commit/6296fdf560f1ac4aa55985558edaa98300aacdbc). - **Filesystem capture:** Accept `entry_unavailable` and `identity_changed` enumeration omission reasons in addition to the previous reason literals. ```
## Improvements - Subscription windows now use their own independent grid layout instead of being nested within a shared row grid.  ### Install  Download from [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai), or `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`. An installed magpie updates itself.  - **macOS (Apple Silicon):** `magpie-darwin-arm64.dmg` - **macOS (Intel):** `magpie-darwin-amd64.dmg` - **Windows:** `magpie-windows-amd64.exe` (ARM: `magpie-windows-arm64.exe`) - **Linux:** `magpie-linux-amd64` / `magpie-linux-arm64` (needs GTK 3 and WebKitGTK 4.1) - **Terminal only:** `magpie-cli-<os>-<arch>`  <!-- lang:zh -->  ## Improvements - 订阅窗口现在使用其独立的网格布局，而不是嵌套在共享的行网格中。  ### 安装  从 [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai) 下载，或运行 `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`。已安装的 magpie 会自动更新。    --- The same release, and the feed magpie updates from: https://github.com/yetone/magpie-releases/releases/tag/v0.1.1820 
```

### 2026-10-28T01:48:56Z [**nyakang/nyaterm**](https://github.com/nyakang/nyaterm)

```
## NyaTerm v2.0.0  NyaTerm 2.0.0 正式启用基于 Rust + GPUI 的原生桌面版本，取代此前的 Tauri/WebView 桌面实现。本次稳定版延续 SSH、本地终端、SFTP、RDP、VNC、隧道、AI 助手及云同步等核心能力，并整合预览阶段后的修复与完善。  ### ✨ 新增功能  - **原生桌面工作区正式上线**：提供原生窗口、终端和多会话工作区；RDP、VNC 与 MCP 辅助程序随桌面应用打包。 - **RDP 高级连接选项与诊断**：增加可配置的连接选项和渲染器诊断信息，便于排查兼容性问题（PR #999）。 - **AI 请求头配置**：支持为不同 AI 凭据单独配置自定义 HTTP 请求头，并完善编辑校验与请求应用逻辑（PR #1004）。 - **WSL 工作目录浏览**：支持通过 `\\wsl.localhost` 浏览 WSL 专属路径，并加强路径映射校验（PR #991）。 - **窗口装饰设置**：增加桌面窗口装饰偏好，并改善 Linux 窗口交互。  ### 🐛 问题修复  - 修复 RDP 远端连续复制文件时，本地剪贴板内容更新滞后或仍引用旧文件的问题，并保留复制文件的时间信息。 - 修复部分 RDP 剪贴板请求超时与辅助进程生命周期处理问题，改善断开和重连期间的稳定性。 - 补全外部 MCP 的终端显示模式处理（PR #1001）。 - 修复部分终端选择、会话信息弹窗及路径识别的交互边界问题。  ### ⚡ 性能与稳定性优化  - 优化云同步检查缓存、元数据读取和冲突处理，增强并发变更、自动同步和恢复场景的可靠性。 - 改善终端渲染帧、光标状态和远程桌面辅助进程的运行稳定性。  ### 🎨 界面与交互优化  - 优化云同步历史面板的内容格式与布局，并调整快照密码提示流程。 - 优化连接编辑器的设置分组及字段校验。  ### 👥 贡献者  感谢以下外部贡献者参与本次正式版前的完善： - @moonmicah - @litcc - @ccyylhj - @Coflys - @victorwon2001   ### ⚠️ 升级说明  **从 NyaTerm 1.x 升级到 2.0.0 前，请退出应用，并备份完整的数据目录、密钥和便携版程序目录。**  - **Windows 1.x 便携版需手动升级**：下载与设备架构一致的完整 ZIP，保留原有 `data/` 与密钥并替换全部程序文件，不能只覆盖 `NyaTerm.exe`；RDP、VNC、MCP 辅助程序和 ConPTY 必须一并更新。旧版内置更新器不能完成此次迁移。 - **旧 MSI 安装需手动迁移**：备份后卸载旧 MSI，再安装同架构的 NSIS `-setup.exe`，不要删除用户数据。 - **从 2.0 预览版迁入正式版**：先导出 `.nya` 备份，再在正式版恢复；预览版与正式版的数据目录不会自动合并。 - 如果升级失败，请恢复迁移前的完整备份，不要使用旧程序打开新版已写入的数据。 - Windows 版本要求 **Windows 10 / 11**，不再支持 Windows 7。  迁移步骤详见：[NyaTerm 2.0 迁移指南](https://nyaterm.app/docs/getting-started/migration-v2)。 
```

### 2026-10-22T11:27:21Z [**ejfkdev/ddc**](https://github.com/ejfkdev/ddc)

```
Highlights  - Non-BMP (supplementary-plane) names and literals now decode and match   correctly: `strings`, `findrefs`, `members` and getclass no longer lose   them to U+FFFD or fail to find them. - `disasm` accepts a full method descriptor to select one exact overload,   e.g. `'com.example.Foo.parse(Ljava/lang/String;)V'`; a bare method name   still lists every overload. - Memory: one-shot query processes use immediate allocator purging and a   byte-budgeted wave, cutting peak physical footprint by roughly 25-30%   on multi-dex APKs. Single-class getclass on a 398 MB APK went from   1.17 GB to about 0.87 GB peak. - Performance: cure 0.1.9 replaces repeated statement-subtree scans with   per-statement name/index tables. A 5k-line class drops from 5.4s to   0.22s in cure; full export of deepseek drops from 8.1s to about 3.0s   and weixin from 53.9s to about 40s, with byte-identical output.  Notes  - No breaking CLI changes. Default output changes only where previously   corrupt non-BMP text is now correct. - Benchmarks in the README/docs were refreshed and corrected: the previous   table was recorded on an earlier (pre-cure) version; full and `--fast`   are now separate columns. - `DDC_GET_TWO_TIER=1` is an experimental low-memory single-class path   (small closures only; automatic fallback to full parse). Off by default. - Dependencies: cure 0.1.9, jdc-core 0.2.17.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ejfkdev/ddc/compare/v0.1.32...v0.1.33 
```

# Vetta 0.5.68  ## 改进  - 设计助手做 App 界面不再套用固定的风格模板。以前点单、外卖、银行、天气这类面向用户的 App 都会被套进同几套后台风格，连做几份几乎同一个蓝、同一款字、同一种白卡片。现在助手先写下「按习惯会做成什么样」，再从这个产品独有的东西出发提出三个明显不同的方向（明暗、主色、字体至少两项不同）让你挑；靛蓝紫主色、被用烂的标题字、对比度不足、三个方向彼此太像或和最近几份设计撞车的，都会被打回重提。代价是开画前多一步，助手提案偶尔要改一两轮。 - 渐变、阴影、毛玻璃、等宽字、小号大写标签、第三种字体不再一律禁止：方向里写明理由就可以用，只在理由适用的地方用。图标方块、标题上方的小标签、编号、靛蓝紫这类一眼就是 AI 做的痕迹照旧不许。 - 手机界面不再被写成「桌面后台折叠版」（顶栏、筛选标签条、两列数字卡、两行列表），构图交还给设计方向；只保留触控尺寸、安全区、对比度这类硬要求。 - 新建设计的默认配色改成中性灰占位，不再是一眼能认出的靛蓝配色；助手没换掉就开画会被提醒。 - 设计可用的字体新增 Fraunces、Space Grotesk、Nunito、Syne、Cormorant Garamond 等十五款。  ## 其他  - 设计助手插件升到 0.7.4。用旧版做的 App 设计照常打开，原来的配色字体保留不动，只是要换风格时改为重新提方向。 
```

# Pebrel 2.3.1  ## English  This maintenance release restores comfortable terminal text spacing while keeping supported character-art logos in proportion. It also makes the default interface font explicit and updates the approved sponsor presentation.  Packages include Windows x64/ARM64 installers and portable ZIPs, macOS Apple Silicon/Intel builds, Linux x64 packages, an Android universal APK and the offline relay package. macOS, Linux and Android retain their Preview distribution labels.  ### Fixed  - Fix overly tight terminal text and list spacing by restoring the font height, baseline and default row padding from 2.2.2. Custom terminal fonts and explicit line-height settings remain available. ([#783](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/783)) - Fix stretched supported character-art logos, including the dense Braille/block and repeated-slash patterns used by Codex and Fastfetch, without compressing surrounding text or changing the terminal grid. Keep partially scrolled artwork complete and align selection, search highlights and link hit testing with its displayed position. ([#787](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/787)) - Fix the interface-font input to display the actual default, Geist, including after clearing a custom value. Interface and terminal font preferences remain independent. ([#783](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/783))  ### Improved  - Improve the Sponsors page and both READMEs with the approved AIAPI logo, bilingual introduction and visit action. Existing provider settings and credentials are unchanged. ([#789](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/789)) - Improve the README badge layout with the official Trendshift badge and the XERJ contributor badge, removing redundant technology badges. ([#782](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/782)) - Improve Linux CI dependency downloads by using Ubuntu's official archive while retaining package signature checks and the existing native test matrix. ([#787](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/787))  ## 中文  本维护版本恢复舒适的终端正文行距，同时保持受支持字符画标志的正确比例，并让界面默认字体显示更明确、同步已批准的赞助商展示。  提供 Windows x64/ARM64 安装器与便携 ZIP、macOS Apple Silicon/Intel 包、Linux x64 包、Android universal APK 及离线中继包。macOS、Linux 和 Android 继续保留 Preview 分发标记。  ### 修复  - 修复终端正文与列表行距过紧，恢复 2.2.2 的字体高度、基线和默认行间留白；保留自定义终端字体与显式行高设置。 ([#783](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/783)) - 修复受支持字符画标志被拉伸的问题，包括 Codex、Fastfetch 使用的密集盲文、块字符及连续斜线图案；不压缩周围正文，也不改变终端网格。滚动时保留仍应可见的图案，并让选区、搜索高亮与链接命中跟随实际显示位置。 ([#787](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/787)) - 修复界面字体输入框的默认值显示，未设置或清空自定义值后均显示真实默认字体 Geist；界面字体与终端字体仍独立设置。 ([#783](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/783))  ### 改进  - 改进应用赞助页与中英文 README，展示已批准的 AIAPI 标志、双语介绍及访问入口；不改变已有服务商设置与凭据。 ([#789](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/789)) - 改进 README 徽章布局，加入官方 Trendshift 徽章与 XERJ 贡献者徽章，移除重复的技术徽章。 ([#782](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/782)) - 改进 Linux CI 依赖下载，使用 Ubuntu 官方软件源，保留软件包签名校验与现有原生测试矩阵。 ([#787](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/787))  ## Contributors  <p>   <a href="https://github.com/Kuddev"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/292709043?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Kuddev" title="Kuddev" /></a> </p>  Thanks to [Kuddev](https://github.com/Kuddev) for the fixes, documentation and release integration.  感谢 [Kuddev](https://github.com/Kuddev) 对本版修复、文档与发布整合的贡献。  ## SHA256  text b40bcd922e7340b51893c247695b4b286d330b4e7b27238694a4aee3c345f94b  Pebrel-v2.3.1-android-universal-preview.apk 516045e26444aa161e4abef7a74cf7e4dbcbe4eecd059c5bfb923da98e33f786  Pebrel-v2.3.1-linux-x64-preview.AppImage 59bff08d2f07f044029b15c50bb37c2e6b0dca1bb9f82611a80ad03c221f4d85  Pebrel-v2.3.1-linux-x64-preview.deb de7586f1afc0d7abea1a4ba20805a7c030f9d281570d2fb25e01a44146ee0808  Pebrel-v2.3.1-linux-x64-preview.tar.gz 0260651ee08fdb384a5441bb3db836690f4296dd6b45ba4a45e360831f3d8a2f  Pebrel-v2.3.1-macos-arm64-preview.dmg 2043effa1328dcd5d7926a4139668fb85a416a69654308ea816de67e8c564a6d  Pebrel-v2.3.1-macos-x64-preview.dmg a86bc7d886c388089be3208f51d619c47bef29014b9688171f553eb9f20a30fd  Pebrel-v2.3.1-relay-manual.tar.gz 0b64c4d5cf4c60c7fa40d3f340105b97889cf08d25bd7f9c66079490950c4ee6  Pebrel-v2.3.1-windows-arm64-setup.exe 583aa3a42b78ed43df732c0d17894482807a6ec5911264e902b5009f7e876605  Pebrel-v2.3.1-windows-arm64.zip 9e21164f493a1b05fcdc3102baf316371e099e5d0532f5f8a26b2bfae9f0472b  Pebrel-v2.3.1-windows-x64-setup.exe e12dcfb573ac5d20e06b234202f1065af12b04979311a2ca10a97969a92f70e5  Pebrel-v2.3.1-windows-x64.zip

### 2026-10-27T00:47:37Z [**anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop**](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop)

# 中文  本次 Next 更新升级上游至 DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.3，新增按需启用 Computer Use 的 `/computer-use` 命令、数据目录状态与备份、独立预启动首次设置窗口及侧栏底部图标布局设置，并修复 Windows 管理员权限沙箱启动失败、Linux 图像处理崩溃和界面恢复等问题。  DSH NEXT 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  **上游版本：** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.3](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.1-rc.3)。  ## 新增与改进  - **Computer Use 按需加载:** 新增 `/computer-use [off|message]` 斜杠命令和插件详情页“按需命令加载（/computer-use）”开关（默认开启），仅在当前会话按需启用时才暴露 Computer Use 工具与系统提示词，避免常规编码会话产生额外 Token 开销。 - **数据目录管理与备份:** 桌面设置显示当前数据目录及来源（应用内选择、`DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` 或默认目录），应用内选择固定保存在系统应用数据目录中；启动时若目标目录变化且原目录仍有数据或无法访问，会先弹窗确认。新增“备份”操作，可暂停 Host 并生成可直接切换使用的带时间戳数据目录备份。 - **独立首次设置窗口:** 首次启动向导改为在启动 Host 前以轻量独立窗口打开，完成或跳过后再启动 Host 并进入主窗口，避免冷启动等待；向导与桌面设置控件样式同步对齐官方设置界面。 - **侧栏与设置界面:** 桌面设置新增“侧边栏底部图标横向排列”和“多行图标均衡排列”开关，支持紧凑并排与多行均分对齐；设置弹窗左侧导航栏支持纵向滚动，并在“检查更新”旁新增“更新公告”链接。 - **Linux 内置运行时:** Linux 安装包（x64 与 ARM64）现已内置 `primary-runtime`、`office-skills` 与 WebAssembly Office 引擎，开箱支持 Office 文档技能。  ## 修复  - **Windows 沙箱启动:** 以管理员拆分令牌启动安装版应用时自动降权重启（可通过 `--next-keep-elevated` 保留提权），避免 Chromium GPU／渲染进程沙箱创建失败导致无提示退出；若沙箱进程仍无法启动，会弹出原生诊断对话框说明错误码、关闭管理员运行的方法及临时 `--no-sandbox` 参数。 - **Linux 稳定性与打包:** Linux 改用 WebAssembly 版 `sharp` 处理图像与图标，修复原生 `libvips` 与 Electron GLib 符号冲突导致的 Host `SIGSEGV` 崩溃；修复 `landlock-run` 未从 `app.asar` 解包的问题，并在 Linux 下隐藏不支持的应用内检查更新入口。 - **界面恢复:** 渲染进程异常退出时保留正在运行的 Host 并先重试重新加载页面（最多 3 次），仅在重试耗尽后进入恢复助手；健康检查简化为检测页面挂载状态，避免历史记录加载较慢或短暂断连触发循环刷新。 - **插件管理与终端命令:** 升级内置 `pnpm` 至 11.28.5，修复卸载插件时偶发卡死的问题；应用内终端执行 `dsh plugin` 命令时始终使用内置 `pnpm` 并关闭误导性的 `pnpm self-update` 提示。减少插件开关切换与恢复助手操作时的重复扫描，提升响应速度。 - **更新安装与存储兼容:** 修复更新重启时若 Host 关闭超时可能跳过启动已准备好的安装器的问题；容忍 PowerShell 5.1 重写 `workspace.json` 等 JSON 存储文件时添加的 UTF-8 BOM，修复 Windows 下 `/C:/...` 文件 URL 打开路径、非字符串草稿导致空白会话页及大型工具参数流式解析高 CPU 占用的问题；固定 Electron 44.7.0 以修复 `app.asar` 读取缓慢导致的启动超时。  ## 下载  从本页 **Assets** 下载对应平台的安装包：  | 平台 | 文件 | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  可使用本页 `SHA256SUMS.txt` 核验全部安装包。  ## 使用提示  - 请先退出旧版应用并备份 `~/.dsh`（或在“设置 → 桌面设置”中使用“备份”），安装后验证常用插件和工作流程。 - 如需通过应用内终端管理 `desktop` Profile 的插件，请先启动 Desktop 完成该 Profile 的初始化。 - 从 2.0.17-next 或更早版本升级时，建议手动下载安装；旧版应用内更新可能无法完成下载。 - Office 文档技能首次使用时将随包运行时安装到 `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime`，需要额外磁盘空间。 - 默认与旧稳定版共用 `~/.dsh`；Profile 不隔离同一目录中的会话、账号和凭据。 - 可在“设置 → 桌面设置”或恢复助手中切换数据目录，也可通过绝对路径 `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` 隔离 DSH 数据（应用内选择优先于环境变量），但不会因此隔离 AA 的默认共享登录与设备绑定状态。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.19-next...v2.0.20-next) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT 已取代原稳定版通道。  ---  # English  This Next release upgrades upstream to DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.3, adds the `/computer-use` command for on-demand Computer Use, data directory status and backups, a standalone pre-Host first-run setup window, and sidebar footer layout settings, and fixes Windows elevated sandbox launch failures, Linux image-processing crashes, and renderer recovery.  DSH NEXT is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  **Upstream version:** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.3](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.1-rc.3).  ## New and improved  - **On-demand Computer Use:** Added the `/computer-use [off|message]` slash command and an "On-demand command loading (/computer-use)" toggle on the Computer Use plugin detail page (enabled by default), exposing Computer Use tools and system prompt guidance only in sessions where it is enabled to avoid extra token overhead in ordinary coding sessions. - **Data directory management and backup:** Desktop Settings now shows the active data directory and how it was chosen (chosen in app, `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME`, or default), persisted under the system application-data directory across restarts. If a launch resolves a different directory while the previous one still holds data or cannot be reached, Next asks before initializing. Added a "Back up" action that pauses the Host and copies the data directory into a timestamped backup folder that can be selected directly. - **Standalone first-run setup:** First-launch onboarding now opens in a lightweight standalone window before starting the Host process, then boots the Host once and opens the main window after completion or skip, avoiding the cold-start wait. Onboarding and Desktop Settings controls are aligned with the official Settings UI. - **Sidebar and Settings UI:** Added "Arrange sidebar footer icons in a row" and "Balance multi-row icon layout" toggles in Desktop Settings for compact horizontal and balanced multi-row footer icons. Enabled vertical scrolling on the Settings left navigation rail and added a "Release notes" link beside "Check for updates". - **Linux bundled runtime:** Linux packages (x64 and ARM64) now bundle `primary-runtime`, `office-skills`, and the WebAssembly Office engine for out-of-the-box Office document skills.  ## Fixes  - **Windows sandbox launch:** Packaged builds launched with an elevated split token on Windows now automatically restart with the normal desktop token (`--next-keep-elevated` keeps intentional elevation) so Chromium GPU and renderer sandboxes do not exit silently. If a sandboxed process still cannot launch, Next shows a native diagnostic dialog with the error code, instructions to disable Run as Administrator, and the temporary `--no-sandbox` workaround. - **Linux stability and packaging:** Switched Linux image processing to WebAssembly `sharp` to prevent Host `SIGSEGV` crashes caused by GLib symbol conflicts between native `libvips` and Electron; unpacked `landlock-run` from `app.asar` on Linux and hid unsupported in-app update controls. - **Renderer recovery:** When the renderer exits unexpectedly, Next now preserves the running Host and retries reloading the main document up to three times before falling back to the recovery assistant. Simplified renderer health checks to a mounted React shell so slow history loads or brief disconnects do not trigger reload loops. - **Plugin management and terminal commands:** Upgraded bundled `pnpm` to 11.28.5 to fix hangs during `pnpm remove`; terminal `dsh plugin` commands now always use the bundled `pnpm` and suppress the misleading `pnpm self-update` notice. Reduced redundant plugin scans during toggles and recovery operations. - **Update installation and storage compatibility:** Launched the staged update installer before forced exit even if Host shutdown times out during an update restart; tolerated leading UTF-8 BOMs in JSON storage files such as `workspace.json` rewritten by PowerShell 5.1; fixed `/C:/...` file-URL path opening on Windows, non-string draft values blanking the session view, and high CPU during large streamed tool arguments; pinned Electron 44.7.0 to fix slow `app.asar` traversal timeouts.  ## Downloads  Choose the matching installer from **Assets** below:  | Platform | File | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.20-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  Use `SHA256SUMS.txt` from this release to verify all installers.  ## Usage notes  - Quit the previous version and back up `~/.dsh` (or use **Back up** in **Settings → Desktop settings**) before installing. Verify your usual plugins and workflows afterward. - Before managing plugins for the `desktop` Profile from the bundled terminal, launch Desktop to initialize that Profile. - When upgrading from 2.0.17-next or earlier, download and install manually; in-app updates in those versions may not complete the download. - The Office skills install the bundled runtime under `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime` on first use, requiring additional disk space. - Defaults to the shared `~/.dsh` home. Profiles do not isolate sessions, accounts, or credentials within that home. - Change the data directory in **Settings → Desktop settings** or the recovery assistant, or set `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` to an absolute path to isolate DSH data (an in-app choice takes precedence over the environment variable); this does not isolate AA’s default shared sign-in and device-binding state.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.19-next...v2.0.20-next) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT replaces the former Stable edition channel.

### 2026-10-24T10:14:57Z [**ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI**](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI)

```
自 v0.16.1 以来，dsh-TUI 跟上 DSH `0.2.1-rc.3` 验证线，加入独立 headless CLI、Windows 离线安装器、 `/settings` 分页与插件底栏字段，并让 TUI 跟随会话的工作目录。  ## 亮点  - **独立 headless CLI（`dst`）** — `dst run`、`-p`、`dst cli` 不加载 React / Ink / channel / TUI 偏好即可被脚本驱动，`dst dsh` 原样转交官方参数；随 npm 包分发，`src/` 零改动。自动化适配器当前只验证 DSH `0.2.0-rc.2`，使用官方 headless 的持久化与恢复规则，不承诺与任意 TUI preset / fork 会话互通，也不是逐 token 正文流。([#1560](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1560) by @T-Auto) - **Windows 离线安装器** — Release 增加 `dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe`：自带轻量 Node 运行时（系统已有满足版本约束的 Node 则复用）、免管理员、把 npm 全局路径追加到 `HKCU` 的 `PATH`、profile 落在 `%USERPROFILE%\.dsh\profiles\dsh-tui`，卸载不触碰 `.dsh` 核心数据。暂未做代码签名，首次运行会有 SmartScreen 提示；旧的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip` 与便携包继续并存。([#1554](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1554) by @FlameTN7) - **`/settings` 分页** — 设置屏分成「常规 + 插件」页签；不声明 `page` 的旧插件区块并入常规页，只有一页时不显示页签条。同时修掉 dsh-tui 自己的设置区块会因注册表竞态从屏上消失的问题。([#1619](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1619) by @ccch1mneyyy) - **插件底栏字段逐项可控** — `statusBar.pluginFieldsOrder` 决定显示哪些插件字段与链内顺序，默认 `*` 表示全部（含以后注册的）；高级行从 `*` 进入后原样保存仍写回 `*`，不会把「还没见过的字段」钉死。([#1628](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1628) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **插件侧栏面板可复用宿主渲染** — `TuiPanelProps.ui` 新增 `ui.Markdown` 与 `ui.CodeBlock`，插件不必自己拼围栏、算表格宽度。([#1652](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1652) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **会话搜索实验包进入 `/plugin list`** — 官方 session-search 包的开关与用法可直接看到，检索卡标题显示查询词；极简模式仍只提供 shell（有意与上游该包「含 minimal」的描述不同，保持 Minimal 对用户的单 shell 承诺）。([#1707](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1707) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **启动页多行草稿带到聊天页** — 启动页用 Shift/Option+Enter 换行后，未发送的文字、光标与图片在切到聊天页时保留，同一批输入中随后的按键按原序重放。([#1703](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1703) by @CikeSeven) - **会话状态与完成提醒** — 经 OSC 7501 向终端报告 `idle` / `working` / `blocked` / `done` / `error`，跨 SSH、容器与多路复用器可用（不包 tmux/screen passthrough）；Windows 上 `notificationMethod` 新增 `windows` 档，用系统原生 toast 提醒完成（点击跳回尚未包含）。([#1561](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1561) by @zerodegress；[#1574](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1574) by @baobaolaodie) - **跟随会话工作目录** — 上游 `working_directory` 或 Git worktree 把会话切到别的目录后，状态栏、Git 分支、`@` 补全、`!` 命令、技能命令与已加载上下文一起跟随，项目归属不变；已知限制是 cd 之前渲染的工具卡里的相对路径，点击时按当前目录解析。([#1733](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1733) by @MomentDerek；[#1634](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1634) by @baobaolaodie) - **会话管理页统一显示项目路径** — 路径放在来源标签下方、横跨两栏，DSH 不再缺目录、外部会话不再逐条重复；超长路径最多四行，可悬停看完整值。([#1264](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1264) by @xuzijian2019) - **DSH 验证线升到 `0.2.1-rc.3`** — 主适配线与开发树、CI alpha-compat、验证常量同批前移，`0.2.1-rc.2` 继续作为已验证兼容线。([#1736](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1736) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **插件市场元数据** — 随包提供中英文本地化的市场标题/描述、方形像素鲸鱼图标与 `dsh-plugin` 发现关键字。([#1677](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1677) by @ccch1mneyyy)  ## 需要留意的行为变化  - **`/team` 只报告状态** — `/team on|off` 不再是启停入口，只接受 `status`；需要启停时改用 `/plugin enable|disable`，旧写法会返回迁移提示。Agent Teams 仍是实验特性，默认关闭；`/plugin list` 里各实验功能的提示也改成直接说明它的作用。([#1604](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1604)、[#1734](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1734) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **`sidePanel.panels` 的显式列表现在是精确集合** — 写过显式 CSV 的用户升级后，之后新注册的插件面板不会自动出现，而是在 `/settings` 里显示为未勾选行，点一下加回。默认值 `*` 仍表示全部已注册面板；临时面板的显示/隐藏随之改成运行态，结构上进不了持久设置。([#1683](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1683)、[#1695](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1695) by @Lucas2011wastaken)  ## 修复  - **输入、选择与复制**：Option+方向键按词编辑、Esc 先消费本地层再中断回合、rewind 重复同一段文本时重新回填草稿、退出时选择可恢复且终端输入干净、复制内容与高亮留在锚定面板内、软换行代码块复制不丢内容。([#1657](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1657)、[#1659](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1659)、[#1680](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1680)、[#1693](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1693)、[#1661](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1661) by @ccch1mneyyy；[#1670](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1670) by @MomentDerek) - **会话与持久化**：detached fork 保留父链、失败的 resume 预检释放预留、频道 token 留在当前 home、已结算回复 / 目标页脚 / 子代理失败保持一致、工作区与会话管理器统计同一批可见会话、损坏挂载账本给出可操作处置、存储打包行预算与发布批次按版本精确匹配。([#1654](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1654)、[#1656](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1656)、[#1653](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1653)、[#1678](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1678)、[#1691](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1691)、[#1694](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1694) by @ccch1mneyyy；[#1581](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1581) by @baobaolaodie) - **渲染与交互**：diff 展开视图折行、`/btw` 分屏无法渲染时回退浮层、装饰动画时钟隔离、面板长正文限高且保留问卷说明全文、面板 / 状态行 / diff 宽度与原始颜色一致、sixel 光标漂移先检测再停放、Windows Terminal 内 chalk truecolor 提升、被卡住的回合仍可 Esc 且发送失败可见、多行插件命令输出保留在本地。([#1602](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1602) by @baobaolaodie；[#1648](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1648)、[#1672](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1672) by @Lucas2011wastaken；[#1658](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1658)、[#1681](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1681)、[#1689](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1689)、[#1690](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1690)、[#1692](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1692)、[#1621](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1621) by @ccch1mneyyy；[#1643](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1643) by @xuzijian2019) - **运行时、更新与后端**：开发模式需显式 opt-in React、启动器解析包根并限制堆快照文件、拒绝已确认不支持的 Node 版本、开发缓存按真实输入校验、更新器在超时与取消时接管安装进程树、发布年龄排除合并为一条区间、effort 选项取自当前模型路由、保留会话 effort 并给 TPS 采样设下限、暴露写入锁。([#1655](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1655)、[#1679](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1679)、[#1682](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1682)、[#1686](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1686)、[#1687](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1687)、[#1688](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1688)、[#1696](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1696) by @ccch1mneyyy；[#1607](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1607) by @MomentDerek)  ## 兼容与升级  - **DSH 主适配线**：`0.2.1-rc.3`；`0.2.1-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.1`、`0.2.0-rc.2` 以及更早的验证线仍在契约内，升级 DSH 后首次启动会按已装版本报告版本线，契约外版本打漂移警告。 - **从 v0.16.1 升级**：程序内 `/update`，或   `npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.17.0`。 - **Windows 安装方式两套并存**：`dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe` 面向默认布局（不读取自定义 `DSH_HOME`），无代码签名；`dsh-tui-setup.zip` 与各平台 `dsh-tui-standalone-*` 便携包仍随 Release 分发，`SHA256SUMS` 覆盖全部资产。 - **`dst` 与 TUI 共用同一个包**：普通启动仍进 TUI，只有 `run`、`-p`、`cli` 走无界面 CLI；自动化适配器当前只验证 DSH `0.2.0-rc.2`。  ## 安装  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.17.0   偏好一键安装的用户可下载 Release 中的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`（解压后按平台运行 `install.sh` / `install.ps1`） 或 Windows 离线安装程序 `dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe`；便携包解压即用。   <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.17.0 -->  ## What's Changed * docs(profile): write ### 2026-10-26T02:10:08Z [**briqt/moke**](https://github.com/briqt/moke)

```
 ### 新增与优化 - **内置 Tailscale**：支持官方与自建控制服务器，可在应用内连接 tailnet 中的主机。 - **代理连接**：支持 HTTP / HTTPS / SOCKS4 / SOCKS5，可为主机选择使用的网络。 - **会话管理**：支持分别编辑会话标题和前缀，并按当前目录分组。 - **tmux**：改进会话状态识别、选择、新建与管理。 - **交互优化**：改善触摸滚动、名称编辑、分组标题和长列表操作。  ### 修复 - 修复终端粘贴、光标键和连接期间的尺寸同步。 - 修复网络登录恢复、连接退出及文件传输相关问题。  ### 安装要求 - Android 11+、arm64-v8a，统一提供一个全功能安装包。 - Android 7–10 不再支持；旧版可能仍提示更新，但无法安装此版本。 - 使用 mosh 或 tmux 时，远端需安装相应程序。 - 经代理使用 mosh 时，需 SOCKS5 服务端支持 UDP 转发；HTTP、HTTPS 和 SOCKS4 代理不能承载 mosh。 
```

### 2026-10-25T12:50:00Z [**OpenMinis/OpenMinis**](https://github.com/OpenMinis/OpenMinis)

```
**[English](#english) · [中文](#中文)**  ## English  ### New features  - **Added Settings > Network** — Tailscale (one-tap sign-in), upstream proxies (HTTP or SOCKS5) and per-scope routes for model requests, MCP, the shell, WebViews and backups, with Network Diagnostics and "Restore Direct Connections". Every request the app makes follows them, and each provider's page has its own Network row. In the shell, programs that ignore proxy variables (ssh, nc, database clients) follow the routes too, for TCP, UDP and DNS, and tailnet names (MagicDNS, short names like "nas") resolve there. Small pills under the home title show whether Tailscale and the proxy are up. - **Added in-app updates with Stable and Beta channels** — Minis checks for a new version when you open it (switch it off in About), shows a dot on Settings › About when one is waiting, lets you skip a version, and verifies the download before it installs. Choose Beta in About › Update channel to get test builds. - **Added questions from the assistant** — the assistant can ask on an in-chat card (options, confirmation, multi-question wizard) and waits for your answer. In the background the question arrives as a notification you can answer in place, and a pending question can still be answered after the app restarts. - **Added provider quota & balance** — remaining balance or subscription usage on the provider page, in the model picker and Model Groups, and in the composer. Supports DeepSeek, Moonshot, SiliconFlow, OpenRouter, Kimi Code, relay services (Sub2API / New API / OpenAI-style) and Claude / ChatGPT subscriptions. - **Added a Usage page** — Today / 7 days / 30 days / All, with totals, a stacked trend chart and a drill-down by provider and model. - **Improved long agent runs** — old reasoning, large tool outputs and old steps are trimmed a little at a time before a summary is needed, so a long task stays within the context window. The turn limit per run is now 500 (was 200). - **Added sub agent cards** — they show the task, its tool calls and latest output, with Steer and Stop. Up to 6 chats, and 3 sub agents per chat, can run at once. - **Improved scheduled tasks** — a chat with a scheduled follow-up stays active between runs; a pill under the chat title counts down in the last minute before a run, and its long-press menu has "Run now". A timed task fires on time while Minis is open. - **Added Search in Chat** — find text in the open chat from the "…" menu, a long-press or a text selection, with matches highlighted. Session search groups results by folder and matches any of several keywords. - **Added Share Chat…** — share any messages as one long image or a PDF with searchable text. - **Improved groups on the home screen** — a group's avatar is a tile of its newest chats, a comet runs round it while one of them is working, and an unread reply shows a red dot on its chat and on the group. - **Added folding of consecutive tool calls** into one "Ran N tools" row that opens on tap (on by default; Settings › Appearance › Tool Calls). - **Added Stop choices** — "Stop Current Tool Only / Stop All" when sub agents or scheduled tasks are running; long-press the stop button to choose whether it confirms first. - **Added a "Show current model" button** to the model picker that jumps to the chat's group or model in a long list. Hidden models are collected in a "Hidden models (N)" group, and any model can be deleted from its long-press menu. - Added browser sign-in (OAuth) for remote MCP servers, and search and Enable All / Disable All in the in-chat MCP sheet. - Added editing of saved backup servers, and ZIP64 for backups over 4 GB or 65,535 files. - Also added: turning environment variables off; Codex speed tiers and GPT-6.1 Sol; a per-provider response timeout for slow local models; read-aloud volume up to 200%; "Show full thinking"; `<br>` and simple HTML in Markdown; minis-config for Network and more settings; Italian, Dutch, Hungarian, Czech and Persian.  ### Fixes  - Fixed crashes: running out of memory mappings in a long session, very large files, a refused foreground service, the first shell start on Android 8-10, an unavailable file picker, and copying a very large text preview (it now suggests Share). - Fixed Stop for a shell command that is still waiting (delay countdown, queue, sandbox boot): Stop now cancels it before it starts. Fixed a short freeze when pressing Stop. - Fixed provider changes on an open chat: a new API key, API type or base URL takes effect on the next turn. Fixed the context window of relay and custom endpoints, which now use the size the endpoint reports. Refresh model list now reports its result and falls back to the built-in models. Tool-only turns now carry `"content": ""` for OpenAI-compatible providers that require it. - Fixed model handling: DeepSeek tool calls written into thinking and empty replies are retried; an empty reply from a model-group member switches to the next member; "Thinking off" on Claude 5 turns thinking off; fixed Gemini 3+ thinking settings and Anthropic refusals. - Fixed sign-in getting stuck after authorizing on some devices (e.g. Huawei). Fixed Tailscale browser sign-in retries, and "Sign Out and Clear" now signs the device out of the tailnet. - Fixed network routes when the local entry of a proxy or Tailscale is down: requests on that route now fail closed. - Fixed traffic through a Tailscale exit node: model requests, the shell, the browser and MCP now leave through the exit node over IPv4 when IPv6 cannot, local addresses stay on the device, and an offline exit node blocks the connection. - Fixed reading position: while you scroll up, the text you are reading stays in place as a reply grows or the keyboard opens. Fixed scrolling interrupting IME voice typing. A streaming thinking block follows its newest line, and a Search in Chat jump keeps its position while a reply streams. - Fixed the shell: garbled Chinese output, git in the sandbox, and hangs in `yes … | head` and busybox `ip addr`. - Fixed backup and restore: restore applies the backup's settings and API keys, OAuth logins are restored completely, local backups keep compact records, and iOS can read Android backups. - Fixed Bailian, iFlytek and Doubao speech recognition for each service's current protocol, and a late AI correction overwriting newer dictation. - Fixed Select All / Deselect All during a search: they apply to the results shown. - Improved battery use: decorative animations pause while Minis is in the background. - Fixed one unreadable encrypted store wiping every saved credential. - Fixed `$5` and `$HOME` rendering as formulas, tables nested in lists, deleting a symlink deleting its target, and the "Deep Thinking" title's language.  ### Polish  - The time under a reply shows its date for earlier days. - The model-fallback notice, compaction progress and Stop choice now use the iOS style; a large chat fades in its messages when opened. - The floating capsule stays fully on screen and remembers its position per orientation. - Updated the About tagline: an AI agent on your device; your keys and files stay here, and conversations go only to the AI provider you choose.  Full changelog: https://github.com/OpenMinis/OpenMinis/compare/1.14...1.15  ---  ## 中文  ### 新功能  - **新增「设置 > 网络」** —— 支持 Tailscale（一键登录）、上游代理（HTTP 或 SOCKS5），以及按范围设置路由（模型请求、MCP、Shell、WebView、备份），附带网络诊断和「恢复直连」。App 发出的所有请求都遵循这些设置，每个服务商页面也有自己的「网络」一行。Shell 中不读代理变量的程序（ssh、nc、数据库客户端）同样按路由走，TCP、UDP 和 DNS 都包括在内，也能解析 tailnet 名称（MagicDNS，以及「nas」这样的短名）。首页标题下方的小标签显示 Tailscale 和代理的状态。 - **新增应用内更新，支持正式版 / 测试版渠道** —— 打开 Minis 时自动检查新版本（可在「关于」里关闭），有新版本时在「设置 › 关于」上显示小红点，可以跳过某个版本，下载的安装包会先校验再安装。在「关于 › 更新渠道」选择测试版即可收到测试版本。 - **新增助手提问** —— 助手可以在对话里用卡片提问（单选、确认、多题向导）并等待回答。App 在后台时问题以通知形式出现，可直接在通知里作答；App 重启后仍可继续回答待回答的问题。 - **新增服务商额度与余额** —— 在服务商页面、模型选择器、模型组和输入框上方显示剩余余额或订阅用量。支持 DeepSeek、Moonshot、SiliconFlow、OpenRouter、Kimi Code、中转站（Sub2API / New API / OpenAI 兼容）以及 Claude / ChatGPT 订阅。 - **新增用量页面** —— 支持今天 / 7 天 / 30 天 / 全部，带汇总数字、堆叠趋势图，并可按服务商和模型下钻。 - **优化了长任务的上下文管理** —— 在需要摘要压缩之前，先逐步清理旧的思考、卸载大的工具输出、合并旧步骤，让长任务保持在上下文窗口内。每次运行的轮数上限提高到 500（原为 200）。 - **新增子代理卡片** —— 显示任务、工具调用和最新输出，可以引导或停止。最多可同时运行 6 个对话，每个对话最多 3 个子代理。 - **优化了定时任务** —— 有定时任务的会话在两次运行之间保持运行中；下次运行前的最后一分钟，对话标题下方的胶囊会倒计时，长按菜单可以「立即执行」。Minis 打开时定时任务准时触发。 - **新增聊天内搜索** —— 从「…」菜单、长按或选中文字即可在当前对话中搜索，匹配处高亮。会话搜索按文件夹分组显示结果，支持多个关键词。 - **新增分享会话** —— 任选消息，导出为一张长图或可搜索文字的 PDF。 - **优化了首页分组** —— 分组头像由最近的会话拼成宫格，组内有会话在运行时边缘有彗星环绕，有未读回复时会话和分组上显示红点。 - **新增连续工具调用折叠**，合并为一行「运行了 N 个工具」，点击展开（默认开启，可在 设置 › 外观 › 工具调用 中修改）。 - **新增停止选项** —— 有子代理或定时任务在运行时，可选「仅停止当前工具 / 全部停止」；长按停止按钮可选择是否先确认。 - **模型选择器新增「定位到当前模型」按钮**，在长列表中直接跳到当前的分组或模型。已隐藏的模型收进「已隐藏模型 (N)」，长按任意模型都可以删除。 - 新增远程 MCP 服务器的浏览器登录（OAuth），对话中的 MCP 面板新增搜索和全部启用 / 停用。 - 新增编辑已保存的备份服务器；超过 4 GB 或 65,535 个文件的备份使用 ZIP64 格式。 - 其他新增：环境变量可停用；Codex 速度档位与 GPT-6.1 Sol；可为慢速本地模型单独设置响应等待时间；朗读音量最高 200%；「显示完整思考」；Markdown 支持 `<br>` 和简单 HTML；minis-config 支持网络等更多设置；新增意大利语、荷兰语、匈牙利语、捷克语和波斯语。  ### 修复  - 修复了多处闪退：长会话耗尽内存映射、超大文件、系统拒绝前台服务、Android 8-10 首次启动 Shell、文件选择器不可用，以及复制超大文本预览（现在会提示改用分享）。 - 修复了停止对仍在等待中的 Shell 命令（延迟倒计时、排队、沙盒启动中）无效的问题，现在停止会在命令开始前取消它；修复了点停止时的短暂卡顿。 - 修复了已打开对话中修改服务商后不生效的问题：更换 API Key、API 类型或 Base URL 后，下一轮即生效。修复了中转站和自定义端点的上下文窗口，现在使用端点返回的数值。刷新模型列表现在会说明结果，并可回退到内置模型。只调用工具的回合现在带上 `"content": ""`，兼容要求此字段的 OpenAI 兼容服务商。 - 修复了模型相关问题：DeepSeek 把工具调用写进思考或返回空回复时自动重试；模型组成员返回空回复时切换到下一个成员；Claude 5 关闭思考时正确关闭；修复了 Gemini 3+ 的思考参数和 Anthropic 拒绝回答的处理。 - 修复了部分设备（如华为）授权后登录卡住的问题。修复了 Tailscale 浏览器登录的重试；「退出并清除」现在会让设备退出 tailnet。 - 修复了代理或 Tailscale 本地入口不可用时的路由问题：该路由上的请求现在会直接失败并阻断。 - 修复了经 Tailscale 出口节点的连接：模型请求、Shell、浏览器和 MCP 在出口节点无法使用 IPv6 时改走 IPv4，本机地址始终留在设备上，出口节点离线时连接会被阻断。 - 修复了阅读位置问题：向上翻阅时，回复变长或键盘弹出，正在看的文字保持原位。修复了滚动打断输入法语音输入的问题。流式思考内容跟随最新一行，聊天内搜索跳转后在回复流式输出时保持位置。 - 修复了 Shell 问题：中文输出乱码、沙盒中的 git，以及 `yes … | head` 和 busybox `ip addr` 卡住。 - 修复了备份与恢复：恢复时应用备份中的设置和 API Key，OAuth 登录完整恢复，本地备份记录保持精简，iOS 可以读取 Android 备份。 - 修复了阿里云百炼、讯飞和豆包语音识别，适配各自最新协议；修复了迟到的 AI 纠正覆盖更新听写内容的问题。 - 修复了搜索时「全选 / 取消全选」的范围，现在只作用于显示的结果。 - 优化了耗电：Minis 在后台时暂停装饰动画。 - 修复了单个加密存储读取失败时清空所有已保存凭据的问题。 - 修复了 `$5`、`$HOME` 被渲染为公式、列表中嵌套表格的显示、删除符号链接时误删目标内容，以及「深度思考」标题的语言。  ### 视觉打磨  - 回复下方的时间在非当天时显示日期。 - 模型回退提示、压缩进度和停止选项改为 iOS 风格；打开大会话时消息淡入显示。 - 悬浮胶囊始终完整显示在屏幕内，并按横竖屏分别记住位置。 - 更新了「关于」页介绍：Minis 是运行在你设备上的 AI Agent，密钥和文件留在本机，对话只发送给你选择的 AI 服务商。  完整变更：https://github.com/OpenMinis/OpenMinis/compare/1.14...1.15 
```

## ✨ Release Highlights  Netcatty **v1.1.90** fixes MCP readiness when the shell shows a Python/conda-style `(env) ` prefix, and stops keyword highlighting from overpainting Starship/truecolor text. Also includes an SFMono Nerd Font picker allowlist fix.  ### 🤖 External MCP  - Idle SSH Bash prompts that start with a Python virtualenv, conda, or BT-panel name such as `(3.12.12) ` or `(venv) ` are recognized again, so MCP / AI `terminal_execute` no longer hangs with `SHELL_NOT_READY` (#3948 / #3947).  ### 🖥️ Terminal  - Keyword highlight rules no longer recolor cells that already have an ANSI foreground (for example Starship truecolor path segments). Shell colors win over default keyword rules (#3941 / #3940). - **SF Mono Nerd Font** appears in the terminal font picker allowlist so it can be selected like other bundled fonts (#3930 / #3929).  ## ### 2026-10-22T14:34:25Z [**SubBoost/subboost**](https://github.com/SubBoost/subboost)

```
# SubBoost v2.10.0  ## 中文  ### 新增与修复  - 扩展 VLESS XHTTP 与 Hysteria2 导入，保留独立下行、Reality 混合密钥交换、Gecko/ECH 等设置；修复 TUIC/Hysteria、WireGuard、Snell、SSH 的部分参数丢失或映射错误。高级选项需要客户端内核支持。 - 修复导入规则后的目标代理组匹配，以及代理组重命名、禁用、删除后的处理，使编辑器与可视化预览保持一致。 - 改善 IPv6 fake-ip DNS 环境下的公共订阅导入，同时保留默认的内网和保留地址保护。 - 更新运行时与依赖的安全基线，并保持 YAML 凭据、合并配置和多行数组、对象的解析兼容性。  ### 升级说明  - Docker 镜像自带新版 Node.js，无需升级宿主机 Node。一键安装用户可在正式发布后运行 `subboost update`；升级器会先创建并验证数据库备份，再短暂重启服务。 - 直接用 Node.js 构建或运行源码时，请先使用 Node 24.x（至少 24.22.1，推荐）或 Node 26.x（至少 26.12.1）；Node 22 和 25 不再支持。 - 从 v2.9.0 升级没有新增数据库迁移或环境变量变更。请保留现有数据、配置和密钥，并在升级前备份；Docker Compose 源码部署继续按部署文档更新并重建镜像。  ## English  ### Features and Fixes  - Expanded VLESS XHTTP and Hysteria2 imports to preserve independent download settings, Reality hybrid key exchange, and Gecko/ECH options. Fixed lost or incorrectly mapped parameters in TUIC/Hysteria, WireGuard, Snell, and SSH. Advanced options require support from the client kernel. - Fixed target proxy group matching after rule imports and handling after groups are renamed, disabled, or deleted, keeping the editor and visual preview consistent. - Improved public subscription imports in IPv6 fake-IP DNS environments while preserving the default protections for private and reserved addresses. - Updated runtime and dependency security baselines while preserving parsing compatibility for YAML credentials, merged configurations, and multiline arrays and objects.  ### Upgrade Notes  - Docker images include the updated Node.js runtime, so the host's Node.js does not need upgrading. One-click installation users can run `subboost update` after the stable release is published. The updater first creates and verifies a database backup, then briefly restarts the service. - When building or running source directly with Node.js, first use Node 24.x (at least 24.22.1, recommended) or Node 26.x (at least 26.12.1). Node 22 and 25 are no longer supported. - Upgrading from v2.9.0 introduces no new database migrations or environment variable changes. Keep existing data, configuration, and keys, and back up before upgrading. Docker Compose source deployments should continue updating and rebuilding images according to the deployment documentation. 
```

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.2.6 修复 Windows 构建中 Shell 集成脚本的换行问题，并完善已有损坏安装的更新与校验。本版完整包含 2.2.5 的更新：加入按连接筛选标签、快捷命令分组筛选与图标颜色、独立 SSH 隧道和可选的命令输出折叠，统一终端与编辑器搜索，并支持在当前终端环境中打开远程 IDE。同时完善界面动效、提权认证、明暗外观方案、插件连接同步及 RDP 显示器布局，修复中文选区、文件链接、SSH Agent 和受限服务器上的文件访问问题。  #### 🛠️ Shell 集成修复  - 修复 Windows 构建将 CRLF 换行带入自带 Shell 脚本，导致远端 Bash 加载时报语法错误的问题；同时覆盖本地 Shell、WSL 启动脚本及编辑器适配脚本。（Issue [#720](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/720)） - 已有旧版集成可通过更新重新写入，同版本内容不符或文件缺失可通过修复恢复。只替换 OxideTerm 自带文件和带标记的启动引用，内容未变的启动文件跳过写入。 - 安装、更新和修复后回读校验完整内容；写入失败或校验不符时保留重试入口，确认完整后才提示成功。  #### 🗂️ 工作区与标签  - 顶部范围选择器可显示全部标签、全局页面、本地终端或指定连接的标签，切换连接时保留各自的页面选择。筛选状态显示可见数量与总数，并提供“显示全部”；取消筛选时保持当前页面。 - 范围名称在标题栏中居中显示。标签宽度按标题和控件内容计算，改善长标题、关闭按钮和横向滚动的布局一致性。 - 标题栏加入常用工作区操作和后台任务摘要，可从任务列表定位对应工作。悬停提示的切换、移动和消失更平滑，禅模式提示使用实际配置的快捷键。 - 助手与主机工具整理到右侧活动栏，工具按用途分组并使用更明确的图标；空间不足时才显示溢出入口。  #### ⚡ 快捷命令  - 命令条左侧增加固定的分组选择入口，支持搜索分组，右侧命令独立滚动。选择会保存到下次启动，与管理面板的筛选相互独立；分组删除后回到全部，空分组仍可切换。（Issue [#717](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/717)） - 分组图标支持自定义颜色、输入校验和恢复主题色，组内命令继承该颜色；颜色随分组保存、导入导出及云同步。 - 长命令名称自动显示省略号，悬停可查看完整名称和命令预览。超长正文限制预览行数与字符数，避免提示覆盖大片终端；排序继续由管理面板负责。  #### 🔎 搜索与远程 IDE  - 终端、IDE 和 SFTP 编辑器统一搜索控件，支持区分大小写、正则表达式和多行输入；编辑器支持捕获组替换与批量替换。（PR [#719](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/719)） - 终端搜索优先定位当前视口附近的结果，手动滚动后重新按当前视口导航；持续输出时更新匹配结果，并修正中文宽字符的高亮范围。编辑器可将最后一行滚入视口，光标与搜索定位同步处理水平滚动。（PR [#719](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/719)） - 完善多行查找与替换的换行输入、独立窗口的搜索焦点，以及拖动选择文本到编辑器边缘时的自动滚动。 - 远程 IDE 可选择 SFTP 或 SSH 文件访问；SSH 可使用纯 Shell 或按需安装／复用代理。支持从当前终端“转换为 IDE”，继承当前用户、容器和目录，并返回原终端。（PR [#716](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/716)） - 远程代理安装显示进度；文件读取提前识别二进制内容，保存按文件串行执行并保留冲突检测。纯 Shell 搜索有结果和执行边界，远程保存临时文件采用受限权限。（PR [#716](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/716)） - IDE 文件树分隔线支持拖动调整；文件访问方式选择复用应用主题对话框，并支持键盘操作。  #### ⌨️ 终端与 Shell 集成  - 增加默认关闭的命令输出折叠，启用后可收起已完成命令的输出，保留摘要、退出码和展开入口；调整窗口大小后保持折叠范围，并增强左侧标记的辨识度。 - 终端触发器增加匹配文本的点击操作、菜单项和悬停提示，可在文本与地址中使用匹配内容和命名捕获组。 - Shell 集成报告工作目录、命令起止和退出状态，并协调 Shell 原生标记与注入标记。远程集成区分安装、更新和修复，支持稍后处理；更新后在新终端中生效。 - 鼠标悬停 Unix 时间戳时显示本地时间。点击受支持的压缩包路径可根据当前 Shell 和可用工具生成解压命令，先预览再填入命令栏。 - 修正半透明选区在中文双列字符上的重复叠色，并正确使用主题的选区颜色和透明度；Windows 文件链接正确处理盘符、网络共享路径、中文及特殊字符。 - 已配置的中键／右键粘贴优先于终端程序的鼠标跟踪；修复 Kitty 图片在同步重绘期间丢失的问题，完善等宽字体回退和不可用字体提示。 - 本地 Shell 支持为当前设备指定程序路径并恢复自动检测；该路径不会作为跨设备设置导出。 - 删除已保存的 Telnet、Mosh 或串口配置时同步清理关联命令历史，并防止异步恢复重新带回已删除历史；空的清理前缀不会误删全部历史。（PR [#709](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/709)、[#712](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/712)）  #### 🔌 SSH、隧道与设备连接  - 新增独立 SSH 隧道配置，支持本地、远程和动态 SOCKS5 转发，保存后可直接启动或设置随应用启动。隧道可在没有终端窗格时运行，配置支持云同步与加密 `.oxide` 导入导出；退出应用会停止隧道。 - SSH Agent 认证先尝试所有身份，再进行 RSA 算法兼容重试，避免无效身份过早耗尽服务端认证次数。同一物理连接上的多个 Shell 通道复用已接受的 Agent 转发。 - SFTP 在服务端 SSH 会话数量受限时复用可用通道，减少额外打开会话导致的失败。 - 主机工具兼容拒绝 exec、仅支持 Shell 或 PTY 的 SSH 服务端，并正确处理探测命令回显。命令已发送但结果未知时不自动重放，避免重复执行。（PR [#701](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/701)） - Telnet 支持分别配置发送与接收换行方式；XMODEM 上传块大小可选择 128 字节或 1024 字节。  #### 🔑 提权凭据与认证  - SSH 通用提权凭据支持主机通配匹配，按连接专属、主机匹配、通用默认的顺序选择。唯一匹配时可选择自动填入并回车。（PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)） - 提示类型与响应模式独立配置，支持仅密码、密码后单独输入 TOTP，以及密码与 TOTP 合并响应。二阶段验证支持独立提示匹配和用户提示；再次要求第二因素时转为手动输入。（PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)） - 加密连接导出仅携带匹配当前连接主机的通用提权凭据副本及其引用的 TOTP 凭据，导入后保持连接作用域。（PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)） - 仅导入凭据元数据时，保留身份、类型和连接作用域一致的本地密钥串引用；替换凭据时清理旧秘密，延迟清理不会删除仍被使用的引用。（PR [#703](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/703)） - 保存凭据失败时保留真正的持久化错误，清理失败会安排重试，避免被后续清理错误掩盖。（PR [#706](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/706)）  #### 🎨 外观与 AI  - 支持固定浅色、固定深色或跟随系统外观，分别配置明暗方案中的应用与终端主题，并可将当前外观保存为所选方案。 - 统一侧边栏、选项高亮、弹窗、通知和按钮反馈的过渡；连续切换或反向操作时从当前状态平滑衔接，避免动画重新开始造成跳变。 - 界面动效统一遵循标准、快速、减少和关闭四档设置。减少模式省略位移和旋转，保留轻量淡入淡出；关闭后立即更新界面，不等待动画结束。 - 知识库顶部“笔记”栏与左侧导航使用一致的半透明主题底色，增强背景素材下的界面层次。 - 网络请求使用操作系统证书信任库，支持已安装的企业或代理 CA；模型刷新失败时显示更明确的错误。（PR [#718](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/718)） - Nix 包保留用户指定的证书环境变量，并优先使用宿主证书；模型刷新通知避免展示服务端原始错误正文及凭据。（PR [#718](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/718)） - 修复自定义 OpenAI 兼容端点的模型列表识别；对话图片显示在文字气泡上方。  #### 🧩 插件连接与远程桌面  - 插件可提供持续更新的工作台和可保存的连接类型，连接配置可在会话管理中打开、编辑和删除。 - 插件连接配置及凭据支持云同步和加密 `.oxide` 导入导出，并保留预览、变更合并和敏感信息选择。 - 统一 RDP 单屏与多屏尺寸控制：单屏随窗口请求分辨率，多屏使用活动显示器布局，窗口调整只改变本地显示比例。完善内置屏关闭、外接屏变化和重连时的布局更新，避免窗口尺寸覆盖多屏布局。 - macOS 发行包改用较新的 SDK 构建，统一发行构建的系统控件样式基础；Intel 与 Apple Silicon 构建均显式使用 macOS 13.0 最低目标，修复 Intel 包的版本信息校验失败。  #### 👥 贡献者  - @BilityLeu：Shell-only SSH 主机工具兼容、终端环境远程 IDE，以及终端与编辑器搜索改进（PR [#701](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/701)、[#716](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/716)、[#719](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/719)）。 - @m00nLi：提权凭据保存与清理，以及命令历史删除和恢复一致性修复（PR [#703](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/703)、[#706](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/706)、[#709](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/709)、[#712](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/712)）。 - @idxdy：主机匹配、自动填入和两阶段提权认证，以及系统证书信任支持（PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)、[#718](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/718)）。  #### 📌 使用与升级说明  - 若曾使用 2.2.5 安装远程 Shell 集成，升级应用后请对受影响主机执行集成“更新”或“修复”，成功后打开新终端；仅升级应用不会立即改写所有远端主机。启用自动集成时会在新建远程终端的检查流程中更新，询问模式需按提示确认。本地集成脚本在新建本地终端时重新生成。  - RDP 显示器与尺寸重新协商修复需搭配 RDP 插件 0.2.0；该插件要求 OxideTerm 2.2.5 或更高版本。 - 从当前终端转换为 IDE 前，应回到空闲 Shell 提示符并处理未提交输入。转换期间该终端输入暂停；纯 Shell 文件访问需要 Linux 和 GNU find、stat、grep、coreutils，代理方式会在所选环境安装或复用代理。 - 自动填入提权凭据需主动开启；SSH 通用凭据的主机匹配为 `*` 时覆盖所有主机，请按实际使用范围配置。 - 本版更新日志范围：[v2.2.4…v2.2.6](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.4...v2.2.6)。  ### English  OxideTerm 2.2.6 fixes Shell integration line endings in Windows builds and improves updating and verifying damaged installations. It includes all changes from 2.2.5: connection-scoped tabs, quick-command group filtering and icon colors, standalone SSH tunnels, and optional command-output folding. It unifies terminal and editor search and supports opening the remote IDE in the current terminal environment. The release also improves interface motion, privilege authentication, light/dark appearance schemes, plugin connection sync, and RDP display layouts, while fixing CJK selections, file links, SSH Agent handling, and file access on restricted servers.   #### 🛠️ Shell integration fixes  - Fixed Windows builds embedding CRLF line endings in bundled Shell scripts, causing syntax errors when remote Bash loaded them. The fix also covers local Shell integration, WSL launch scripts, and editor adapter scripts. (Issue [#720](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/720)) - Existing older integrations can be rewritten through Update; mismatched contents or missing files at the current version can be restored through Repair. Only OxideTerm's bundled files and marked startup references are replaced, and unchanged startup files are not rewritten. - Installation, updates, and repairs now read back and verify the complete contents. Write failures or verification mismatches remain retryable, and success is reported only after verification.  #### 🗂️ Workspace and tabs  - The title-bar scope selector shows all tabs, global pages, local terminals, or tabs for one connection, remembering the selected page in each scope. Filtered scopes show visible and total counts and a Show All action; clearing the filter preserves the current page. - Scope names are centered in the title bar. Tab widths account for titles and controls, improving long titles, close buttons, and horizontal scrolling. - Added compact workspace actions and a background-task summary to the title bar, with navigation to the associated work. Tooltips transition more smoothly when moving, switching, or disappearing, and Zen-mode hints reflect configured shortcuts. - Moved the assistant and host tools to the right activity rail, grouped tools by purpose, and clarified their icons. Overflow appears only when the rail runs out of space.  #### ⚡ Quick commands  - Added a fixed group selector with search to the left of the quick bar while commands scroll independently on the right. The selection persists across restarts independently of management-panel filtering; deleting a group returns to All Groups, and empty groups remain switchable. (Issue [#717](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/717)) - Group icons support custom colors, validation, and resetting to the theme color. Commands inherit their group's color, which is preserved in saved data, import/export, and cloud sync. - Long command names use an ellipsis, with the full name and command preview available on hover. Long previews have line and character limits to avoid covering the terminal; ordering remains in the management panel.  #### 🔎 Search and remote IDE  - Unified search controls across terminals, the IDE, and SFTP editors, including case sensitivity, regular expressions, and multiline input. Editors support capture-group replacement and Replace All. (PR [#719](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/719)) - Terminal search starts near the current viewport and reanchors navigation after manual scrolling. Matches update during ongoing output, and CJK highlight ranges use the correct cell widths. Editors can scroll the final line into view and coordinate horizontal scrolling with cursor and search navigation. (PR [#719](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/719)) - Improved newline entry in multiline find/replace, search focus in detached windows, and automatic scrolling when dragging a text selection to editor edges. - Remote IDE access can use SFTP or SSH; SSH supports pure Shell access or an agent installed or reused on demand. Convert to IDE from an existing terminal preserves its current user, container, and directory and supports returning to the original terminal. (PR [#716](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/716)) - Agent installation shows progress, file reads detect binary content early, and saves are serialized per file while retaining conflict checks. Pure Shell search has bounded results and execution, and remote save temporaries use restricted permissions. (PR [#716](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/716)) - The IDE file-tree divider is draggable. File-access choices use themed application dialogs with keyboard support.  #### ⌨️ Terminal and Shell integration  - Added opt-in command-output folding. Completed output can be collapsed while retaining a summary, exit status, and an expand action. Fold ranges survive window resizing, with clearer gutter markers. - Terminal triggers support click actions, menu entries, and hover hints for matched text, with matched content and named captures available in text and addresses. - Shell integration reports the working directory, command boundaries, and exit status, coordinating native and injected Shell markers. Remote integration distinguishes installation, updates, and repairs and supports deferring an update; changes take effect in new terminals. - Hovering Unix timestamps shows local time. Clicking supported archive paths generates an extraction command for the current Shell and available tools, with a preview before inserting it into the command bar. - Fixed translucent selection backgrounds being painted twice over wide CJK characters and applied theme selection colors and opacity correctly. Windows file links now handle drive letters, network shares, Unicode, and special characters. - Configured middle/right-click paste takes precedence over application mouse tracking. Fixed Kitty images disappearing during synchronized redraws and improved monospace font fallback and unavailable-font notices. - Local Shell executables can be overridden per device or restored to automatic detection. These paths are excluded from cross-device settings exports. - Deleting saved Telnet, Mosh, or serial profiles removes their associated command history, and asynchronous restoration cannot bring deleted history back. Empty retirement prefixes no longer risk clearing all history. (PR [#709](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/709), [#712](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/712))  #### 🔌 SSH, tunnels, and device connections  - Added standalone SSH tunnel profiles for local, remote, and dynamic SOCKS5 forwarding, with Save and Start and optional application-startup activation. Tunnels can run without a terminal pane; profiles support cloud sync and encrypted `.oxide` import/export. Exiting the application stops the tunnels. - SSH Agent authentication tries all identities before RSA algorithm retries, avoiding premature exhaustion of the server's authentication budget. Multiple Shell channels on one physical connection reuse accepted Agent forwarding. - SFTP reuses available channels when the server limits SSH sessions, reducing failures caused by opening additional sessions. - Host tools support servers that reject exec or require Shell/PTY access and correctly handle echoed probe commands. Commands whose results are unknown after submission are not automatically replayed. (PR [#701](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/701)) - Telnet send and receive line endings are configurable independently. XMODEM upload blocks can use 128 or 1024 bytes.  #### 🔑 Privilege credentials and authentication  - Common SSH privilege credentials support host wildcards, with connection-specific credentials taking precedence over host matches and generic defaults. A unique match can optionally be filled and submitted automatically. (PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)) - Prompt types and response modes are configured independently: password only, password followed by a separate TOTP response, or a combined password-and-TOTP response. Two-stage verification supports separate prompt matching and user hints; repeated second-factor requests fall back to manual input. (PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)) - Encrypted connection exports include only common privilege credentials matching the connection's host and the TOTP credentials they reference. Imported copies remain scoped to the connection. (PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708)) - Metadata-only credential imports retain existing keychain references when identity, type, and connection scope match. Replacements clean up old secrets, and deferred cleanup preserves references still in use. (PR [#703](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/703)) - Credential save failures retain the original persistence error. Failed cleanup is queued for retry rather than masking the save failure. (PR [#706](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/706))  #### 🎨 Appearance and AI  - Choose fixed light, fixed dark, or system-following appearance, configure application and terminal themes independently for each scheme, and assign the current appearance to a scheme. - Unified transitions across sidebars, selection highlights, dialogs, notifications, and button feedback. Rapid changes and reversals continue smoothly from the current state instead of restarting and jumping. - Interface motion consistently follows the Normal, Fast, Reduced, and Off profiles. Reduced removes movement and rotation while retaining subtle fades; Off updates the interface immediately without waiting for animations. - The Knowledge workspace's Notes header now matches the navigation sidebar's translucent theme background for clearer structure over background media. - Network requests use the operating system's certificate trust store, including installed corporate or proxy CAs. Model-refresh failures show clearer errors. (PR [#718](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/718)) - Nix packages preserve explicit certificate environment overrides and prefer host certificates. Model-refresh notifications avoid exposing raw server error bodies or credentials. (PR [#718](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/718)) - Fixed model-list discovery for custom OpenAI-compatible endpoints. Conversation images now appear above the text bubble.  #### 🧩 Plugin connections and remote desktops  - Plugins can provide live workbenches and persistent connection types that can be opened, edited, and deleted in Session Manager. - Plugin connection profiles and credentials support cloud sync and encrypted `.oxide` import/export, including previews, change merging, and sensitive-data selection. - Unified RDP single- and multi-monitor sizing: single-monitor resolution follows the window, while multi-monitor sessions use the active display layout and window resizing changes only local scaling. Improved layout updates when the internal display is disabled, external displays change, or sessions reconnect, preventing window dimensions from overriding multi-monitor layouts. - macOS release packages use a newer SDK to align the system-control styling baseline. Intel and Apple Silicon builds explicitly target macOS 13.0 as the minimum, fixing build-version verification failures for Intel packages.  #### 👥 Contributors  - @BilityLeu: Shell-only SSH host-tool compatibility, remote IDE access in the terminal environment, and terminal/editor search improvements (PR [#701](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/701), [#716](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/716), [#719](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/719)). - @m00nLi: Privilege-credential persistence and cleanup, and command-history deletion/restoration consistency (PR [#703](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/703), [#706](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/706), [#709](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/709), [#712](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/712)). - @idxdy: Host matching, automatic filling, two-stage privilege authentication, and system certificate trust (PR [#708](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/708), [#718](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/718)).  #### 📌 Usage and upgrade notes  - If remote Shell integration was installed using 2.2.5, update the application, run integration Update or Repair for affected hosts, and open a new terminal after success. Updating the application alone does not immediately rewrite every remote host. Automatic integration updates during the checks for a new remote terminal; Ask mode requires confirmation. Local integration scripts are regenerated when a new local terminal is opened.  - The RDP display and resize-renegotiation fixes require RDP plugin 0.2.0, which requires OxideTerm 2.2.5 or later. - Before converting the current terminal to IDE mode, return to an idle Shell prompt and handle any pending input. Terminal input is paused during conversion. Pure Shell file access requires Linux with GNU find, stat, grep, and coreutils; agent mode installs or reuses an agent in the selected environment. - Automatic privilege filling is opt-in. A common SSH credential with the host pattern `*` applies to every host; configure patterns for the intended scope. - Full changelog: [v2.2.4…v2.2.6](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.4...v2.2.6).  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_windows_x64-setup.exe) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_windows_arm64-setup.exe) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.2.6/OxideTerm_2.2.6_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper.
