### 2026-10-27T14:54:39Z [**zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui**](https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui)

```
 ✨ Features - **Update the bundled Claude Code SDK from the chat that needs it** (#1937): the API rejects requests from a Claude Code older than a model's minimum version (`claude_code_version_too_old`), and the hint to update Claude Code does not help because the plugin runs the copy bundled in its own SDK — updating the plugin or the terminal CLI leaves it untouched. The diagnostic card now matches that error and offers an **Update SDK** button that runs the existing dependency update in place and reports the result there. A successful install bumps a shared SDK generation, so every Claude daemon in the IDE reloads before its next request, while a daemon that is mid-reply is left alone (an update no longer ends a reply with “Daemon stopped”); installs of the same SDK run one at a time, a run where every npm attempt timed out reports failure instead of success, the card names the version it replaced and points at a custom Claude CLI override when that is what needs updating, and its state survives a remount (by @EeeasyCode, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Send editor context beside the message instead of inside it**: editor state used to be pasted into the user's message text, spending that message's native context budget and polluting history replay. Each Codex turn now freezes structured `additionalContext` fragments instead (`ide_context`, `open_tabs`, `workspace_context`, `referenced_files_<index>`, `terminal_context_<index>`), so every fragment gets its own budget and the request text stays pristine. The bridge sniffs the selected CLI once per child process (`app-server generate-json-schema --experimental`) to learn whether `turn/start` accepts `additionalContext`, re-sniffs after a runtime replacement, and older CLIs still get the official desktop text envelope with UTF-8 byte ranges shifted to match; history replay strips that envelope so users only ever see their own request (by @gadfly3173)  🔧 Improvements - **Keep the Node version probe off the JCEF UI thread** (#1776): sending a message resolved the Node version synchronously on the UI thread on a cache miss, so the webview froze before the message was even dispatched — 58 ms for a warm `<node> --version`, 80 ms for a full re-detection, and 5 s when the child wedged. The probe now runs at the top of the existing background task in both send paths, with the same error messages, trigger conditions and `invokeLater` push semantics (by @yibeiqingke) - **Bound SDK version lookups and show why a lookup failed** (#1972): `npm view` drained both pipes only after the fact, so a stalled registry request kept the 30 s timeout from firing, and a failure was indistinguishable from an empty list. Queries now drain stdout and stderr while enforcing the deadline, kill the process tree when it expires and surface the npm error code; an EPERM/EACCES cache failure is retried once against a plugin-owned cache (converted for WSL) without losing registry, auth or proxy settings; bundled recommendation versions are no longer presented as available remote versions; and Settings > Dependencies shows the failure with a retry button instead of an empty dropdown (by @GGMGG, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Clean up the metadata of deleted sessions in one batch** (#1979): deletion used to start two Node processes per session, one after another, each holding a JVM-wide favorites or titles lock, so deleting 100 sessions meant 200 process starts while history loads in other windows queued on the same locks. `removeFavorites` and `deleteTitles` now take a list and do one read and one write, and cleanup calls each service once per batch; the ids travel as JSON on stdin, which has no size limit (a Windows environment variable is capped at 32 KiB) and also reaches WSL node (by @zhukunpenglinyutong)  🐛 Fixes - **Stop MCP server configs from silently vanishing** (#1758): `~/.claude.json` is shared with the Claude CLI, and three defects combined to make configured servers disappear “on their own” — a plain `FileWriter` truncated the file before writing, so a crash left a torn config that every later reader treated as empty; read-modify-write cycles were not serialized, so a concurrent writer's snapshot could revert a just-saved list; and `syncMcpToClaudeSettings` copied an empty `mcpServers` over a non-empty one in `settings.json`, spreading the loss to the second store. Plugin writes to `~/.claude.json` and `settings.json` now go through a staged temp file plus `ATOMIC_MOVE`, keep a `.ccgui-backup` that is written atomically and born owner-only on POSIX (these files can hold `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`), and take an advisory `.ccgui-lock`; every mutation fingerprints the file with SHA-256, rejects the replace when it changed underneath and re-plans up to 3 times instead of overwriting the other writer. Reads never heal the live file (a failed read may be a newer CLI write in progress), a missing `mcpServers` key means “no servers” while the loss guard still blocks accidental empties, symlinked configs update their target and keep their POSIX mode, identical content is a no-op with no backup rotation, a corrupt live file is never promoted into the backup, an unreadable file is reported instead of rendered as an empty list, and deleting through the legacy `~/.codemoss/config.json` path clears only that entry from `settings.json` (by @hebulin, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Make the Node service timeout a real deadline** (#1778, #1978): `executeNodeScript` drained stdout to EOF before checking its 30 s timeout, so a child that hung with the pipe open blocked the caller forever and the timeout was dead code for favorites, titles and input history; `InputHistoryHandler` also wrote stdin synchronously before the timer started, which blocked once the payload outgrew the pipe buffer of a child that never read it. The pattern is now `bridge/NodeProcessRunner`: stdout is drained and stdin written on named daemon threads, `waitFor(timeout)` is the only blocking step and also covers stdin, the child is killed and a `TimeoutException` thrown on expiry, and output is only used once the reader reached EOF — a stdout still open 2 s after exit fails the call instead of returning a truncated result. `NodeJsServiceCaller`, `InputHistoryHandler` and `ClaudeSessionQueryService` all use it, and the session query's 30 s now covers the whole run, including streaming the output (by @yibeiqingke, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Put a real deadline on npm install and cache clean**: npm install and `npm cache clean` read stdout to EOF before waiting, so their timeouts never fired while npm hung with the pipe open — and with the per-SDK install lock in place, one stalled install blocked every later install of that SDK until the IDE restarted. `NpmInstallRunner` streams output on a daemon thread, enforces the deadline with `waitFor` and kills the process tree when it passes, and lines never reach the caller's log after it returns; install attempts and cache clean both run through it, waiting for a running install is capped at 20 minutes before reporting that it is still busy, and a WSL install that times out is not retried because killing `wsl.exe` may leave npm running inside Linux (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Guide users when JCEF is missing instead of failing to open a chat** (#1912, #1953): without the Web Browser (JCEF) module, `JBCefBrowser` and `JBCefOSRHandlerFactory` threw a linkage error while the chat window was being constructed, so the tool window broke with no explanation. The entry point now checks that both classes load through the plugin class loader without running their static initializers and shows a Swing panel with install/enable/update guidance before bridge startup, tab restoration or listener registration; Android Studio 2026.2+ on Windows/macOS keeps the plugin-specific text, while other IDEs get IDE-neutral module text because Boot Runtime advice cannot restore missing platform classes. Quick Fix and New Tab surface the same steps in a sticky balloon (Quick Fix checks before its prompt popup), and every locale now says “install, enable, or update” (by @mvanhorn, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Stop JCEF browsers from being recreated while they are being disposed** (#1971): tab activation, hierarchy events and remote work could each request the first browser, and a watchdog recreation could run while native resources were being torn down, producing duplicate preparation and use-after-dispose failures. Initial startup is now a single coalesced request (activation and hierarchy requests wait for the tab to be active, remote work forces it), browser creation re-checks ownership after native creation and disposes an instance that lost the race, the default-constructor fallback is only allowed after the failed instance released its native resources and is refused outright when an OSR handler factory is required, disposal still tears native resources down when an unrelated listener or service cleanup throws, and a failed cleanup shows the webview recovery panel instead of a blank tab (by @GGMGG) - **Recover remote chat tabs instead of leaving them on “Checking SDK status…”** (#1967): Remote JCEF can replace a hidden chat page without delivering `onLoadEnd`, while Java still reports `frontendReady` for the old document, so tab activation skipped recovery and the new page waited forever for `sendToJava`. Activation now runs the idempotent bootstrap for the current browser and page generation before scheduling retries (disposed, inactive and mismatched pages are still skipped, and ready pages start none), and a `frontend_ready` that arrives while Java already holds the flag can only come from such a replaced page, so it is treated as runtime recovery and the current provider, model and reasoning state are pushed before the transcript is replayed (by @Jefung) - **Keep Codex streaming and replaying every item consistently**: streaming could omit commentary, reasoning and edit cards between batches until the conversation was reloaded, and history replay collapsed supported parallel calls into generic exec cards with command labels that differed from live rendering. The transport now compares complete raw message content when choosing the suffix to send (structural signatures only schedule snapshots), and a buffered full snapshot is applied before its dependent tail update advances the sequence barrier. Replay associates `Promise.all` / `allSettled` results with their original array slots, keeps labeled arrays, primitives and plain text, restores mixed command and `write_stdin` batches with polling tied to the original process card, matches identical commands through confirmed process identity or a unique absolute working directory (including file URIs and Windows paths, while preserving literal POSIX backslashes), never assigns unindexed parallel receipts by arrival order, and keeps local Promise computations out of the visible timeline. Recorded summaries, approval reasons and parsed command actions are preserved, `list_files` and `listFiles` are both accepted, and generated English command summaries are gone (by @gadfly3173) - **Reopen Codex approvals only when they are still pending**: dialogs closed as soon as the page handed an answer to Java; when Java could not deliver it, the failure acknowledgement reached the page but nothing handled it, so Codex kept waiting on a request the user could no longer see and the turn looked stuck. Each answer is now tracked — a failure shows a toast and reopens the dialog once, unless Java marks it not retryable, the deadline passed, it was already retried, or the runtime closed the dialog. A request that was already answered or released reports `INTERACTION_NOT_PENDING`, so a later answer cannot resurrect the dialog and override the first choice, and a `respondInteraction` timeout is marked “delivery unconfirmed” with a warning not to answer again instead of reopening. Control-command timeouts now carry a readable error — a null message became `JsonNull`, which Gson drops, so timeouts read as success — and command approvals no longer show two identical “allow with this rule” buttons, because the advertised `availableDecisions` list is used as-is; rule buttons show the command prefix or host they would allow, with the full text on hover (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Keep a user's `API_TIMEOUT_MS` and stop leaking provider env values** (#1307, #1729): `API_TIMEOUT_MS` was listed as plugin-managed, so every provider sync deleted a user-configured value, and users running slow local backends (Ollama with 80k+ token contexts) had no way to extend the request timeout. It is no longer managed. The other half of the leak is fixed too: presets that set it themselves, such as MiniMax's 50-minute timeout, carried it over to the next provider, and removing a value while editing a provider left it behind. The sync plan receives the provider recorded in `codemossProviderId` and removes the unmanaged env values that provider wrote — but only while `settings.json` still holds that exact value, so a value changed by hand stays (by @hebulin, @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Delete favorites and custom titles together with the session**: `HistoryDeleteService.cleanupSessionMetadata` called `removeFavorite`, which the favorites whitelist did not allow, so the call threw and — because both steps shared one try/catch — the custom-title deletion was skipped as well; deleted sessions kept entries in `favorites.json` and `session-titles.json`. `removeFavorite` is whitelisted, the two steps run independently, and a false result (a failed save still exits 0) counts as a failure, so the log no longer reports a broken cleanup as done. Since every call rewrites the whole file and deletion can now overlap a rename or a favorite toggle from any chat window, `NodeJsServiceCaller` serializes calls per file with a JVM-wide lock (by @zhukunpenglinyutong) - **Keep a bad turn cursor from reloading or duplicating history** (#1976): `getSessionPage` parsed the optional `beforeTurn` cursor inline and accepted any integer-valued number, so a cursor beyond the history length returned the latest page with `cursorReset=true` and the client discarded its cached transcript for a value that was never usable. Parsing is now a pure `parseOptionalTurnCursor()`: absent, empty or non-numeric cursors fall back to the latest page, an explicit `0` still means “the page before the first turn”, and a numeric cursor beyond the history keeps `cursorReset` — that flag is what tells the orchestrator to replace the transcript instead of prepending a latest page and duplicating every visible message (by @hebulin) - **Treat an empty provider id as “disabled” instead of a config change** (#1969): `ProviderManager` treated an explicit empty `currentId` as invalid input and rewrote the provider config, so a deliberately disabled provider caused a config write on every load. An empty `currentId` is now a valid disabled state and no longer triggers a rewrite (by @zhouxiangsuper-create)  中文：  ✨ 新功能 - **在需要更新的会话里直接更新内置 Claude Code SDK**（#1937）：当模型要求更高版本的 Claude Code 时，API 会拒绝请求并返回 `claude_code_version_too_old`，而错误里“请更新 Claude Code”的提示没有用——插件运行的是自己 SDK 内置的那一份，更新插件或终端 CLI 都不会改动它。现在诊断卡片会识别该错误并提供「更新 SDK」按钮，复用已有的依赖更新流程原地执行并就地展示结果。安装成功后会推进共享的 SDK 版本号，IDE 内每个 Claude 守护进程都会在下次请求前重新加载，而正在回复的守护进程不会被中断（更新不再让回复以「Daemon stopped」结束）；同一 SDK 的安装串行执行，所有 npm 尝试都超时的那次运行会如实报告失败，卡片会说明被替换掉的版本，若真正需要更新的是自定义 Claude CLI 覆盖项也会直接指出，卡片重新挂载后仍保留更新状态（by @EeeasyCode、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **把编辑器上下文放在消息旁边而不是塞进消息里**：过去编辑器状态会被直接粘贴进用户消息文本，占用该条消息的原生上下文预算并污染历史回放。现在每轮 Codex 请求改为冻结结构化的 `additionalContext` 片段（`ide_context`、`open_tabs`、`workspace_context`、`referenced_files_<index>`、`terminal_context_<index>`），每个片段各自拥有原生预算，请求文本保持原样。Bridge 会为每个子进程嗅探一次所选 CLI（`app-server generate-json-schema --experimental`）以确认 `turn/start` 是否接受 `additionalContext`，运行时被替换后会重新嗅探；旧版 CLI 仍使用官方桌面端文本信封，并相应平移 UTF-8 文本元素的字节范围，历史回放会剥离该信封，用户只会看到自己写的内容（by @gadfly3173）  🔧 优化 - **把 Node 版本探测移出 JCEF UI 线程**（#1776）：版本缓存未命中时，发送消息会在 UI 线程上同步解析 Node 版本，导致消息还没派发 webview 就已卡住——预热过的 `<node> --version` 阻塞 58 ms，完整重新探测阻塞 80 ms，子进程卡死时阻塞 5 s。现在两条发送路径都会把探测放在已有后台任务的起始位置，错误提示、触发条件和 `invokeLater` 推送语义均未改变（by @yibeiqingke） - **为 SDK 版本查询设定上限并暴露失败原因**（#1972）：`npm view` 原本读完两个管道后才开始等待，registry 请求卡住时 30 s 超时永远不会生效，而且失败与空列表无法区分。现在查询会在执行截止时间的同时持续排空 stdout 与 stderr，到期后结束整个进程树并回传 npm 错误码；EPERM/EACCES 缓存失败会用插件自有缓存（WSL 路径已转换）重试一次，同时保留 registry、认证与代理设置；内置的推荐版本不再被当作可用的远程版本展示；「设置 > 依赖」会显示失败原因与重试按钮，而不是一个空下拉框（by @GGMGG、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **批量清理已删除会话的元数据**（#1979）：过去删除会话会逐个会话启动两个 Node 进程，且各自持有 JVM 级的收藏或标题锁，删除 100 个会话意味着 200 次进程启动，其他窗口加载历史时还要排队等同一把锁。`removeFavorites` 与 `deleteTitles` 现在接受列表，一次读取、一次写入，清理逻辑每批各调用一次；id 通过 stdin 以 JSON 传递，没有大小上限（Windows 环境变量上限为 32 KiB），也能被 WSL 下的 node 接收（by @zhukunpenglinyutong）  🐛 修复 - **修复 MCP 服务器配置静默消失**（#1758）：`~/.claude.json` 与 Claude CLI 共用，三个缺陷叠加导致已配置的服务器“自己消失”——普通 `FileWriter` 会先截断文件，崩溃后留下残缺配置，后续读取一律当作空文件；读-改-写没有串行化，并发写入者的快照可能把刚保存的列表回滚；`syncMcpToClaudeSettings` 会把空的 `mcpServers` 覆盖到 `settings.json` 的非空列表上，把数据丢失扩散到第二份存储。现在插件对 `~/.claude.json` 与 `settings.json` 的写入都走临时文件加 `ATOMIC_MOVE`，同时维护一份原子写入的 `.ccgui-backup`（在 POSIX 上创建即为仅属主可读，因为文件可能含有 `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`），并加 `.ccgui-lock` 建议锁；每次修改都会用 SHA-256 记录文件指纹，读取后被其他写入者改动就拒绝替换并最多重新规划 3 轮，而不是覆盖对方的写入。读取不再“自愈”写回活动文件（一次失败的读取可能只是 CLI 正在写入更新的内容），缺失 `mcpServers` 键即表示“没有服务器”，同时丢失防护仍会阻止意外清空；符号链接配置只更新其目标并保留 POSIX 权限，内容相同视为空操作且不轮转备份，损坏的活动文件永远不会被提升为备份，无法读取的文件会报错而不是渲染成空列表；通过旧版 `~/.codemoss/config.json` 路径删除时只清除 `settings.json` 中对应条目（by @hebulin、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **让 Node 服务超时真正生效**（#1778、#1978）：`executeNodeScript` 先读 stdout 到 EOF 再检查 30 s 超时，子进程卡住并保持管道打开时会永久阻塞，收藏、标题与输入历史的超时形同虚设；`InputHistoryHandler` 还会在计时开始前同步写 stdin，载荷超过管道缓冲区且子进程不读取时同样会永久阻塞。现在统一为 `bridge/NodeProcessRunner`：stdout 由具名守护线程排空、stdin 由具名守护线程写入，`waitFor(timeout)` 是唯一阻塞步骤且同时覆盖 stdin，超时后终止子进程并抛出 `TimeoutException`，只有在读取线程到达 EOF 后才使用输出——退出 2 s 后 stdout 仍未关闭则直接失败，而不是返回可能被截断的结果。`NodeJsServiceCaller`、`InputHistoryHandler` 与 `ClaudeSessionQueryService` 都改用它，会话查询的 30 s 现在覆盖整个执行过程（包括流式输出）（by @yibeiqingke、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **为 npm 安装与缓存清理设定真实截止时间**：npm install 与 `npm cache clean` 原本先读 stdout 到 EOF 再等待，npm 卡住并保持管道打开时超时永不触发——配合按 SDK 的安装锁，一次卡死的安装会阻塞该 SDK 之后的所有安装，直到 IDE 重启。`NpmInstallRunner` 用守护线程流式读取输出，用 `waitFor` 强制执行截止时间并在超时后结束整个进程树，返回后不会再向调用方日志输出任何行；安装尝试与缓存清理都走这条路径，等待正在进行的安装最多 20 分钟，之后报告仍在忙；WSL 安装超时后不再重试，因为杀掉 `wsl.exe` 可能让 npm 继续在 Linux 内运行（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **JCEF 缺失时给出指引，而不是让会话打不开**（#1912、#1953）：缺少 Web Browser (JCEF) 模块时，`JBCefBrowser` 与 `JBCefOSRHandlerFactory` 会在构造聊天窗口阶段抛出链接错误，工具窗口直接坏掉且没有任何说明。现在入口会在启动 bridge、恢复标签页或注册监听器之前，检查这两个类能否通过插件类加载器加载（不触发静态初始化），失败时显示带安装/启用/更新指引的 Swing 面板；Windows/macOS 上的 Android Studio 2026.2+ 保留插件专用文案，其他 IDE 使用与 IDE 无关的模块文案，因为 Boot Runtime 建议无法恢复缺失的平台类。Quick Fix 与新建标签页会用常驻气泡展示同样的步骤（Quick Fix 在弹出输入框前先检查），所有语言文案统一为“安装、启用或更新”（by @mvanhorn、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **避免在销毁 JCEF 浏览器的同时重建它**（#1971）：标签页激活、层级事件与远程任务都可能请求创建首个浏览器，看门狗重建还可能在原生资源正在释放时执行，导致重复准备与释放后使用。现在初次启动合并为一次请求（激活与层级事件会等标签页处于活动状态，远程任务则强制启动），浏览器创建后会在原生创建返回时重新校验归属并释放竞争失败的那个实例；默认构造函数兜底只允许在失败实例释放原生资源之后使用，且必须有 OSR 处理器工厂时直接拒绝；即使无关的监听器或服务清理抛异常，释放流程仍会完成原生资源清理；清理失败时显示 webview 恢复面板，而不是空白标签页（by @GGMGG） - **远程聊天标签页不再卡在「正在检查 SDK 状态…」**（#1967）：远程 JCEF 可能在不投递 `onLoadEnd` 的情况下替换隐藏的聊天页面，而 Java 仍为旧文档保留 `frontendReady`，于是标签页激活时跳过恢复，新页面永远等不到 `sendToJava`。现在激活会先对当前浏览器与页面代次执行幂等引导，再安排重试（已释放、非活动与代次不匹配的页面仍会跳过，已就绪的页面不会重试）；若 Java 仍持有就绪标记时又收到 `frontend_ready`，那只能来自被替换的页面，会被当作运行时恢复处理，先推送当前的供应商、模型与推理强度状态，再回放会话记录（by @Jefung） - **Codex 流式输出与历史回放保持一致**：流式过程中可能丢失批次之间的评论、推理与编辑卡片，必须重新加载会话才能看到；历史回放还会把支持的并行调用压缩成通用 exec 卡片，命令标签也与实时渲染不一致。现在传输层选择要发送的增量时会比较完整的原始消息内容（结构签名只用于安排快照），缓冲的完整快照会在其依赖的尾部更新推进序号屏障之前应用。回放会把 `Promise.all` / `allSettled` 的结果对应回原始数组槽位，保留带标签的数组、原始值与纯文本，恢复命令与 `write_stdin` 混合批次并让轮询绑定到原进程卡片；相同命令通过已确认的进程身份或唯一的绝对工作目录匹配（包括 file URI 与 Windows 路径，同时保留 POSIX 字面反斜杠）；无索引的并行回执绝不会按到达顺序分配；本地 Promise 计算不再出现在可见时间线中。记录下来的摘要、审批原因与解析后的命令动作都会保留，`list_files` 与 `listFiles` 两种写法都接受，自动生成的英文命令摘要已移除（by @gadfly3173） - **只在 Codex 仍在等待时重新打开审批框**：页面把答案交给 Java 后对话框立即关闭；若 Java 无法投递，失败回执会到达页面却没有人处理，Codex 便一直等待用户已经看不到的请求，这一轮看起来就卡住了。现在会跟踪页面发出的每个答案——失败时弹出提示并重新打开一次对话框，除非 Java 标记为不可重试、已过截止时间、已经重试过，或运行时已关闭该对话框。已作答或已释放的请求会返回 `INTERACTION_NOT_PENDING`，之后的答案无法再复活对话框并覆盖第一次选择；`respondInteraction` 超时会被标记为「投递未确认」，提示用户不要再次作答，而不是重开对话框。控制命令超时现在带有可读错误——null 消息会变成 `JsonNull` 并被 Gson 丢弃，导致页面把超时读成成功；命令审批也不再出现两个一模一样的「允许并使用此规则」按钮，因为服务端公布的 `availableDecisions` 列表已包含该修正，现在直接按原样使用；规则按钮会显示它将要允许的命令前缀或主机，悬停可看完整内容（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **保留用户配置的 `API_TIMEOUT_MS`，不再泄漏供应商环境变量**（#1307、#1729）：`API_TIMEOUT_MS` 曾被列为插件托管键，因此每次供应商同步都会删掉用户配置的值，使用较慢本地后端（80k+ token 上下文的 Ollama）的用户无法延长请求超时。现在它不再由插件托管。另一半泄漏也一并修复：自身设置该值的预设（例如 MiniMax 的 50 分钟超时）会把它带到下一个供应商，编辑供应商时删除某个值也会残留。同步计划现在接收 `codemossProviderId` 中记录的供应商，并清除该供应商写入的非托管环境变量——但仅当 `settings.json` 中仍是该确切值时才清除，用户手动改过的值会保留（by @hebulin、@zhukunpenglinyutong） - **删除会话时一并清理收藏与自定义标题**：`HistoryDeleteService.cleanupSessionMetadata` 调用了 `removeFavorite`，但收藏白名单不允许该函数，调用直接抛异常，而两步共用一个 try/catch，导致自定义标题也一并被跳过；被删除的会话在 `favorites.json` 与 `session-titles.json` 中留下残留。现在 `removeFavorite` 已加入白名单，收藏移除与标题删除作为两个独立步骤执行，服务返回 false（保存失败仍以 0 退出）也会被视为失败，日志不再把失败的清理报告为完成。由于每次调用都会重写整个文件，且删除现在可能与任意聊天窗口的重命名或收藏切换重叠，`NodeJsServiceCaller` 会用 JVM 级锁按文件串行化调用（by @zhukunpenglinyutong） - **避免错误的翻页游标导致历史重载或重复**（#1976）：`getSessionPage` 曾在原地解析可选的 `beforeTurn` 游标，并接受任意整数值，超出历史长度的游标会返回最新一页且 `cursorReset=true`，客户端于是为一个从未可用的值丢弃缓存的历史。现在解析逻辑抽成纯函数 `parseOptionalTurnCursor()`：缺失、空值或非数字游标回退到最新一页，显式的 `0` 仍表示“第一轮之前的那一页”，超出历史长度的数字游标则保留 `cursorReset`——正是该标记让编排器替换会话记录，而不是把最新一页前置导致每条可见消息重复（by @hebulin） - **空供应商 id 视为「已禁用」而不是配置变更**（#1969）：`ProviderManager` 曾把显式为空的 `currentId` 当作非法输入并重写供应商配置，于是有意禁用的供应商每次加载都会触发一次写入。现在空 `currentId` 是合法的禁用状态，不再触发重写（by @zhouxiangsuper-create）  ## What's Changed * docs(changelog): add v0.6.2 release notes by @zhukunpenglinyutong in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1964 * Feature/claudecode provider noop config write by @zhouxiangsuper-create in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1969 * fix(jcef): fence browser startup, recreation and resource disposal by @GGMGG in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1971 * fix(settings): stop stripping user-configured API_TIMEOUT_MS on provider sync by @hebulin in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1729 * fix(webview): restore lost bridges when reactivating remote chat tabs by @Jefung in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1967 * fix(dependencies): bound SDK version queries and expose lookup failures by @GGMGG in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1972 * refactor(bridge): extract turn-cursor parsing into a tested helper by @hebulin in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1976 * fix(claudecodegui): show JCEF installation guidance before chat window construction by @mvanhorn in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1953 * feat(claude): offer an in-place SDK update when Claude Code is too old for the model by @EeeasyCode in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1937 * perf(session): move node version probe off the JCEF UI thread by @yibeiqingke in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1776 * fix(history): make the node-service timeout actually reachable by @yibeiqingke in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1778 * fix(mcp): stop MCP server configs from silently vanishing by @hebulin in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1758 * fix: make the Node child timeout a real deadline for input history and session queries by @zhukunpenglinyutong in https://github.com/zhukunpenglinyutong/jetbrains-cc-gui/pull/1978 * fix: clean up favorite and custom title when a session is deleted by @zhukunpenglinyutong in ```
### 新功能  - RabbitMQ 发送面板新增消息属性区，支持 content_type、delivery_mode、priority、TTL、时间戳等字段，并可在消息列表中查看与复制 (contributed by @lcq928) (PR [#13553](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13553)) - Spanner 的索引与主键保留列的升降序方向，DDL 查看和索引面板不再丢失 `DESC` (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#13550](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13550))  ### 改进  - 连接后自动展开对象树恢复为默认开启，与 v0.6.55 及更早版本行为一致  ### 修复  - 修复 v0.6.56 中首次点击数据库连接只连接、不展开子节点的问题，Redis、MongoDB、MQTT 等各类连接均受影响 - 修复「连接后自动展开对象树」设置无法保存、开关不生效的问题 (contributed by @Gaiokane) (PR [#13574](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13574)) - 修复 PostgreSQL 的 `FETCH` 语句不返回游标结果、只显示空结果集的问题 (contributed by @heeeex100) (PR [#13556](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13556)) - 修复选中超长数值单元格时结果面板底部按钮被挤出、无法加载全部和导出的问题 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#13578](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13578)) - 修复 SQL 格式化设置中的下拉框使用原生样式、与其他设置项不一致的问题 (contributed by @Ezreal-byte) (PR [#13548](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13548))  --- <!-- dbx-cnb-mirror --> > 国内下载：如果 GitHub 下载较慢，可从 [CNB 镜像](https://cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx/-/releases/tag/v0.6.58) 下载桌面端安装包，Docker 镜像从 `docker.cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx:v0.6.58` 拉取。

### 2026-10-23T14:09:21Z [**razvandimescu/numa**](https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa)

```
This release keeps Numa resolving for users in France after Quad9 leaves on 24 November. It adds `-c/--config`, list URLs in `allowlist` and `authoritative = false` for a proxy TLD that is a real domain. It also hardens recursive mode and the Windows service, and picks up three RustSec fixes.  ## ⚠️ Check before upgrading  **A config file you name must exist.** A path that did not exist used to start Numa on defaults without a warning. Now the server and the CLI stop with "config file not found". Check the path in your service unit or Docker command. (#451)  **Override batches are all or nothing.** `POST /overrides` and `/overrides/environment` used to store the items before the first invalid one. Now one invalid item rejects the whole batch. (#466)  **DNSSEC shows as off in forward and odoh mode.** Validation only runs in recursive mode. The dashboard, `/health` and `numa dnssec on` now say so instead of reporting DNSSEC on. A monitor that checks `/health` for `dnssec` sees the change. (#463)  ## 🌍 Quad9 leaves France  Quad9 stops serving users in France on 24 November 2026. Numa's built-in DoH fallback was Quad9 alone. Numa uses it in auto mode when the recursive probe fails, and in forward mode when it finds no system DNS. The fallback is now Quad9, then Cloudflare (`https://1.1.1.1/dns-query`). Cloudflare only sees the queries that Quad9 failed. If you set `address`, nothing changes. (#477)  ## ⚙️ Config  - `-c FILE` / `--config FILE` selects the config file, before or after the subcommand. Thanks @id4vip (#450, #451) - Unknown keys in `numa.toml` are logged with their path. A `zones = [...]` pasted under `[proxy]` gets a hint to move it above the first section. (#457) - Parse errors name the file and show the line and column, instead of a debug dump of the whole config. (#456)  ## ✅ Allowlist URLs  `allowlist` accepts list URLs and paths next to plain domains. Numa fetches, caches and refreshes them with the block lists. A list that someone else maintains cannot undo a manual block from the dashboard. Thanks @id4vip (#467, #469)  ## 🏷️ Your own domain as the proxy TLD  With `authoritative = false` under `[proxy]`, Numa answers its `.tld` services and sends every other name under the TLD to forwarding rules and the upstream. Before, Numa returned NXDOMAIN for the apex, MX and hosts that live elsewhere. The default stays `true`. Thanks @id4vip (#467, #468, #473)  ## 📝 Zones  - TXT records work in zones, so a DKIM key is a plain `value = "v=DKIM1; ..."`. MX values split on any whitespace. Thanks @bcookatpcsd (#224, #454) - A zone CNAME target written as `nas.home.lan.` or `NAS.home.lan` now reaches the local record instead of going upstream. Overrides get the same fix. (#455) - numa.toml shows the inline `zones` array and where it has to go. (#458)  ## 🔒 Recursive mode  Recursive mode validates an answer before it is cached or shared with waiting queries. A refresh resolves the name again instead of reusing its own cache entry. AD bits from nameservers are cleared. NODATA answers with an SOA are cached, so a removed record is replaced. (#476)  ## 🪟 Windows  - The service reports a failure when serve stops on an error, so the SCM restart policy runs. Before, a boot-time bind error left the machine without DNS. (#470) - The UDP listener keeps running after per-datagram receive errors. (#476) - `numa install` sets the owner and ACL of `%PROGRAMDATA%\numa` to SYSTEM and Administrators. (#476)  **Run `numa install` again after you upgrade on Windows** to apply the restart flag and the new permissions.  ## ⚡ Performance  - The Linux release binaries and the Docker image (musl) use mimalloc. On a 2 vCPU x86_64 VPS this gave 2.1-2.5x the cached queries per second, and 1.8-1.9x on a Pi 4. macOS, Windows and glibc builds are unchanged. Thanks @Guara92 (#394, #395) - Blocklist checks look at the allowlist only after a block hit, and skip empty sets. Against v0.24.1 on an Apple Silicon laptop, a blocked lookup is 31% faster and a not-blocked lookup 56% faster. (#469, #474) - Blocklist reloads no longer allocate and drop extra copies of the domain table. (#443, #445)  ## Also notable  - Dependency updates for RustSec advisories: rustls (#452), rustls-webpki and webpki roots (#472), ctutils (#478) - The retired KSK-2017 root trust anchor is removed. The root rollover completed on 11 October, so behaviour does not change. (#449) - On hosts where systemctl cannot reach the system bus, its errors no longer fill the daemon log on every dashboard poll. (#461) - numa.toml has one commented `[upstream]` block per mode. README covers upgrading, calling Numa by path under sudo, and `-c`. (#446, #453, #460, #462)  <details> <summary>All changes</summary>  ## What's Changed * ci(docker): capture stderr in the alpine smoke test by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/441 * perf(blocklist): size the parse set up front and stop copying it on load by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/443 * perf(blocklist): grow the parse set instead of reserving per line by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/445 * docs(readme): document upgrading and calling numa by path under sudo by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/446 * chore(dnssec): drop the retired KSK-2017 root trust anchor by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/449 * refactor(ctx): build the blocked sinkhole response in one place by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/447 * test(svcb): pin ech SvcParam delivery through the rewrite paths by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/381 * fix(deps): bump rustls to 0.23.46 for RUSTSEC-2026-0346..0349 by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/452 * feat(cli): add -c/--config to pick the config file by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/451 * docs(readme): mention -c next to config path and config edit by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/453 * perf(alloc): use mimalloc on musl targets by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/395 * feat(zones): accept TXT records, split MX values on any whitespace by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/454 * docs(zones): show the inline zones array and where it has to go by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/458 * fix(cli): print errors with Display, name the config file on parse errors by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/456 * fix(ctx): chase CNAME targets written with a trailing dot or capitals by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/455 * feat(config): warn on unknown config keys by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/457 * docs(readme): drop /etc/numa from the uninstall leftovers by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/460 * fix(system_dns): keep systemctl errors out of the daemon log by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/461 * docs(config): give each upstream mode its own block in numa.toml by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/462 * test: share the zone() fixture and drop a duplicated TXT case by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/465 * refactor: trim what the changes since 0.24.1 left behind by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/464 * fix(deps): bump rustls-webpki + webpki roots for the new rustsec advisories by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/472 * fix(windows): restart the service when serve fails by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/470 * fix(dnssec): don't report DNSSEC on in forward or odoh mode by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/463 * fix(api): store an override batch only when every item is valid by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/466 * perf(blocking): skip lookups in empty sets by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/474 * feat(blocking): accept list URLs and paths in allowlist by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/469 * fix: recursive refresh and coalescing, windows udp recv and install permissions by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/476 * feat(proxy): add authoritative = false for a tld that is a real domain by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/468 * fix(deps): bump ctutils for RUSTSEC-2026-0385 by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/478 * fix(upstream): try cloudflare after quad9 in the built-in DoH fallback by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/477   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/compare/v0.24.1...v0.25.0  </details>

### 2026-10-27T09:16:58Z [**tw93/Kaku**](https://github.com/tw93/Kaku)

```
<div align="center">   <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Kaku/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Kaku Logo" width="120" height="120" />   <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Kaku V0.27.1</h1>   <p><em>A fast, out-of-the-box terminal built for AI coding.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog 1. **Crash Fix**: Kaku no longer quits with every tab open while drawing certain lines that contain links. 2. **Remote Pairing**: A new browser pairs only after both it and `kaku remote` show the same six-digit code and you press Enter in the terminal, so someone who gets hold of the link cannot get in alone. 3. **Remote Direct**: Remote terminals now connect directly even with a TUN-mode proxy such as Stash or Clash running, and a stranger can no longer cut that direct connection. 4. **Remote Colors**: In the web terminal, Claude Code's scrolled transcript and other ANSI black backgrounds now draw dark like the Mac window, instead of light grey under white text. 5. **Custom Themes**: Settings lists the color schemes in `~/.config/kaku/colors` and applies them just like the built-in ones. 6. **Yazi and Lazygit**: Cmd+Shift+Y and Cmd+Shift+G open the tool in a new tab when Codex, Claude Code or vim holds the pane, instead of typing the command into it. 7. **Tab Bar**: With the tab bar at the bottom, clicking the last terminal row no longer switches tabs. 8. **Kaku Assistant**: The chat no longer redraws the whole screen while it waits for a reply, and saving its settings writes the file in one step, so an interrupted save no longer leaves it half written.  ### 更新日志 1. **崩溃修复**：绘制某些带链接的行时，Kaku 不会再连同所有标签一起退出。 2. **远程配对**：新浏览器要等它和 `kaku remote` 显示同一个六位码、并在终端按下回车后才会配对，别人拿到链接也进不来。 3. **远程直连**：开着 Stash、Clash 这类 TUN 模式代理时，远程终端也能直接连接，外人也无法再掐断这条直连。 4. **远程配色**：网页终端里 Claude Code 往上滚动的内容和其他 ANSI 黑色背景，现在和 Mac 窗口一样画成深色，不再是浅灰底配白字。 5. **自定义主题**：Settings 会列出 `~/.config/kaku/colors` 里的配色方案，可以像内置主题一样直接应用。 6. **Yazi 和 Lazygit**：Codex、Claude Code 或 vim 占着当前分屏时，Cmd+Shift+Y 和 Cmd+Shift+G 会在新标签页打开工具，不再把命令打进去。 7. **标签栏**：标签栏放在底部时，点击终端最后一行不会再切换标签。 8. **Kaku 助手**：等待回复时不再反复重绘整个界面，保存设置时一次写完整个文件，中途打断也不会留下写了一半的配置。  Special thanks to @Konata9, @sunbird89629 and @darion-yaphet for their contributions to this release.  > https://github.com/tw93/Kaku 
```

### 2026-10-27T20:59:58Z [**rvben/rumdl**](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl)

```
 ### Added  - `rumdl check-assets` reports assets unreferenced in complete Markdown source   scans, with explicit include/keep globs and text or versioned JSON output.   Literal filename mentions are kept separately; incomplete scans emit no findings. - MD022 `lines-above-mode = "exact"` removes surplus blank lines above headings   while preserving comments and the existing minimum-spacing default (#838). - Opt-in code-block tool version verification against `uv.lock`, declared   `pyproject.toml` constraints, or pre-commit/prek hook revisions for Ruff,   Black, djLint and SQLFluff. Supports automatic source selection and one   configurable policy for mismatched or unresolved versions. - MD013 `link-text-punctuation = "trailing"` optionally recognizes sentence   endings in link text and image alt text while keeping each construct intact.   When enabled, semantic reflow also preserves detected sentence boundaries   when merging short lines. - MD063 `style = "consistent"` infers heading capitalization per document while   respecting ignored words, proper names, level filters, and inline disables. - Independent code blocks can share the existing worker pool when error policies   permit parallel execution, preserving tool order and applying formatted   replacements together from their original source ranges. - MD013 `break-words` adds project-specific or localized words to the semantic   reflow defaults, with case-insensitive Unicode matching. - **custom-rules**: prototype versioned checker interchange ([602a8f7](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/602a8f7fe4224c4d9b012f3fd877278501461d72)) - **code-block-tools**: verify pre-commit and prek revisions ([0cfe861](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/0cfe861a2e71a82c968bad19627d5a0b82d22457)) - **MD096**: add opt-in table sorting by named column ([43a7858](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/43a78584866c9579c6f21486eeeeb40a21e335ef))  ### Changed  - Code-block formatting warnings appear in source order, including single-worker   runs. - MD013 semantic reflow joins wrapped list and blockquote prose consistently   with paragraphs, including items containing only one semantic sentence. - Reduce silent-formatting work for nested lists and unsupported proof syntax,   and avoid copying line text while checking Python-Markdown list structure.  ### Fixed  - Prevent stack overflows when releasing parsed MDX contexts with deeply   nested JSX, including headings checked in parallel. - MD013 sentence reflow uses surrounding context to distinguish technical labels   (`service A.`, `plan B.`) from name initials (#965).   A lone initial after a lowercase word (`edited by A. Smith`) can now also split.   Custom `abbreviations` preserve ambiguous initials globally, including when   the same letter is used as a technical label. - Silent formatting applies native bare-URL fixes in continued reference   definitions and reference labels with empty destinations. - Silent formatting applies native list-indentation fixes when display-math   delimiters span blocks and close inside a code span. - MD013 semantic break words keep offsets in the original Unicode text, avoiding   incorrect splits after characters whose lowercase forms change byte length. - MD013 semantic reflow keeps balanced parenthetical asides together during   sentence detection and counts them as single units in paragraph diagnostics.   Overlong asides prefer inner sentence boundaries before further width wrapping;   explicit hard breaks remain separate reflow segments. - **MD007**: honor start-indent at nested list levels ([fbf3d10](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/fbf3d10d98c2c1311f1d7139f10caee4ecfcebbc)) - MD005: keep adjacent ordered and bullet lists separate (#970) ([2c1d543](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/2c1d543bb7f554849b9e12027bfa60be5dcf617d)) - **MD033**: guard HTML conversions with parser byte ranges ([dea14d8](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/dea14d83d66c2604aa679fc0acd46e388c21c370)) - **docs**: retire AI search and retain keyword search ([f9f4f2e](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/f9f4f2e8ac6a86139a25b4c0ed6109190bfbf323)) - **MD070**: detect collisions in list items opened on a quoted item line ([21c3149](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/21c314927080a564a6a6d255ec011f4609e362ee)) - **MD070**: judge nested fences relative to their list item or blockquote ([68dbb3e](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/68dbb3e9d6d1a9f030570a62797f4ed736375d7b)) - **mdx**: bundle maintained parser and avoid redundant image scans # GhidraMCP Release 7.0.0  Production-ready Model Context Protocol server for Ghidra reverse engineering platform.  ## By the Numbers  | Metric | Value | |--------|-------| | **MCP Tools** | 209 | | **GUI Endpoints** | 205 | | **Headless Endpoints** | 190 | | **Java** | 21 LTS | | **Ghidra** | 12.1.4 |  ## Installation  1. Download **GhidraMCP-7.0.0.zip** below 2. In Ghidra: **File > Install Extensions > Add** and select the ZIP 3. Restart Ghidra, enable plugin: **File > Configure > Configure All Plugins > GhidraMCP** 4. Start server: **Tools > GhidraMCP > Start MCP Server** 5. Install Python bridge: `uv tool install ./ghidra_mcp_bridge-7.0.0-py3-none-any.whl` (or `pip install` the same wheel) 6. Run: `bridge-mcp-ghidra` (or `python -m bridge_mcp_ghidra`)  ## What's Included  - **GhidraMCP-7.0.0.zip** — Ghidra extension (GUI plugin) - **ghidra_mcp_bridge-7.0.0-py3-none-any.whl** — MCP protocol bridge wheel (209 tools)  See [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/bethington/ghidra-mcp/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md) for detailed release notes. See [README.md](https://github.com/bethington/ghidra-mcp#readme) for full documentation. 
```

### 2026-10-24T11:00:11Z [**lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager**](https://github.com/lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager)

```
-   **[Adaptive QoS Scheduling System via Pure Peer Calibration & Ladder Probing] (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @lbjlaq)**:     -   **Real-time Time-To-First-Token (TTFT) Metric**: Captured pure elapsed time from request dispatch to the first effective chunk, completely eliminating noise from output length or streaming transfer to serve as the physical benchmark for silent QoS throttling. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **5-Tier Pure Peer Baseline & Anti-False-Positive Shield**: Peer baselines strictly exclude: self, heterogeneous tiers, disabled accounts, 429 rate-limited/zero-quota accounts, Jailed, Probing, Suspicious, and Half-Open accounts to prevent baseline poisoning; thinking models receive a dynamic 35s latency compensation to prevent false positives during deep reasoning; global outage brake halts strikes when the peer average exceeds 20s, coupled with an 8s~20s hysteresis deadband to prevent oscillation. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Transient Tagging & Immediate Session Handover**: Tags account as `Suspicious` on the first strike (instantly excluded from peer calculations, with the scheduler activating drain mode); 2 strikes within a 5-minute window isolates the account in jail and triggers immediate sticky session unbinding, allowing P2C to rebind healthy accounts with 100% Prompt Cache efficiency. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Background Ladder Probing & Half-Open Anti-Flapping**: Cooldown ladders scale at 2m -> 5m -> 10m -> 15m -> 20m -> 30m, resetting the timer at each step; dispatches lightweight synthetic "Hello" probes (< 5 tokens, 8s hard abort timeout); passing probes triggers `Half-Open` probation requiring 2 consecutive real requests under 8s before full release; 30-minute penalty memory retention prevents rapid looping. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **One-Click QoS Jail Release & Remaining Cooldown Display**: Displayed real-time QoS cooldowns on cards, tables, and monitor logs, providing an instant manual release button and backend API (`release_account_qos_jail`) across all 12 supported locale catalogs. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[Official Model Suite Upgrade, Tiered Thinking Protocol & Argon Standalone Quota Isolation] (Fixes #3735, Ref #3720, Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @xiaozhi-xmu, @chenshin166-arch)**:     -   **Decoupled Model Ingestion from quotaInfo**: Completely eliminated the legacy `if let Some(quota_info) = info.quota_info` filter gate in `quota.rs`, benchmarking exclusively against official model catalogs to preserve Gemini models (`gemini-3.8-flash-*`, `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`, etc.) when upstream omits model-level `quotaInfo`, permanently resolving local 503 scheduling rejections. (Fixes #3737, Thanks to @xiaozhi-xmu, @chenshin166-arch, @jeikl)     -   **Tiered Thinking Protocol (Thinking Budget vs Thinking Level)**: Governed new-generation tiered models (Gemini 4 Argon, Claude 5.5) exclusively with `thinkingLevel` (`"LOW"`, `"MEDIUM"`, `"HIGH"`) and traditional models with `thinkingBudget`, eliminating HTTP 400 dual-field conflicts. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Disabled Model Variant Ingestion Filtering & False Quota Elimination**: Ingested the `disabled: Option<bool>` field and `is_disabled()` helper in `OfficialModelInfo`, intercepting `-disabled` / `_disabled` suffixes at the top of the baseline check and stripping them from `normalize_to_standard_id` and the account pool; purged false badges and progress bars from frontend cards. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Gemini 4 Argon Dispatch Closure & Standalone Quota Inheritance**: Extended candidate filtering with `token_supports_advanced_model` and standalone bucket checks (`normalized_target != "claude" && token.model_quotas.contains_key(...)`) supporting camelCase and snake_case properties, eliminating phase-two 503 rejections while preserving Claude 5.5 / 4.6 pool separation. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Deprecate Obsolete gemini-pro-agent Codename**: Standardized `gemini-3.1-pro` mappings in `variant_mapping.rs` from deprecated internal codename `gemini-pro-agent` to official physical model `gemini-3.1-pro-high`. (Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Claude Parity for Dynamic Model Candidate Rewriting**: Integrated `token_manager.resolve_dynamic_model_for_account` into the Claude handler post-scheduling to match OpenAI and Gemini parity for adaptive per-account model rewriting. (Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Standardized Context Window & Output Limits**: Standardized context and output limits across OpenAI (`/v1/models`), Claude (`/v1/models`), and Gemini (`/v1beta/models`) metadata endpoints. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[OpenAI Responses API Structured Outputs, Pipeline Tool Conflict Mitigation & Zero-Loss Unwrapping] (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @jeikl)**:     -   **Inbound Protocol Normalization**: Supported `text.format` specifications in `OpenAIRequest` for the Responses API, extracted `effective_response_format` in request mapping, and normalized it into Gemini native `generationConfig.responseSchema` and `responseMimeType: "application/json"`, unblocking Codex Desktop's automatic thread naming (`thread_title`) and summary generation. (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Background Thinking Budget Convergence & Timeout Prevention**: Explicitly honored `reasoning.effort: "low"` to constrain the thinking budget to 1024 tokens for background short tasks, eliminating 30-second hard timeout aborts by the Codex client during thread title generation on high-effort models. (Fixes #3710, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Unified Tool Conflict Mitigation in Pipeline (Pipeline First)**: Moved the mutual exclusivity handling of `responseSchema` and `tools` / `toolConfig` down to `align_google_request_prefix_topology_with_model` in `src/proxy/pipeline/inbound.rs`, suppressing `canonical_tools` and `canonical_tool_config` across all protocols when `responseSchema` is active to eliminate upstream HTTP 400 rejections. (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Guarded Outer Unwrap & Zero-Loss Conversational Protection**: Guarded `convert_chat_response_to_responses` with `is_structured_output` to preserve 100% of conversational commentary in normal turns (preventing conversational data loss), while applying `unwrap_outer_code_fence_if_structured` strictly when the entire response is enclosed in markdown fences to unblock Codex Desktop's native `JSON.parse` without corrupting embedded code blocks. (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel) -   **[Mini Floating Window Native macOS Transparency, Bidirectional Millisecond Sync & Deadlock-Free Scheduling] (Fixes #3733, Ref #3718, #3721, Ref #3738, Ref #3741, Thanks to @lbjlaq, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @jeikl)**:     -   **Native macOS Transparency & Window Drag Permissions**: Added Tauri `macos-private-api` and native `macOSPrivateApi: true` configuration, eliminating WebKit black background edges and bringing native frosted glass transparency; injected transparency scripts on the first frame and declared drag/always-on-top capabilities. (Fixes #3733, Ref #3718, PR #3738, Thanks to @lbjlaq)     -   **Dark/Light Adaptation & Cross-Window Millisecond i18n Sync**: Mini view dynamically inherits theme changes; bound `app://theme-changed` and `app://language-changed` cross-window events for bidirectional seamless synchronization across all 12 localization files. (Fixes #3733, PR #3738, Thanks to @lbjlaq)     -   **Native Main Thread Scheduling & Win32 Deadlock Elimination**: Introduced `switch_to_main_window` and `hide_mini_view` commands in the Rust backend, dispatching main window reconstruction and reactivation (`exit_lightweight_mode`) fully onto the OS native UI main thread (`app.run_on_main_thread`), completely eliminating Win32 message loop deadlocks and spinlock hangs when creating windows cross-thread on Windows. (Ref #3733, Ref #3738, Ref #3741, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Mini Window React Router 404 Fallback & Synchronous Resolution**: Enhanced synchronous resolution in `App.tsx` using `window.location.search` (`?mini=1`) and `__TAURI_INTERNALS__` to eliminate routing re-entry caused by asynchronous IPC window label delays; supplemented `createBrowserRouter` with `errorElement` and `index.html` wildcard fallbacks. (PR #3741, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Lightweight Mode Seamless Fade-in on Render Ready**: Kept main window invisible by default (`visible: false`) during reconstruction, fading in and focusing smoothly only after ThemeManager mounts theme and configurations, completely eliminating black screen/white frame flickers before WebView2 paints the React DOM. (Ref #3733, Ref #3738, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[Cross-Model Signature Architecture Refactor: Aligning with Official Antigravity Sentinel Discipline & Eliminating Claude Concurrent Tool 400 Errors] (Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:     -   **Official Base64 Sentinel Parity**: Standardized the global sentinel constant from plaintext to the official 44-byte Base64 ciphertext `c2tpcF90aG91Z2h0X3NpZ25hdHVyZV92YWxpZGF0b3I=`; retained backward-compatible recognition for legacy plaintext sentinels (`LEGACY_SENTINEL_SIGNATURE`) to guarantee zero regression on cached turns. (Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Claude Zero-Sentinel & Concurrent Tool Cleanliness**: Fully exempted Claude models from synthetic sentinel injection; in concurrent tool use scenarios, signatures exclusively anchor on the first non-thought part (first tool or prose), while all subsequent concurrent tools strictly omit `thoughtSignature`, permanently eliminating `Invalid signature in thinking block` 400 rejections. (Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Claude ➔ Gemini Transition Foreign Tool Passports**: When switching from Claude back to Gemini, the inbound Gatekeeper automatically detects foreign Claude tools carrying the `toolu_` prefix and equips every foreign `functionCall` with a Base64 sentinel passport for seamless Gemini engine verification bypass, while strictly keeping prose parts pure without sentinels. (Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Gemini ➔ Claude Transition History Sanitization & Zero-Persistence**: When switching to Claude, the inbound pipeline completely purges legacy Gemini short signatures and sentinels; enforced strict guards in `normalize_and_heal_signature` so neither plaintext nor Base64 sentinels ever persist into SQLite storage. (Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[Streaming Giant Tool Call Grace Period Transition, Dynamic Timeout Budget Convergence & Claude 429 Elimination] (Fixes #3739, Fixes #3713, Ref #3506, Ref #3525, Ref #3720, PR #3740, PR #3714, PR #3742, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:     -   **Streaming Giant Tool Call Buffer Transition**: Relaxed the cliff transition condition to `matches!(phase, StreamPhase::Thinking | StreamPhase::Streaming)`, automatically transitioning to StreamPhase::Transition with a 180s grace period when generating 10,000+ tokens giant `functionCall` payloads, preventing premature termination at 120s. (Fixes #3739, PR #3740, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **TTFT Header Watchdog & Total Budget Convergence**: Tightened primary streaming response header watchdog to a 30s base (elastically extended up to 45s for large payloads) with a 25s fallback margin, bounding total fallback budget to 55~70s (30s for non-streaming) and eliminating 85s~140s hangs on congested endpoints. (Fixes #3713, PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Integrated FailureStatusTracker into Claude Protocol**: Fully integrated `FailureStatusTracker` into `claude.rs` retry loops, recording `GATEWAY_TIMEOUT` on network errors and downgrading final status to 504 Gateway Timeout unless all attempts were genuine 429s, eliminating 64-second client backoff freezes. (PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Refactored Multi-Day Hard Quota Exhaustion Detection**: Accurately identified responses with `quotaResetDelay > 30min` (e.g., 68h) as account-level hard quota exhaustion, triggering 50ms rapid rotation in multi-account pools; applied 30s cooldown and unbinding upon full endpoint network errors. (PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Claude Thinking Budget Official Default Fallback**: In `resolve_custom_budget`, ensured Claude models return `None` when `claude_budget` is `<= 0`, enabling `InboundThinkingPipeline` to retrieve the authoritative default budget from `official_info.thinking_budget` (e.g. 1024). (Ref #3735, PR #3742, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel) -   **[Asynchronous High-Performance Batch Import & Cold Start Sub-Second Rendering] (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)**:     -   **Streaming Concurrency & Backpressure Control**: Added `batch_import_accounts` streaming pipeline with `buffer_unordered(6)` bounding in-flight tasks, slashing 494 account import durations from 28 minutes to 15~20 seconds (60x speedup) and eradicating memory spikes and OOM termination. (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Full-Quota Persistence on Import & Cold-Start Lock Decoupling**: Concurrently fetched tokens and quotas during import so accounts write to disk with 100% full quota and tier data; switched `list_accounts` read locks to non-blocking `try_read()` to prevent the backend server from stalling frontend instant rendering during cold starts. (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Local State Snapshot Hydration Eliminating Empty State Flashes**: Introduced `localStorage` synchronous snapshot hydration in `useAccountStore`, painting previously loaded account lists and quota matrices on the very first frame to eradicate "0 accounts" empty flashes. (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Anti-Throttling Jitter Architecture & Strict Idempotency**: Injected 30ms ~ 90ms dynamic hash jitter and integrated 429 rate limit automatic 1.5s backoff retries; enforced lowercase email and Refresh Token deduplication with in-place upserts and orphan file self-healing. (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[System Tray Tiered Quota Awareness, CLI (agy) Credential Isolation & Native Account Switching] (Fixes #3719, Fixes #3728, Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Wiojelt, @bkspi, @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq, @jeikl)**:     -   **Cross-Platform Tray Hover Quota Tooltip & Status Bar Title**: Implemented rich-text hover tooltips strictly conforming to Windows 128-char limits; displayed native persistent percentage titles on macOS status bars (e.g. `C:85% G:100%`); added Mini HUD one-click shortcut in tray context menu. (Fixes #3719, Thanks to @Wiojelt, @jeikl)     -   **Linux Window Focus Stealing & Auto-Spawn Root-Cause Eradication**: Removed dangerous external `--version` invocations in favor of static file canonicalization, symlink traversal, and lightweight `asar` in-memory parsing, eliminating focus stealing and accidental window reactivation while introducing a 60s global cache. (Fixes #3728, Thanks to @bkspi, @lbjlaq)     -   **Decoupled Classic Account Switching from Overwriting CLI Credentials**: Introduced `sync_agy_cli` toggle to avoid wiping CLI-specific credentials during classic switching, completely resolving credential stomping across client variants. (Fixes #3728, Thanks to @lbjlaq)     -   **Native Command Line Tooling Suite (`at` / `antigravity-tools`)**: Rolled out a lightweight terminal account switcher supporting `at list`, `at current`, and `at switch [query]`, with `--continue` for seamless session recovery. (Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq) -   **[Proxy Pool Leak Prevention, Multi-Protocol Auth Enhancements & Local Agent Probe Compatibility] (Fixes #3703, Fixes #3729, Fixes #3730, PR #3708, PR #3706, Thanks to @CarlitoDon, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)**:     -   **Bound Account Anti-Direct Leak Safeguard**: Refactored dedicated proxy routing so that if a bound proxy is unhealthy and no public pool node exists, the request strictly continues over the bound proxy (preferring network errors over silent direct connections), eradicating source IP leaks to Google authorization systems. (Fixes #3703, PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **WHATWG URL Credential Cleaning & Ciphertext Containment**: Sanitized passwords before usernames across HTTP/HTTPS/SOCKS5h; refused to forward `ag_enc_` ciphertext in CONNECT headers upon migration-induced decrypt failures. (PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Hermes Agent & Local Probe Endpoint Compatibility**: Added `/props`, `/v1/props`, `/version`, and `/v1/version` read-only endpoints, injecting `context_length` and `max_tokens` into `/v1/models` to eliminate handshake probe warnings and 404 errors. (PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)     -   **Gemini Protocol URL Query (?key=) Auth Support**: Refactored credential extraction pipelines to support `Authorization: Bearer`, `x-api-key`, `x-goog-api-key`, and URL Query `?key=`. (Fixes #3729, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)     -   **Web/Headless Mode In-Memory Hot Reloading**: Fully synchronized `only_raw_quota_models` write locks and client cache invalidations for 100% feature parity with GUI desktop environments. (Fixes #3730, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq) -   **[Cross-Platform AI Agent / CLI Identity Normalization & Anti-Throttling Prompt Defense] (Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl)**:     -   **Broad-Spectrum Agent Identity Normalization**: Normalized non-official Antigravity Harness openings (OpenCode, OpenClaw, Grok Build, Cursor, Cascade, Cline, etc.) to official Antigravity identities, stripping external fingerprints to maintain priority scheduling. (Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **Adaptive Dual Scan Window & Essential Prompt Protection**: Expanded system prompt header scan windows to the first 8 sentences or 1500 characters to penetrate plugin context shims; instituted top-priority whitelist guards ensuring essential prompts like `JeikCode` pass through completely unaltered. (Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[Gemini Upstream Protocol Strict Compliance, Tool Ordering & Token Estimation Hardening] (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, PR #3702, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:     -   **Multi-Turn Role Strict Alternation (user <-> model)**: Merged continuation prompts when previous turns were user roles, preventing consecutive duplicate user turns from violating Google 400 validations. (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Native & Function Tool Topological Ordering**: Preserved relative declaration priority between custom function tools and native tools (GoogleSearch, CodeExecution). (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Google countTokens Dedicated Envelope Cleaning**: Introduced `align_count_tokens_envelope` to strip extraneous `project`, `model`, `userAgent`, and `safetySettings`, eliminating top-level envelope pollution 400 errors. (Ref #3697, PR #3702, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **PipelineTokenEstimator Tool Thought Signature Accounting**: Extracted implicit thought signatures in Claude `tool_use` and `server_tool_use` blocks during token estimation, preventing deep tool sessions from undercounting and breaching 200k auto-compact thresholds. (Ref #3697, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Session Fingerprint Multimodal Stabilization & Responses Concurrency Guards**: Stabilized single-character short input fingerprints and summarized image metadata features to prevent hash avalanches; managed completions concurrency with RAII Drop Guards. (Ref #3697, PR #3699, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)  ---  -   **[基于纯净同侪校准与阶梯探针的自适应 QoS 调度系统] (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @lbjlaq)**:     -   **真实首包延迟 (TTFT) 瞬态感知**: 以发出请求至收到上游首个有效 Chunk 的纯粹时间为核心指标，彻底排除输出代码量与流式传输的干扰，作为判定静默 QoS 分桶降级的权威物理依据。(Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **五重纯净同侪基线比对与防误杀体系**: 同类账号基线严格排除自身、异构 Tier、本地禁用、429 限流/零配额、小黑屋 (Jailed)、探测中 (Probing)、瞬态可疑 (Suspicious) 以及缓刑 (HalfOpen) 账号，彻底杜绝劣币毒化基准；为深度思考模型提供 35s 动态时间补偿，杜绝推理耗时误杀；设置全局雪崩制动刹车（同行均值 >= 20s 时不滥杀）与 8s~20s 迟滞死区消除边界震荡。(Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **瞬态打标与会话即刻解绑 (Session Handover)**: 首次超标打上 `Suspicious` 瞬态可疑标记（同行算均值瞬间排除它，调度器开启“只出不进”保护）；5 分钟滑动窗口内累计 2 次超标判定处于降级池，立即强制解绑粘性会话切断死锁灌水；由 P2C 调度器在纯健康号池中重新选号并立即焊死会话绑定，后续请求稳定享受 100% Prompt Cache 极速响应。(Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **后台阶梯轻量自愈探针与半开防抖机制**: 阶梯冷却梯度为 2m -> 5m -> 10m -> 15m -> 20m -> 30m，每次升级等待时钟严格从 0 重新计时；到达节点后台发起纯文本“你好”极简探针（8 秒硬超时中止，消耗 < 5 tokens）；探针通过后进入 `Half-Open` 缓刑期，经受住连续 2 次真实请求考验（TTFT < 8s）才满血出狱；引入 30 分钟惩罚记忆，复发违规直接继承高阶梯防止高频进出。(Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **QoS 小黑屋一键解除与状态倒计时透明化**: 账号管理卡片、表格与流量监控视图实时展示 QoS 状态与剩余冷却时长，并提供一键解除小黑屋限制按钮及后台接口（`release_account_qos_jail`），完整覆盖 12 种单语语言字典。(Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[官方权威模型全景升级、分级思考传输协议与 Argon 独立配额防穿透隔离] (Fixes #3735, Ref #3720, Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @xiaozhi-xmu, @chenshin166-arch)**:     -   **模型可用性与额度解析解耦**: 彻底移除旧逻辑中 `if let Some(quota_info) = info.quota_info` 的强过滤门禁，以官方公开模型目录为基准，对 Google 上游不再为 Gemini 系（`gemini-3.8-flash-*`、`gemini-3.1-flash-lite` 等）下发 `quotaInfo` 的账号无损保留模型可用性，彻底消灭全系模型被误丢弃导致本地秒回 503 的严重缺陷。(Fixes #3737, Thanks to @xiaozhi-xmu, @chenshin166-arch, @jeikl)     -   **思考预算与思考等级分类模型传输 前的黑底白框闪烁。(Ref #3733, Ref #3738, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[跨模型切换签名逆向重构：对齐官方 Antigravity 哨兵规范，根治 Claude 并发工具 400 崩溃] (Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:     -   **官方 Base64 哨兵全局对齐**: 逆向分析官方 **异步流式并发与背压控制**: 新增 `batch_import_accounts` 高性能流式处理管道，基于 `buffer_unordered(6)` 严格控制在途活跃任务上限，将 494 个账号导入耗时由 28 分钟锐减至 15~20 秒（提速超 60 倍），彻底杜绝 `join_all` 造成的内存暴涨与 OOM 内核击毙。(Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **恢复批量导入满血配额落盘与冷启动锁解耦**: 在流式并发中每个账号并行完成 Token 换取与配额拉取，导入期 100% 具备完备配额与等级数据落盘；将 `list_accounts` 读锁改为非阻塞 `try_read()`，后台服务器启动期间绝不阻塞前端账号列表向界面的直出呈现。(Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **本地快照水合机制彻底消除冷启动图2空状态闪烁**: 在 `useAccountStore` 初始状态中引入 `localStorage` 本地快照同步水合，冷启动首帧直接渲染上一次全部账号列表与配额矩阵卡片，彻底消灭“0 总账号数”空态闪现。(Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **科学防 Google 风控体系与严格幂等性**: 注入 30ms ~ 90ms 动态散列微抖动（Jitter），集成 429 速率限制 1.5s 自动退避重试；确立小写邮箱与 Refresh Token 双重查重与原地复用（In-Place Upsert），引入孤儿文件自愈清理机制。(Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[系统托盘分层配额感知、CLI (agy) 凭证隔离与原生切号] (Fixes #3719, Fixes #3728, Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Wiojelt, @bkspi, @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq, @jeikl)**:     -   **全平台系统托盘悬停配额卡片与状态栏配额标题**: 富文本悬停卡片鼠标滑过即时呈现各模型剩余百分比，严格适配 Windows 128 字符限制；macOS 菜单栏图标旁原生常驻展示核心模型剩余百分比（如 `C:85% G:100%`）；托盘右键新增【迷你悬浮窗】一键呼出与状态双向联动。(Fixes #3719, Thanks to @Wiojelt, @jeikl)     -   **Linux 窗口抢占置顶与自动拉起根治**: 移除对外部可执行文件执行 `--version` 的危险探测，改为安全的静态文件、符号链接穿透与 `asar` 内存轻量解析，彻底消灭 Linux 桌面端主窗口被频繁抢占焦点或意外启动新实例的致命缺陷，并加入 60 秒全局版本缓存。(Fixes #3728, Thanks to @bkspi, @lbjlaq)     -   **解除经典款切号对 CLI 专属凭证的覆写覆盖**: 引入 `sync_agy_cli` 显式控制开关，切换经典款应用时严禁盲目冲刷 CLI 独立凭证，彻底杜绝切号凭据相互踩踏冲突。(Fixes #3728, Thanks to @lbjlaq)     -   **原生命令行工具集成 (`at` / `antigravity-tools`)**: 推出轻量高效的终端切号套件，支持列表查看 (`at list`)、多端当前账号探查 (`at current`) 与智能切号 (`at switch [query]`)，支持 `--continue` 原地无缝接续当前会话。(Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq) -   **[代理池防源站泄漏、多协议鉴权增强与本地 Agent 探针兼容] (Fixes #3703, Fixes #3729, Fixes #3730, PR #3708, PR #3706, Thanks to @CarlitoDon, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)**:     -   **专属绑定账号防 Direct 泄漏机制**: 重构专属代理路由决策，当绑定的专属代理不健康且无可用公共节点时，强制沿用专属代理尝试出站（宁可网络报错也绝不静默回退至直连），彻底杜绝服务器真实源站 IP 泄漏至 Google 授权系统导致的风控封号风险。(Fixes #3703, PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **WHATWG URL 凭据清理规范化与密文防扩散**: 先清除密码再清除用户名，兼容 HTTP/HTTPS 及 SOCKS5h 代理；当容器跨机器迁移导致解密失败时拒绝将 `ag_enc_` 密文随 HTTP CONNECT 报头明文发送给代理服务器。(PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Hermes Agent / 本地探针端点兼容与 OpenAI 模型上下文元数据增强**: 新增 `/props`、`/v1/props` 与 `/version`、`/v1/version` 只读端点，在 `/v1/models` 中注入 `context_length`、`max_tokens` 等权威容量限制，全面消除本地 Agent 握手探针警告与 404 错误。(PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)     -   **Gemini 协议支持标准 URL Query 传参提取 API Key**: 重构凭据提取管道，完整覆盖 `Authorization: Bearer`、`x-api-key`、`x-goog-api-key` 及 URL Query `?key=`。(Fixes #3729, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)     -   **Web/Headless 模式保存配置补全内存热更新**: 完整补齐 `only_raw_quota_models` 内存写锁同步与底层 HTTP 客户端缓存热失效逻辑，实现全平台 100% 动态热重载体验。(Fixes #3730, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq) -   **[跨平台主流 AI Agent / CLI 身份归一化与提示词特征流控防御] (Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl)**:     -   **Agent 身份归一化广谱纳管**: 针对 Google 上游对非官方 Antigravity Harness（OpenCode、OpenClaw、Grok Build、Cursor、Cascade、Cline 等）加强语义特征流控打入慢速池的问题，扩展识别原生名谓式开场与宿主集成式声明，统一收敛对齐为官方原生 Antigravity 身份，抹除竞品生态指纹。(Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl)     -   **自适应双重扫描窗口与核心提示词绝对保护**: 将系统提示词头部扫描窗口扩展为前 8 句或前 1500 字符，可靠穿透前置元数据垫片；并在流水线入口设立最高优先级白名单守卫，确保 `JeikCode` 等核心主要提示词 100% 豁免原样透传。(Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl) -   **[Gemini 上游协议严苛合规、工具拓扑保序与 Token 估算加固] (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, PR #3702, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:     -   **多轮对话角色严格交替合规 (user <-> model)**: 在上一轮为 user 时就地合并续跑提示词与 parts，防止生成连续两个 user 轮次触发 Google 400 校验拦截。(Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **原生与函数工具声明拓扑保序**: 严格保留客户端传入的自定义工具与 GoogleSearch、CodeExecution 等原生工具的原生相对优先级。(Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **Google countTokens 专属顶层信封清洗**: 引入 `align_count_tokens_envelope`，剥离外层 `project`、`model`、`userAgent` 及内层 `safetySettings`，杜绝顶层信封污染引发上游 400 报错。(Ref #3697, PR #3702, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **PipelineTokenEstimator 补全工具思考签名统计**: 在预估 Claude 协议的 `tool_use` 与 `server_tool_use` 块时，提取隐式思考签名累加至总 Tokens，解决深度工具交互下预估偏小穿透 200k 自动压缩阈值的缺陷。(Ref #3697, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)     -   **会话指纹多模态雪崩消除与 Responses 并发守卫**: 单字符短输入指纹稳定，多模态纯图片提取元数据特征摘要防止全量 Base64 序列化引发内存激增；为 completions 统一托管 RAII Drop Guard 消除幽灵并发泄露。(Ref #3697, PR #3699, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)  ## What's Changed * fix(proxy): 修复角色交替合规、工具声明保序、降级限流盲区与流终结防重 (Fixes #3695) by @cubelikeplayDaniel in ```
# v3.3.0 · 2026-10-25  ## 中文  ### 主要变化  - **失效绑定自愈**：命名会话绑定的节点拨号失败时，本次请求会额外重新选一次节点（即使 `retry_enabled: false`），成功后把绑定迁到新节点；额外那次也失败则保留原绑定，避免一次抖动就悄悄更换出口 IP。 - **失效节点立即拉黑**：上述失败同时把该节点拉黑，真实传输故障（握手/证书/协议错误、拨号被拒）按 `blacklist_duration` 直接生效，不必等 `failure_threshold` 累计；`429` 与超时/连接重置/`503` 仍只走对应冷却。新增 `pool.blacklist_failed_pin`（默认 `true`）可关闭立即拉黑、只保留自动换节点重试，面板「绑定节点拨号失败时立即拉黑」同款开关。 - **会话 ID 上限 100**：`[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}`，代理认证、管理 API 与 WebUI 校验收口一致。 - **绑定空闲续期修正**：只有成功拨号才刷新 `sticky.session_ttl`，失败连接不再给死绑定续命，LRU 顺序与过期时间重新对齐。  ### 典型现象  修复前：某个会话 ID 第一次绑定到的节点后来失效，该 ID 会一直返回 `502`，而其它会话正常；`wang.abc_1` 与 `wang.abc-1` 表现不同纯属绑到了不同节点，与会话 ID 里的下划线/短横线无关。  修复后：  text 1. 拨号仍走原绑定节点 → 失败 2. 本次请求额外选一个健康节点重试 → 成功，绑定迁移（switch_count + 1） 3. 原节点按 blacklist_failed_pin 立即拉黑（默认 24h），其它会话不再被分到它 4. 若第 2 步也失败 → 保留原绑定，下次连接继续尝试；连续失败不再续期 TTL   需要提前放回节点：面板节点列表，或 `POST /api/nodes/{tag}/release`。  ### 配置  yaml pool:   failure_threshold: 3   blacklist_duration: 24h   transient_cooldown: 60s   rate_limit_cooldown: 60s   retry_enabled: true   retry_attempts: 3   blacklist_failed_pin: true   # 命名会话绑定节点拨号失败时立即拉黑（429/超时类仍只冷却）   yaml listener:   username: wang   password: "1234"   # 仅为示例，请更换 sticky:   session_enabled: true   session_ttl: 24h   会话 ID 现在支持 1–100 个 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` 字符；`基础用户名 + 点号 + ID` 仍需落在 SOCKS5 的 255 字节用户名上限内。  ### 升级注意  1. 备份挂载的配置目录并保留 `node_ports.json`；升级不会改动已有会话绑定（但重建容器会清空内存绑定）。 2. Compose 可固定 `image: ghcr.io/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies:3.3.0`；镜像发布任务完成后执行：     bash    docker compose pull easy_proxies    docker compose up -d --force-recreate easy_proxies      3. 旧版本（≤3.2.0）对超过 64 位的会话 ID 仍返回 `407`；`blacklist_failed_pin` 对旧版本无效，需要在升级后的二进制上生效。 4. 工具链要求 **Go 1.26.10+**（修复 `crypto/x509` 的 GO-2026-6809），Docker builder 同步固定为 1.26.10。 4. `blacklist_failed_pin: false` 只是关闭“立即拉黑”，自动换节点重试仍然生效；节点会回到 `failure_threshold` 累计逻辑。  交付延续 GitHub 源码归档和 GHCR `linux/amd64`、`linux/arm64` 镜像，不附独立二进制下载资产。  ### 验证范围  本地验证使用临时配置、合成节点/MMDB、回环服务与单元测试夹具；没有使用真实订阅做公网可用性、吞吐或长期稳定性验证。发布工作流要求默认/生产标签测试、race、vet、UI/入口 fixture、可达漏洞扫描及 Linux amd64/arm64 构建通过，远端检查与镜像状态以 GitHub Actions 实际结果为准。  ## English  ### Highlights  - **Stale pins heal themselves**: when a named session's bound node fails to dial, that request gets one extra selection even with `retry_enabled: false`, and a successful re-route migrates the binding; if that attempt fails too, the original pin is kept so a single blip does not silently change the exit IP. - **Failed node blacklisted immediately**: the same failure blacklists the node. Transport faults (handshake/cert/protocol errors, refused dials) apply `blacklist_duration` right away instead of waiting for `failure_threshold`; 429 and timeout/connection-reset/503 keep their cooldowns. New `pool.blacklist_failed_pin` (default `true`) turns the immediate blacklist off while keeping the re-route; the pool settings panel exposes the same switch. - **Session IDs up to 100 characters**: `[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}`, applied consistently in proxy auth, the management API, and WebUI validation. - **Idle renewal fixed**: only a successful dial refreshes `sticky.session_ttl`; failed attempts no longer keep a dead pin alive, and LRU order stays aligned with binding expiry.  ### Typical symptom this fixes  Before: a session ID whose first binding later died kept answering `502` while other sessions worked. `wang.abc_1` and `wang.abc-1` behaving differently was never about the underscore or hyphen — they had simply pinned different nodes.  After:  text 1. dial the bound node -> fails 2. this request tries one fresh healthy node -> succeeds, binding migrates (switch_count + 1) 3. the old node is blacklisted per blacklist_failed_pin (default 24h); other sessions stop seeing it 4. if step 2 also fails -> the pin is kept, the next request retries; repeated failures no longer renew the TTL   Release a node early from the node list in the panel or with `POST /api/nodes/{tag}/release`.  ### Configuration  yaml pool:   failure_threshold: 3   blacklist_duration: 24h   transient_cooldown: 60s   rate_limit_cooldown: 60s   retry_enabled: true   retry_attempts: 3   blacklist_failed_pin: true   # immediate blacklist for a failed session pin (429/timeouts keep their cooldown)   yaml listener:   username: wang   password: "1234"   # example only, change it sticky:   session_enabled: true   session_ttl: 24h   Session IDs now accept 1–100 `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` characters; `base username + dot + ID` must still fit the SOCKS5 255-byte username field.  ### Upgrading  1. Back up the mounted configuration directory and keep `node_ports.json`; existing bindings are untouched, but recreating the container clears in-memory bindings. 2. Pin `image: ghcr.io/jasonwong1991/easy_proxies:3.3.0`, then after the image workflow finishes:     bash    docker compose pull easy_proxies    docker compose up -d --force-recreate easy_proxies      3. Builds ≤3.2.0 still answer `407` for session IDs longer than 64 characters, and ignore `blacklist_failed_pin`; both need the upgraded binary. 4. The toolchain requirement is **Go 1.26.10+** (fixes `crypto/x509` GO-2026-6809); the Docker builder is pinned to 1.26.10 as well. 4. `blacklist_failed_pin: false` only disables the immediate blacklist — the automatic re-route stays, and the node returns to the `failure_threshold` path.  Published as source on GitHub plus `linux/amd64`/`linux/arm64` images on GHCR, without standalone binary assets.  ### Validation  Local verification used temporary configuration, synthetic nodes/MMDB, loopback services, and unit-test fixtures; real subscriptions were not exercised for public reachability, throughput, or long-running soak. The release workflow requires default/production-tag tests, race tests, vet, UI/entrypoint fixtures, reachable vulnerability scanning, and Linux amd64/arm64 builds to pass; remote check and image status follow the actual GitHub Actions result. 
```
