### 2026-10-22T11:07:14Z [**SUDOKU-ASCII/sudoku**](https://github.com/SUDOKU-ASCII/sudoku)

```
## **SUDOKU一岁啦！Happy Birthday！**  > **sudoku**从2025年10月22日项目建立到11月底第一个release，再到今天，sudoku迎来了**v0.5.2**版本，但为了图个喜庆，我们将此版本定为**v0.5.2.0**(520在中文里的谐音是我爱你)，以此表达对支持sudoku的用户的感谢。  ---  ## 有个好消息和一个坏消息  - 好消息是sudoku从`v0.5.1->v0.5.2.0`经历了上百个commit，大幅优化了一系列性能和占用，并引入了用户管理、外部api、全新的一键脚本TUI、全新设计的官方[安卓客户端](https://github.com/SUDOKU-ASCII/sudoku-android/releases)、[桌面客户端](https://github.com/SUDOKU-ASCII/sudoku-desktop/releases)等等，并同步把性能优化同步带到了目前支持sudoku的所有内核，也就是说接下来sudoku相关的使用体验会更加丝滑，由于用户管理和api的引入使得机场使用、拼车部署变得更方便。    - 坏消息则是我们一次性在v0.5.2.0更新了太多，以至于~~原计划一直更新到明年的计划现在都更新完了~~，再加上后续开发人员有限，原本月更的sudoku也许会进入更新间隔周期更长的维护期，类似于半停更。  - 但不用担心，v0.5.2.0的sudoku就最初原型协议设计的理念来看已经是非常理想的结果了，团队对现版本都很满意。  ---  ## 更新概览：  feat(server). 新增 users 白名单机制，支持按用户哈希识别并配置别名、带宽限制与每月流量配额；  feat(cli). 重构 -tui 交互向导，采用分页确认流程并预设 HTTP mask 自动模式；  feat(controller). 实现 Clash/mihomo 语义的外部控制器，支持第三方面板接入及服务端用户状态监控；  feat(config). 支持可选的节点名称 (name/n)，兼容旧版短链接解析；  perf(uot). 优化 UoT 数据报写入为单次操作并引入批量写入队列；缓存域名解析，显著提升 UDP 吞吐；  perf(memory). 移除空闲连接缓冲区并引入 sync.Pool 借用 record 帧；大幅降低 RSS 内存占用；  perf(codec). Sudoku 表查找改为位集并采用 wyrand 无分支路径；优化解码块处理，提升编解码效率；  perf(mux). 实现并发小帧合并写入并统一 keepalive 实现；降低单条 stream 内存占用；  perf(httpmask). 优化空闲 session 唤醒机制与缓冲池化；预构造请求头并精简定时器回收；  perf(handshake). 减少 KIP 与 OpenTCP 写入次数；实现地址编解码零分配，优化探测缓冲分配；  perf(app). UDP 中继与日志格式化实现零分配；优化路由决策路径与请求读取器复用；  perf(geodata). 引入不可变快照与写时复制域名集；纯文本列表绕过 YAML 解析，匹配过程零分配；  perf(dns). 缓存渲染后地址并合并并发同名查询，限制缓存大小；  perf(reverse). 响应改写按 Content-Length 分配缓冲并使用惰性拼接器；  refactor(httpmask). 拆分职责文件并合并 splitConn 逻辑；抽离 sessionStore 并简化重试机制；  refactor(tunnel). 统一握手 hello 交换并集中连接包装；合并 dialBase 变体与密钥派生逻辑；  refactor(app). 引入 routeAction 类型并重构 SOCKS/Mixed 处理函数；按数据报拆分 UDP 中继；  refactor(protocol). UoT、SOCKS5 UDP 与 KIP 共用统一的 IP 地址编码器；  refactor(geodata). 将 manager 拆分为解析、缓存、下载、匹配与地址段五个模块；  refactor(reverse). 将 manager 拆分为管理、路由与代理模块，并将响应改写移入独立子包；  refactor(config). 细化 Finalize 步骤并统一枚举规整逻辑；  fix(app). 修复 Accept 失败空转问题；处理 SOCKS4 超长请求并修复 HTTP CONNECT 字节丢失；  fix(geodata). 限制规则下载大小为 32MiB；固定关键字匹配顺序并修复 srcset 索引处理；  fix(config). Save 函数现在正确返回 Close 错误；  fix(dns). 移除内置 DoT 支持，统一默认使用 DoH；  fix(httpmask). 确保每个 session 仅持有一个 pull 租约并移除 downlinkMu；  build(deps). 升级 Go 至 1.27.2 并更新 huh、websocket 等核心依赖；  ---  ## **SUDOKU Turns One! Happy Birthday!**  > From the project’s creation on October 22, 2025, through its first release at the end of November, and now, **sudoku** has reached **v0.5.2**. To mark the occasion, though, we’re calling this release **v0.5.2.0**—“520” sounds like “I love you” in Chinese—as a way to thank everyone who supports sudoku.  ---  ## Good News and Bad News  - The good news: sudoku has gone through hundreds of commits between `v0.5.1` and `v0.5.2.0`, bringing major performance and resource-usage improvements. It also adds user management, an external API, a completely redesigned one-click script TUI, a brand-new official Android client, and more. These performance improvements have also been carried over to all currently supported sudoku cores. In other words, using sudoku should feel much smoother from here on. With user management and the API, using it with proxy services and setting up shared deployments is now more convenient, too.  - The bad news: we packed so much into `v0.5.2.0` that ~~we’ve already finished everything we had planned to work on through next year~~. And with a limited number of developers available going forward, sudoku—which has previously received monthly updates—may enter a maintenance phase with longer gaps between releases, almost like a pause in development.  - But don’t worry: judged by the design philosophy of the original prototype protocol, sudoku in `v0.5.2.0` is already a very solid result, and the team is happy with the current version.  ---  ## Update Overview  feat(server). Added a `users` allowlist mechanism, with user identification by hash and support for aliases, bandwidth limits, and monthly traffic quotas;  feat(cli). Reworked the `-tui` interactive wizard with a paginated confirmation flow and HTTP mask automatic mode enabled by default;  feat(controller). Implemented an external controller with Clash/mihomo semantics, supporting integration with third-party dashboards and server-side user-status monitoring;  feat(config). Added optional node names (`name/n`) while maintaining compatibility with legacy short-link parsing;  perf(uot). Optimized UoT datagram writes into a single operation and added a batched write queue; cached domain lookups to significantly improve UDP throughput;  perf(memory). Removed idle connection buffers and introduced `sync.Pool` borrowing for record frames, substantially reducing RSS memory usage;  perf(codec). Replaced Sudoku table lookups with bitsets and adopted a branchless wyrand path; optimized decode-block processing to improve encoding and decoding efficiency;  perf(mux). Implemented concurrent small-frame write coalescing and unified keepalive handling, reducing memory usage per stream;  perf(httpmask). Optimized idle-session wakeups and buffer pooling; prebuilt request headers and streamlined timer cleanup;  perf(handshake). Reduced the number of KIP and OpenTCP writes; implemented zero-allocation address encoding and decoding, and optimized probe-buffer allocation;  perf(app). Made UDP relaying and log formatting allocation-free; optimized the routing decision path and reused request readers;  perf(geodata). Introduced immutable snapshots and copy-on-write domain sets; plain-text lists now bypass YAML parsing, and matching is allocation-free;  perf(dns). Cached rendered addresses, coalesced concurrent queries for the same name, and capped cache size;  perf(reverse). Response rewriting now allocates buffers based on `Content-Length` and uses a lazy concatenator;  refactor(httpmask). Split files by responsibility and consolidated `splitConn` logic; extracted `sessionStore` and simplified retry handling;  refactor(tunnel). Unified the handshake hello exchange and centralized connection wrapping; consolidated `dialBase` variants and key-derivation logic;  refactor(app). Introduced the `routeAction` type and refactored SOCKS/Mixed handlers; split UDP relaying by datagram;  refactor(protocol). UoT, SOCKS5 UDP, and KIP now share a unified IP address encoder;  refactor(geodata). Split the manager into five modules: parsing, caching, downloading, matching, and address ranges;  refactor(reverse). Split the manager into management, routing, and proxy modules, and moved response rewriting into its own subpackage;  refactor(config). Refined the `Finalize` steps and unified enum normalization logic;  fix(app). Fixed a busy loop when `Accept` fails; added handling for oversized SOCKS4 requests and fixed lost bytes in HTTP CONNECT;  fix(geodata). Capped rule-download size at 32 MiB; fixed keyword matching order and corrected `srcset` index handling;  fix(config). The `Save` function now correctly returns errors from `Close`;  fix(dns). Removed built-in DoT support and standardized on DoH by default;  fix(httpmask). Ensured each session holds only one pull lease and removed `downlinkMu`;  build(deps). Upgraded Go to 1.27.2 and updated core dependencies, including huh and websocket; 
```

### 2026-10-26T10:32:25Z [**kunkundi/crossdesk**](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)

```
**v1.7.3-patch-1** **问题修复：**  1. 修复 Windows 以管理员权限运行时，局域网身份文件所有者异常导致 IP 直连初始化失败的问题。新建文件明确归当前用户所有，并兼容处理符合条件的旧版身份文件。 2. 更新 MiniRTC，修复 WebSocket++ 头文件在 C++20 下的构建兼容性问题。 3. 修复 Android 签名流程对带补丁号版本的校验，支持补丁版本正常签名和发布。  ---  **Bug Fixes:**  1. Fixed direct LAN initialization on Windows when identity files created by an elevated process were owned by the Administrators group. New files explicitly belong to the current user, and compatible legacy files are repaired. 2. Updated MiniRTC to fix WebSocket++ header compatibility with C++20 builds. 3. Fixed Android signing version validation to support signing and publishing hotfix releases.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog：** [v1.7.3-20261026…v1.7.3-1-20261026](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.3-20261026...v1.7.3-1-20261026)  --- **v1.7.3** **新增功能与改进：**  1. 桌面端新增局域网 IP 直连，支持在无需连接信令服务器的情况下，通过 IPv4 地址建立远程会话。提供独立连接密码、记住密码、多网卡地址选择及每个 IP 的端口设置，近期连接按 ID / IP 模式分别保存和显示。 2. 新增“连接断开后锁屏”选项，默认开启。最后一个远程控制端断开后，自动请求锁定被控电脑；可在常规设置中关闭。 3. H.264 默认优先使用硬件编解码，不可用时回退到软件，完善 Windows 硬件编解码支持。普通会话与桌面墙的网络状态新增实际编码器、解码器信息，方便查看当前使用的编解码方式。 4. 桌面端所有窗口和控件统一采用扁平化风格，移除拟物阴影和装饰渐变，通过纯色背景、清晰边框与焦点状态区分交互。新增 ID / IP 连接模式滑动切换，优化远程会话工具栏、控件对比度和多语言文案。突出断开连接入口，并在关闭远程会话或桌面墙时增加确认提示，减少误操作。 5. 移动端新增“连接后自动横屏”设置，可选择进入远程会话时自动切换至横屏，改善手机上的远程操作体验。  **问题修复：**  1. 改善远程键鼠输入的响应与释放处理，避免输入操作阻塞网络传输；完善多控制端场景下的按键释放与重试，减少按键或鼠标按钮未正确松开的情况。 2. 修复鼠标操作经过远程工具栏等控制浮层时，拖动等手势被意外中断的问题。 3. 修复应用空闲时仍可能持续阻止系统休眠的问题，仅在实际共享屏幕期间保持被控电脑唤醒。 4. 修复设备名称中的中文等 Unicode 字符丢失，以及 iOS 连接密码输入不便输入英文字母的问题。 5. 改善 Windows 关机确认与取消过程中的远程连接保持，完善相关进程退出顺序，并修复部分硬件编码器输出格式变化的处理问题。 6. 修复近期连接缩略图在不同显示缩放比例下的适配，统一网络状态中的分辨率显示，并完善 Web 客户端文件传输支持。  **使用提示：**  - IP 直连目前适用于 Windows、macOS、Linux 的普通桌面客户端，双方均需升级到支持此功能的版本，并确保 IPv4 地址可互相访问。当前不支持主机名或 IPv6；默认端口为 **52888**，请以被控端界面显示的实际端口为准。浏览器、移动端、Windows 登录前服务及 Linux 无头控制台继续使用设备 ID 连接。 - IP 直连默认开启，使用独立的 **6 位英文字母或数字密码，区分大小写**。切回 ID 模式不会停止 IP 监听；如需停用，请在“设置 → 常规 → 网络连接”中关闭 IP 直连。 - “连接断开后锁屏”默认开启，如不需要可在“设置 → 常规 → 隐私保护”中关闭。Linux 自动锁屏需要当前桌面环境提供可用的锁屏服务。  ---  **New Features and Improvements:**  1. Added direct LAN connections to desktop clients, allowing remote sessions over IPv4 without connecting to a signaling server. Includes an independent connection password, saved passwords, network interface selection, and per-address port settings. Recent connections are stored and displayed separately for ID and IP modes. 2. Added “Lock screen on disconnect,” enabled by default. The host requests a screen lock after its last remote controller disconnects. This option can be disabled in General settings. 3. H.264 now prefers hardware codecs and falls back to software when unavailable, with improved hardware codec support on Windows. Network statistics in individual sessions and DeskHub now show the actual encoder and decoder in use. 4. Applied a consistent flat design across all desktop windows and controls, replacing skeuomorphic shadows and decorative gradients with solid surfaces, clear borders, and distinct focus states. Added a sliding ID/IP connection-mode toggle and refined the session toolbar, control contrast, and translations. Disconnect actions are more prominent, and closing a remote session or DeskHub now requires confirmation to reduce accidental disconnections. 5. Added a mobile setting to automatically enter landscape orientation when a remote session connects, improving remote control on phones.  **Bug Fixes:**  1. Improved remote keyboard and mouse responsiveness and release handling, preventing input operations from blocking network transport. Improved key release and retry handling with multiple controllers to reduce stuck keys and mouse buttons. 2. Fixed dragging and other mouse gestures being interrupted when crossing the remote toolbar or other control overlays. 3. Fixed the application potentially preventing system sleep while idle. Sleep inhibition now applies only while the host is actively sharing its screen. 4. Fixed loss of Chinese and other Unicode characters in device names, and improved entry of passwords containing letters on iOS. 5. Improved remote connection preservation during Windows shutdown confirmation and cancellation, refined process shutdown ordering, and fixed output format change handling for some hardware encoders. 6. Improved recent-connection thumbnails at different display scales, standardized resolution formatting in network statistics, and improved file transfer support for Web clients.  **Usage Notes:**  - Direct IP connections currently support standard Windows, macOS, and Linux desktop clients. Both endpoints must use a version supporting this feature and have mutually reachable IPv4 addresses. Hostnames and IPv6 are not supported. The default port is **52888**; use the actual port shown on the host. Browser and mobile clients, pre-login Windows services, and the Linux headless console continue to use device IDs. - Direct IP connections are enabled by default and use an independent, **case-sensitive, six-character alphanumeric password**. Switching back to ID mode keeps IP listeners active. To stop them, disable direct IP connections under Settings → General → Network. - “Lock screen on disconnect” is enabled by default and can be disabled under Settings → General → Privacy. Automatic locking on Linux requires an available screen-lock service in the current desktop environment.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog：** [[v1.7.2-20261022…v1.7.3-20261026](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.2-20261022...v1.7.3-20261026)](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.2-20261022...v1.7.3-20261026)  
```

### 2026-10-24T00:50:05Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.15.0  You can now install OpenSpec without Node.js, using one command, and `openspec update` can now show what it would change before changing anything.  ### New  - **Standalone install** - Install OpenSpec with one command and no Node.js: `curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/main/scripts/binary/install.sh | sh` on macOS and Linux, or `irm https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/main/scripts/binary/install.ps1 | iex` on Windows. Every release now ships binaries for macOS, Linux (glibc and musl) and Windows on x64 and arm64, with `SHA256SUMS`. npm and the other package managers keep working as before. - **`openspec update --dry-run`** - Reports the files an update would add, change or delete without writing anything; add `--json` for machine-readable output.  ### Improved  - **Faster `--json` commands** - `status`, `instructions`, `new change`, `templates`, `validate` and `schema` no longer load the spinner package when run with `--json`, so they start a little faster. Output is unchanged. - **`openspec feedback`** - Filed issues now match the bug form, and the manual-submission link opens the bug form with your message, version and environment filled in.  ### Fixed  - **`openspec new change`** - The progress line now names the schema the change is actually created with, instead of always saying `spec-driven`. - **`/opsx:verify`** - A REMOVED requirement is matched to its baseline by its exact name, so a similarly named replacement is no longer mistaken for it.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.14.3...v1.15.0 
```

### 2026-10-26T13:17:44Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.11.1 fixes three security problems. All three were found and reported by @Gsirawan, and I am very grateful for the careful reports. Please update, then restart the daemon with `tuios restart-server`. The fixes are in the daemon, so an old daemon keeps the old behaviour.  This release also has the fixes that landed after v0.11.0: floating panes in tiling mode, a calmer cursor while an agent works, the dim and borders with no theme, Mermaid in TML surfaces, and more. Read [Security](#security) first.  ## Install  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.11.1 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.11.1   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`. Then run `tuios restart-server` to move the daemon to the new build.  ## Security  ### A linked machine could read and overwrite files  v0.11.1 fixes [GHSA-5q2q-f2hv-wf4h](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-5q2q-f2hv-wf4h). Its severity is high. It affects v0.8.0 to v0.11.0. Thanks to @Gsirawan for the report.  - A linked machine or a paired phone with only the `list` or `mail` class could read any file on this machine through `stash-put`. - With only `list`, it could overwrite a file with a pane's screen text through the `out` path of `screenshot`. For example, it could replace `~/.ssh/authorized_keys`. - With only `list`, it could read the changed lines of fan worktrees through `compare-fan` with `detail`. These can hold secrets, such as a `.env` file. - Now a linked machine sends stash content as bytes. It cannot choose a screenshot path, and the picture goes under `screenshot.directory`. It needs `write` for the changed lines of a fan. A test holds every `list` and `mail` verb to the same rule ([#1163](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1163)).  What you notice: nothing, in normal use. `tuios stash put` to another machine already sends bytes. Commands that you run on this machine do not change.  ### A repository could run its own programs outside the pane  v0.11.1 fixes [GHSA-qcx6-vr32-xr8q](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-qcx6-vr32-xr8q). Its severity is high. It affects v0.8.0 to v0.11.0. Thanks to @Gsirawan for the report.  - tuios runs git in a pane's repository for checkpoints, reviews, counts and new worktrees. A program in the pane can change that repository, including `.git/hooks` and `.git/config`. - So the repository could name a hook, a filter or a textconv program that the daemon ran. The program ran outside the pane, and outside any sandbox that the pane's agent ran in. A checkpoint at the end of an agent's turn was enough, with no action from you. - Text shown in a pane could also set the folder that the New agent sheet used, so the sheet could run git in another repository. - Now tuios runs its own git with no hooks. A filter, textconv or diff program that only the repository's own config names does not run. diff, log and show get `--no-ext-diff` and `--no-textconv`. The New agent sheet, the sidebar git section and the daemon use the folder the pane's shell is in, not the folder that the pane reports ([#1164](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1164)).  What you notice:  - A filter from your global git config, such as Git LFS, still runs. - In a repository with its own filter, such as git-crypt, checkpoints and reviews read the files as they are on disk. - `tuios ship` and the other ship commands that you run yourself keep the repository's config and hooks. A call from inside a pane does not.  ### Text in a pane could type a command at the prompt  v0.11.1 fixes [GHSA-5cvj-c8rj-c749](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-5cvj-c8rj-c749). Its severity is high. It affects tuios v0.7.0 to v0.11.0, and `tuios.dev/vt` v0.3.1 and earlier. Thanks to @Gsirawan for the report.  - A program asks for the clipboard with an OSC 52 query. The terminal writes the answer to the pane's input, as if you typed it. - The answer copied the selection field of the query as it was. So text in a file that you `cat`, a log line or the output of an ssh session could put a command on your shell prompt. One Enter ran it. - A program in the pane could also set the pane's copy of the clipboard with OSC 52 and then ask for it, to put its own text on the prompt. - Now `tuios.dev/vt` keeps only the selection names `c`, `p`, `q`, `s` and `0` to `7`, as xterm does. A pane keeps no copy of the clipboard, and an OSC 52 query gets empty content back, as in xterm ([#1165](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1165)).  What you notice: a program that reads the clipboard with an OSC 52 query gets empty content. A program that sets the clipboard with OSC 52 works as before, under `appearance.selection.osc52_write`. If you use `tuios.dev/vt` in your own program, update it to v0.3.2.  ## Fixes  - **Floating panes in tiling mode.** An `alt` drag on a floating pane resizes it at once, and the tiled panes behind it keep their size. A press on a floating pane's edge grabs the edge ([#1150](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1150)). - **The cursor while an agent works.** The rail shimmer drew 15 frames a second, and each frame moved the cursor to the rail and back. It now draws 8 frames a second, and draws nothing when pane output drew a frame already. With one working agent, tuios sends about half as many frames ([#1155](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1155), which fixes [#1140](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/1140)). Thanks to @Soleone for the report. - **Hover on a wide or busy screen.** Hover over the settings could still trail the pointer on a wide screen or a loaded machine. The motion budget now counts from the end of the frame's write ([#1123](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1123), [#1137](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1137), which fix [#1058](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/1058)). - **Dim and borders with no theme.** With `theme = "none"`, `dim_unfocused` now dims text in the default colour, with the colours your terminal reports. A translucent terminal shows no opaque block. The focused and unfocused borders are now easy to tell apart, on no theme and on the shipped themes ([#1146](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1146)). - **Stale Pi rows.** When you quit Pi soon after it started, its agent row could stay in the rail. A late hook no longer keeps the row ([#1149](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1149)). - **Mermaid in TML.** A `mermaid` element in a TML surface now draws the diagram, not its source. A long `kv` value wraps ([#1147](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1147)). - **Read a TML surface as text.** `tuios capture-pane` on a TML pane prints the surface as drawn. `tuios tml render FILE` prints a document as text and opens nothing. So an agent can read a surface before and after it shows it ([#1148](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1148)). - **Long TML documents.** A document taller than its pane scrolls as a page, with a scroll bar. A TML popup minimizes to the dock ([#1130](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1130)). - **`tuios mermaid` in a pane.** Diagrams stack in a pane without cuts, at the size of the terminal's text. `--background none` draws a picture on the terminal's own background ([#1132](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1132)). - **A new worktree from a pane.** `ctrl+b U` makes a worktree of the focused pane's repository, with a new branch and a session in it. The action is `new_worktree` ([#1161](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1161)). Thanks to @Soleone for the request. - **The gilt theme.** `theme = "gilt"` is a dark theme of obsidian, parchment and gold ([#1159](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1159)). Thanks to @Soleone. - **A settings crash.** tuios no longer panics when `appearance.scroll_column_max = 20` ([#1153](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1153), which fixes [#1152](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/1152)). Thanks to @z10-arch. - **herdr `agent.prompt`.** The herdr compat API now types a prompt into a working agent, as herdr 0.9.5 does. A blocked agent is refused with `agent_blocked` ([#1142](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1142), which fixes [#1138](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/1138)). Thanks to @chambers75 for the report. - **Pi threads.** The Inbox reads transcripts of newer Pi versions, and follows the entry that `/tree` selects. A tool result that arrives late now updates the card that was drawn already ([#1120](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1120), [#1134](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1134), [#1135](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1135)). Thanks to @AdrielVelazquez. - **Focus reports.** A program in a pane hears when the host terminal loses focus and gets it back. Neovim's `FocusLost` now fires when you switch to another application ([#1131](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1131)). - **Session size.** Under `window_size = latest`, a session that you leave and come back to keeps its size ([#1072](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1072), which fixes [#1071](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/1071)). Thanks to @udiNur. - **The first session.** A bare `tuios` starts a new default session in the folder you run it from ([#1122](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1122), which fixes [#1112](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/1112)). Thanks to @agammann. - **The first prompt.** A new agent started with a task gets the task. Claude Code no longer leaves it unsent in its input box ([#1126](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1126)). - **Pasted images.** An image pasted into a reply shows its picture at once ([#1127](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1127)). The `@` list in a reply works as a menu ([#1129](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1129)). - **The files section.** The rail's files section is a tree that scrolls with the cursor ([#1124](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1124)). - **Plugins.** `tuios plugins uninstall ID` removes a plugin ([#1125](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1125)). - **sketch and pele 1.10.0.** `tuios mermaid` follows pele 1.10.0 ([#1156](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1156)). A narrow label breaks at a hyphen or a space before it breaks inside a word ([#1089](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1089)). Thanks to @shubham-tomar.  ## Performance  - **Pane output memory.** Each daemon pane's output queue in the client starts empty and grows with its backlog. Before, each pane reserved about 229 KB when it opened ([#1160](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1160)). Thanks to @Gsirawan.  ## tuios-web  - **The worker engine, opt in.** tuios-web moves to sip v0.10.0, which has a new renderer. It runs the terminal and the connection in Web Workers, so a flood of output does not freeze the page. In my test with 50 MiB of output, the page's main thread went from 6,788 ms to 10 ms, and the longest frame gap went from 250 ms to 16.8 ms. The default is unchanged. To try it, open the page with `?renderer=engine`, or set Renderer to engine in the settings panel ([#1162](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1162)).  ## Upgrading  Run `tuios restart-server` after you update. The security fixes are in the daemon. The command shows which programs stop and what comes back, and asks first.  ## Contributors  Thank you to everyone who sent a pull request for this release:  - @Gsirawan: the three security reports, and the pane output queue ([#1160](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1160)). - @AdrielVelazquez: Pi threads in the Inbox ([#1120](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1120), [#1134](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1134), [#1135](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1135)). - @Soleone: the gilt theme ([#1159](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1159)). - @z10-arch: the settings meter fix ([#1153](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1153)). - @udiNur: the session size under `window_size = latest` ([#1072](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1072)). - @agammann: the folder of the first session ([#1117](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1117), landed as [#1122](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1122)). - @shubham-tomar: label breaks in sketch ([#1089](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1089)).  And thanks to @Soleone for the cursor report and the worktree request, @chambers75 for the herdr report, @MunsuBahk for the first session report, @reflog for the mouse lag report, and @srackham for the focus report (discussion [#1128](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/1128)).  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.11.0...v0.11.1
```

### 2026-10-26T08:27:19Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.8.1  GPUI Kit 0.8.1 improves text selection, Markdown rendering, editor layout, scrolling, focus and accessibility. It also adds compatible customization APIs for text rendering, editor scrollbars and Diff parsing and highlighting.  To preserve Kit-owned API compatibility with v0.8.0, this patch defers the `MotionTokens` field additions from #3616 and the Textarea associated-type change from #2842. Behavior fixes remain included. The default backend is pinned to gpui-pre 0.3.10, and the optional GPUI Fast backend uses 0.1.12. These upstream snapshots change several GPUI APIs re-exported by Kit; see the migration notes below.  ## Editor and text input  - **Tab stops**: code-editor tabs align to the configured tab stops during measurement, wrapping and painting. Horizontal scroll extents follow laid-out text widths. ([#3622](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3622), [#3604](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3604)) - **Custom scrollbars**: `set_scrollbar_renderer` lets an application supply its editor scrollbar presentation using `EditorScrollbarContext`. ([#3644](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3644)) - Focused inputs ask surrounding scroll containers to reveal them, taps request the software keyboard, and accessibility semantics follow the actual editor focus. ([#3625](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3625), [#3623](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3623), [#3585](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3585))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - **Selection**: publish selection snapshots once per frame to avoid invalidating unrelated retained views. Double- and triple-click drags and subsequent Shift-clicks extend selections by words, lines or paragraphs, including paragraphs with hard breaks. Drags starting inside a table cell stay in that cell. ([#3686](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3686), [#3681](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3681), [#3646](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3646)) - **Text styles**: `inline_code_style`, `list_style` and `blockquote_style` refine the corresponding text presentation while inheriting unspecified properties from the resolved style. ([#3663](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3663), [#3687](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3687)) - **Image sources**: Component TextView exposes `image_source` to resolve document images consistently for measurement and rendering. ([#3694](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3694)) - Preserve sparse Markdown table rows and release shaped text retained by dropped TextViews on the next frame. ([#3673](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3673), [#3628](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3628))  ## Diff  - **Truncated patches**: `DiffFile::parse_with` and `DiffParseOptions::truncated_hunks` accept incomplete hunks without relaxing the default parser. `DiffFile::is_truncated` identifies affected files; unified file boundaries and empty-hunk ordering remain validated. ([#3688](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3688)) - **Syntax highlighting**: `with_syntax_highlighter`, `set_syntax_highlighter` and `clear_syntax_highlighter` let an application provide its own highlighting without resetting selection or scrolling. ([#3618](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3618))  ## Scrolling and layout  - Scrollable regions can scroll content beyond their maximum size. Bounce handling avoids rounding-induced overscroll and false overscroll at large offsets, and release inertia applies only to stretched edges. ([#3613](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3613), [#3632](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3632), [#3661](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3661), [#3631](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3631)) - `last_horizontal_scroll_claim` exposes the last horizontal scroll step consumed by a mask. Resizable dividers read the active drag state when events arrive. ([#3621](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3621), [#3629](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3629)) - `on_prepaint` reports bounds from the element's corner, aligning geometry callbacks with their intended coordinate origin. ([#3689](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3689))  ## Components  - **Dialog**: `Dialog::centered` and `AlertDialog::centered` center the surface vertically; exit animation matches entrance animation. Sheets and notifications avoid system bars and the software keyboard. ([#3635](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3635), [#3603](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3603), [#3624](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3624)) - **Button**: `track_focus` accepts an application-owned handle. Disabling a focused button preserves the focus binding so Tab can still leave it in either direction. ([#3684](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3684), [#3606](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3606)) - **DataTable**: honor centered and right-aligned columns; expose table structure and cell labels to assistive technology through `TableDelegate::cell_label`. ([#3617](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3617), [#3667](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3667)) - **Toolbar**: grow with taller content and preserve focus when blank toolbar space is clicked. ([#3651](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3651), [#3652](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3652)) - **HoverCard**: `trigger_style` applies layout styling to the trigger container. ([#3653](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3653)) - **ColorPicker**: preserve the first hex edit after a programmatic value update and round color channels instead of truncating them. ([#3641](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3641), [#3640](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3640)) - **TimeField**: pressing outside a segment focuses the first segment. **Command** applies empty-list padding only while its empty state is visible. ([#3655](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3655), [#3654](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3654)) - **Focus trap**: stop Tab traversal at the first repeated stop. **Dock** restores legacy empty StackPanel layouts as empty splits. ([#3642](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3642), [#3610](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3610))  ## Shell and localization  The release also prepares `gpui-shell` for crates.io publication with matching `quickjs-jit`, `quickjs-jit-runtime` and `quickjs-jit-stdlib` 0.14.1 registry dependencies.  - **Shell Button**: `accessibility_label` supplies an accessible name independently of the visible label. ([#3665](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3665)) - Add Brazilian Portuguese translations and preserve valid Portuguese words in spell checking. ([#3638](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3638), [#3664](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3664))  ## Upstream GPUI compatibility  The updated GPUI snapshots change public APIs re-exported at the Kit root. Internal compilation checks and Kit API checks do not preserve these upstream signatures:  - `Modifiers::secondary()` and `Modifiers::secondary_key()` now require `&App`. - `Keystroke::parse` and `KeyBinding::new` require static strings. Dynamic strings and `secondary` modifiers use OS-aware parsing/loading. - `Keystroke::unparse` and `KeybindingKeystroke::unparse` require an `OperatingSystem` argument. - `PlatformInput::ScrollWheel` becomes `PlatformInput::Scroll`; scroll event literals gain a `source` field.  Use `cx.platform_info().os` for OS-aware parsing/formatting and pass `cx` to modifier helpers. Use `KeyBinding::load(..., cx)` for dynamic keybindings or choose explicit `cmd`/`ctrl` literals with `KeyBinding::new`.  Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!  ## What's Changed * typos: Ignore the two release-notes contributor handles by @noosxe in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3608 * dock: Read the legacy empty `StackPanel` as an empty split by @obito-t in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3610 * input: align accessibility semantics with editor focus by @TomiXRM in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3585 * dialog: Animate the exit to match the entrance by @YidaWeng in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3603 * input: Measure the horizontal scroll extent from laid-out lines by @YidaWeng in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3604 * button: preserve Tab traversal when the focused control becomes disabled by @TomiXRM in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3606 * table: Apply Column::text_right and text_center in DataTable by @Elitelord in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3617 * diff: Let the application supply the syntax highlighter by @bcl-dev in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3618 * scroll: Expose the last horizontal strip scroll step a mask claimed by @yuanzhuang-svg in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3621 * story: Add an application-owned completion menu to the Input story by @noosxe in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3607 * scroll: Scroll content past a scrollable's maximum size by @UncertaintyDeterminesYou4ndMe in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3613 * input: Ask for the virtual keyboard when an input is tapped by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3623 * sheet, notification: Keep out from under the system bars and the keyboard by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3624 * scrollable_mask: Dispatch the overscroll steps of the scroll claim test within one frame by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3648 * resizable: Read active drag state when mouse events arrive by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3629 * input: Lay out tabs to tab stops in the code editor by @claudio-azevedo in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3622 * input: Ask scroll containers to reveal a focused input by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3625 * text: Release the shaped text of dropped text views at the next frame by @TastyCZ in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3628 * locales: Add Brazilian Portuguese (pt-BR) by @sergiobalecho95 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3638 * color_picker: Round hex channels instead of truncating them by @boboshan in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3640 * scroll: Ignore rounding in the part of a packet the text did not use by @Tryanks in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3632 * scroll: Throw the edge on release only when it is stretched by @Tryanks in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3631 * dialog: add centered() to seat a dialog in the middle of the window by @mastro993 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3635 * input: Let an editor supply its own scrollbar by @boboshan in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3644 * color_picker: Keep the first hex ### 详细说明文档 https://cat-catch.94cat.com/  ## 2.7.4 **[Updated]** 读取剪贴板权限改为可选，设置 - 替换标签 打开 **[Updated]** Change clipboard read permission to optional, Settings - Replace tags enabled  ## 2.7.3  **[test]** 新增测试项目`边下边存 测试` 支持非firefox浏览器，不经过浏览器下载api 直接保存到指定目录。 **[Added]** 增加 显示分组 (设置 - 其他设置 - 显示分组) [详见文档](https://cat-catch.94cat.com/docs/settings#%E6%98%BE%E7%A4%BA%E5%88%86%E7%BB%84) **[Added]** 增加 External API 其他扩展可获取数据 [详见文档](https://cat-catch.94cat.com/docs/extension-api) **[Added]** 增加 `${clipboard}` 标签，**为此添加读取剪贴板权限，需重新开启扩展**。从剪贴板获取内容，测试中谨慎使用，遇到问题请反馈。[详见文档](https://cat-catch.94cat.com/docs/tag) **[Fixed]** 修复 `缓存捕捉` 在某些网站无法正常工作 **[Fixed]** 修复 `深度搜索` 导致某些网站无法显示 **[Fixed]** 修复 `${userAgent}` 留空未能获取浏览器userAgent **[Updated]** 获取更多请求头，确保下载成功率 **[i18n]** 增加法语 感谢 @zhr0913  https://github.com/xifangczy/cat-catch/compare/2.7.2...2.7.3  <details> <summary>[English]</summary>  ### documentation https://cat-catch.94cat.com/  **[test]** Added test item `Download and Save Test`, supports non-Firefox browsers, saves directly to the specified directory without going through the browser download API. **[Added]** Added Display Groups (Settings - Other Settings - Display Groups) [See documentation](https://cat-catch.94cat.com/en/docs/settings#show-grouping) **[Added]** Added External API so other extensions can retrieve data [See documentation](https://cat-catch.94cat.com/en/docs/extension-api) **[Added]** Added `${clipboard}` tag. Gets content from the clipboard; use with caution during testing, and please provide feedback if you encounter issues. [See documentation](https://cat-catch.94cat.com/en/docs/tag) **[Fixed]** Fixed `Capture` not working properly on certain websites **[Fixed]** Fixed `Search` causing certain websites to fail to display **[Fixed]** Fixed `${userAgent}` being left blank and failing to retrieve the browser userAgent **[Updated]** Added retrieval of special request headers **[i18n]** Added French, thanks to @zhr0913  https://github.com/xifangczy/cat-catch/compare/2.7.2...2.7.3 </details>
```

### 2026-10-26T05:04:29Z [**komari-monitor/komari**](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari)

```
# 1.6.1  ## 主要更新内容  - **优化后台导航**：重新整理侧边栏菜单分组，将运维工具、外观与扩展等入口归类展示，插件自定义页面独立收纳到“插件功能”菜单，减少查找和切换页面的成本。 - **优化插件管理体验**：调整插件列表布局，支持搜索和快捷启停，插件配置可直接在弹窗中修改，减少页面来回跳转。 - **完善插件市场交互**：支持按分类浏览、配合关键词搜索筛选；在市场中即可查看已安装版本与运行状态，并直接完成启停、配置、更新和卸载操作。 - **统一加载体验**：更新加载动画，统一后台页面、登录、终端及文件管理等场景的加载展示，改善操作等待时的视觉反馈。 - **优化历史数据查询**：重构监控指标与 Ping 历史数据的查询链路，优化批量读取、聚合、降采样和索引，减少对象分配与 CPU 开销。 - **插件支持 WebSocket 服务**：插件可通过 `req.upgradeWebSocket()` 接受 WebSocket 连接，实现实时消息收发与交互。 - **JavaScript 运行时升级**：服务端 JavaScript 引擎由 Goja 切换为 MoeJS，同步适配插件、JavaScript 通知及相关内置模块，并允许插件修改自身运行环境中的内建对象。  ## Bug修复  - 修复大量节点集中上报 Ping 结果时，队列容量不足导致部分结果丢失的问题。Ping 队列容量独立调整为 4096，常规上报队列保持 512。相关 [#694](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/issues/694)、[#695](https://github.com/komari-monitor/komari/pull/695) @cristianchiriac - 完善新指标查询与索引结构在 PostgreSQL、MySQL 下的兼容性。  ## 其他  - 更新开源依赖清单与引用说明。 - 同步多语言翻译，完善新增菜单、插件分类及操作提示文案。 - 简化部分服务端逻辑，优化内存分配与资源复用。 - 更新项目说明与赞助信息，感谢 MooProxy 的支持。  **升级提示**：本次更换了 JavaScript 运行时，使用自定义插件或 JavaScript 通知脚本的用户，建议升级后检查脚本运行情况。 
```

### 2026-10-27T22:09:54Z [**AChep/keyguard-app**](https://github.com/AChep/keyguard-app)

```
### New - Export: Added exporting a vault as a KDBX v4 database. - SSH agent: Added an automatic configuration wizard for SSH and GPG agents. - Vault: Added SSH certificates.  ### Improvements - Desktop: Global keyboard shortcuts can now be customized (#1576). - Desktop: Licenses can now be purchased with a web checkout. - Linux: Flatpak builds now support FIDO2 and YubiKey devices. - Vault: Existing tags are now auto-suggested when adding a new one (#1535).  ### Security - Desktop: The agent's auth token is now explicitly wiped when authentication is cancelled.  ### Fixes - Android: Fixed license keys so they grant premium when the Play store is unavailable. - Linux: Fixed desktop SSH agent approval windows so they map correctly on Wayland (#1641, thanks @mvanhorn). - Export: Fixed exporting SSH private keys so they are complete and in their original armor. - SSH agent: Fixed SHA-256 SSH fingerprints so they are emitted unpadded like OpenSSH. 
```

```
<!-- [INPUT]: GitHub #65 设置读取失败、#69 Dota 2 原位翻译回归，与 2.0.3 版本契约。 [OUTPUT]: 发布工作流使用的本次更新说明、玩家操作步骤与现场验收边界。 [POS]: 当前版本交付说明；操作入口交给 README，完整需求与历史验收交给 docs/issues.md。 [PROTOCOL]: 变更时更新此头部，然后检查 CLAUDE.md --> # DeepRant 2.0.3  游戏内嘴臭交流，自动翻译多语言。  - 修复 [#69：Dota 2 原位翻译无法复制](https://github.com/liseami/DeepRant/issues/69)。恢复旧版选择整条聊天的方式，读取原文与准备“翻译中”占位均使用全选，不再依赖行首 / 逐字选择。模型返回后仍只核验当前选区，文字、选区或焦点改变就停止替换。 - Dota 2 使用方法：主页选择 **Dota 2** 场景，输入方式选 **自动** 或 **原位翻译**；在游戏聊天框输入原文后按翻译快捷键，检查译文再自己发送。无需切换输入浮窗。日常文档仍先选中文字并使用日常模式。 - 修复 [#65：设置读取失败导致所有页面报错](https://github.com/liseami/DeepRant/issues/65)。兼容旧模型缓存中的界面状态；真正无法解析的配置可在主窗口确认后，先完整备份设置，再恢复默认值。备份失败不重置，系统凭据库的 Key 保留，界面显示备份位置。 - 三平台安装包与签名更新归档沿用现有验收链；macOS 使用同组织 Developer ID 签名、公证与装订，全部平台校验后公开。  正常设置、个人 Key、自定义提示词、快捷键与原版游戏录屏保留；显式恢复默认值会重置设置，原始文件留在备份中。macOS 需允许辅助功能输入，Windows 应与游戏使用相同权限级别。Dota 2 游戏现场及 #65 报告者配置尚待玩家确认，不把自动回归与安装验收视为游戏实测。 
```

### 2026-10-27T15:30:06Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - New option "remove unused glyphs" in the Fonts section (experimental, off by default): the glyphs the page does not draw are removed from the embedded TrueType, WOFF and WOFF2 fonts. The characters kept are those the page text, the CSS-generated content, the list markers and the `text-transform` values draw, plus their canonical decompositions and compositions, their bidi mirrors and their variation selectors, so a font that shapes them with ligatures or marks keeps the glyphs it needs. A font is left untouched when it is a CFF or a variable font, when it declares a `MATH` table, when the rewritten font is not smaller, or when the browser fails to load it. WOFF2 fonts need Brotli in the browser's Compression Streams, available in Firefox 147 and later; in Chrome they are kept as they are. On the sites tested the saved page shrinks by 3% to 30%. - New `%titlecase` template function for filenames: the first letter of every word is uppercased, the other letters are left as typed (#2026). - Whitespace runs in a filename are collapsed to a single space after the replacement rules are applied, so a removed character no longer leaves a double space behind (#2025). - The labels of the options page start with a capital letter (#2013).  --- ### Fixes  - The filename replacement rules now match characters outside the Basic Multilingual Plane, so a rule written to remove emojis removes every one of them (#2025). - The repair of invalid nesting no longer drops the text between displaced siblings: a sentence built as `<div>` blocks inside a `<p>`, one per word, separated by spaces, was saved without its spaces (#1935). - A page holding a very large amount of text no longer fails with `RangeError: Invalid array length` in the "remove unused fonts" step (#2029). - A stylesheet holding large embedded resources no longer costs a multiple of their size in memory: the `url()` codec of the vendored css-tree built every value one character at a time, and the syntax tree kept every intermediate node alive. Contributed by @pirate in single-file-core#10. - A stylesheet modified by the page's scripts no longer loses the rules under a media or container query whose value Firefox serializes as `infinity * 1px`, the way styled-components writes an open-ended last breakpoint. - A styled XML document no longer hangs the capture. - Text can be selected again in the options page on Firefox (#2027). - The editor configures the path of its zip worker explicitly, so the zip.js 2.8 update no longer makes it fall back to the main thread. 
```

### 2026-10-25T13:14:29Z [**ypq123456789/TrafficCop**](https://github.com/ypq123456789/TrafficCop)

```
## 修复 issue #23：每日推送的「当前使用」一天比一天少  **现象**：设置每日推送后，即使没有流量，推送里的「当前使用」也逐日下降 （客户实测 22.960 → 17.167 → 14.200 GB）。  **根因**：`get_traffic_usage()` 固定用 vnstat 的**小时级**数据累加整个计费周期， 而 vnstat 默认 `HourlyDays=4` —— 小时级记录只保留 4 天，更早的每天被轮转删除。 于是「本周期累计」实际只是**最近 4 天的滚动窗口**：旧日期被删，数字就变小一次。 更严重的副作用是**限速永远触发不了**（4 天的量远小于整月额度）。  真机（Debian 11 / vnStat 2.6）对照：  | 指标 | 值 | |---|---| | 小时级数据实际覆盖 | 仅从 10-21 起（97 条） | | v1.0.92 算出的「本周期」 | **17.5 GB** | | vnstat 原生月累计 / v1.0.93 | **111.3 GB** |  ## 修复  - **按数据覆盖能力选粒度**：小时级（4 天）→ 日级（62 天）→ 月级（25 个月），   取第一个真正覆盖到周期起点的粒度。月度周期走日级，结果与 `vnstat -m` 逐位一致。 - **数据不完整明确告警**：库建于周期中途、或建于起点当天但不是 00:00 时，   标注 `(不完整)` 并写明数据实际开始时刻，不做猜测性补数。 - **取数失败绝不动作**：读不到数据时跳过限速/解限判断、不写快照、不动 tc 规则，   避免一次瞬时读失败被当成「本周期没用量」而解除限速。 - **同周期累计量只增不减**：算出比上次低时沿用旧值并告警；换周期 / 换换算进制 /   换统计模式 / 换网卡 / 骤降过半（疑似库重建）/ 读到 0 时不沿用。  ## 升级  用户脚本从 main 拉取，`trafficcop-manager.sh` → 「12. 更新所有脚本」即可， 或直接下载 `trafficcop.sh` 替换。  > 升级后月度周期的读数会**一次跳到真实值**（如 17.5 → 111.3 GB）—— 这是修正，不是异常。 > 此前被低估的周期不会补算，下一个周期起恢复正常。  ## 验证  - `test_usage_window.sh`（新增，随仓库发布）**68/68 PASS**：三种粒度切换、   各类取数失败（无输出 / `traffic:{}` / 空数组 `[]` / 周期内无记录 / bc 不可用）、   四种统计模式、累计量兜底 9 个分支 - 真机真实数据端到端：111.358 GB 与 `vnstat -m` 逐位一致 - 回归：crontab 原子化 66、日志轮转 52、cron 修复 40、进制默认值 22、状态快照 12 全绿 - CodeRabbit 四轮共 9 条意见，逐条核实全部属实并修复  **完整变更**：https://github.com/ypq123456789/TrafficCop/pull/24 
```
