[#32830](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32830) - 🔏 **Access button respects sharing.** The Access button and menu entry of models, prompts, tools, knowledge, skills and notes and the Access button in folder settings are now hidden from users without the matching sharing permission, who had nothing they could change in its dialog. [#32979](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32979), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ac12b04aada033117f9f7e8ee4fb9562c7d5a417), [#32830](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32830) - 🖇️ **Attach entries without uploads.** When files cannot be attached, because of a missing permission or a selected model without File Upload, the greyed Attach Knowledge, Reference Chats and Attach Notes entries in the + menu no longer open and the # and @ lists leave out what cannot be attached, including pasted links for users without the web upload permission, so a picked item no longer vanishes from the input without a message. [#32968](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32968), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/75c4421b3ab94b1a39d74674475294571bd20bcd), [#32830](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32830) - ⏱️ **Exact last active time.** Hovering the last active time in the admin user list and in a group's member list now shows the exact date and time. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7484c75e163cb17fee9ebcc11e57f462289cc03e) - 🗨️ **New chat from folders.** A folder's menu in the sidebar now has a New Chat item that starts a chat in that folder. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0512f1b3763ea4e15b5adc25d087c96f728fa25e) - 🪪 **Profile cards across the workspace.** Hovering an owner's name in the workspace lists of models, knowledge, prompts, skills and tools and in admin Functions now opens their profile card instead of an email tooltip, with their profile picture next to the name, profile pictures in access settings, version history and the Analytics user list open the card too and the Notes list shows each owner's profile picture. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/02974f41e9b7c9d34f2cd7c664c2a04fd6ac12ae), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/72cc79b8f336a753b6e75e0f3415630601b14759), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ba34b1c47c1d054d2f9c498d0aed862ec06da41a), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4491e60a296c89b11f22a43f416ea04770fd687f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0de8d03c625739ed3fe5e5f2a33de79a760238bd), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0628670f56133d2cdc20c59b06fbb9de36932eb9) - 🔢 **Turn count in chat previews.** The preview shown when hovering a chat in the sidebar now shows how many user and assistant turns its current branch has. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b5046f9d8686e18450a200dccda181f2ac7f97c9) - 🎚️ **Chat Controls saved per setting.** A change in a chat's Controls, such as its system prompt or a parameter, now saves only that setting, so a copy of the chat open in another tab or on another device no longer wipes it with older settings. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b4a58be193031827f2fc19b943646136d1e43575), [#32755](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32755) - 🪃 **Questions survive reconnects.** When the page reloads or loses its connection while Ask User, a tool approval, an MCP server's input request or a function's confirmation or input dialog waits for you, the question now comes back once you reopen that chat within a minute, so the request no longer waits without an answer. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c596d40bd4f554057738ba779b38781a7c2de108) - ✍️ **Note edits by text.** The model can now edit a note by finding and replacing exact text instead of only by character offsets, picking one match or all of them and getting the positions and surrounding text back when a match is ambiguous. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/243ef36c496714d9dd855de9fd261daa43caddb9), [#32752](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32752) - 🔍 **Compare view stays aligned.** Matching rows in the two panes of the Compare view now stay lined up and in place while scrolling, resizing or editing, and a colored bar now marks changed lines also while whitespace or emphasis markers are ignored. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/32170b96fc5edc8c324c5de188be36e9d32059a2), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/596cdbb6caf6022947285722949de1f52272b673), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5c9865a1f119d33ab11d77e79d8c8c8fb93704ec), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/aa8ce52c7a3afbb164bc16a3b7c20e40ba6390ed) - 🖱️ **Clickable setting labels.** Clicking the label of a setting across user and admin settings, including advanced parameters and connections, now toggles its switch, focuses its field or presses its button, and the label highlights while the row is hovered or focused, so the right control is easier to find. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/33e4c5f514d57ca8f654c1015dfb32774b833db6), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/131ddc32ecaeb06f9aa8740ff004710bacef72e0), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b93b81246817b651bb47bb1f5e453503649d30d0), [#32768](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32768), [#32900](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32900) - ☑️ **Select several sidebar chats.** Shift-clicking a chat in the sidebar starts a selection that more clicks or a shift-click range extend, and the selected chats can then be archived or, with the delete permission, deleted together, while Escape clears the selection. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/acc231774d00d8856feefd2e7a3ef4b503a8224e), [#32543](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32543) - 🫲 **Swipe open Chat Controls.** On a phone, swiping left across a chat now pulls in the Chat Controls panel, which follows your finger like the sidebar does and closes again with a swipe to the right. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f83f9f40b3513acc82f53696aaf78a2c5d251e70) - 🗃️ **Bulk file management.** Manage Files in Settings > Data Controls can now download a single file and lets you select several files or all files matching a search to delete them or download them together as one zip archive, with progress and a stop button. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2a86805648f45c36c479c6f9a0127cae799ee14f) - 📥 **Accurate upload status.** A file uploaded to a knowledge base now only shows as done once it is indexed and linked to the knowledge base, so it no longer shows as done and then goes missing from the list until a reload. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f5e8f84e69d0f4224cc27cabd4f8d2c441d4d58c), [#32803](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32803) - ⏳ **Knowledge files show Processing.** A file added to a knowledge base now switches from Uploading to Processing once it reaches the server, and the list keeps itself up to date until the file is ready, so it no longer says Uploading until you reopen the knowledge base. [#32982](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32982), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0a568f6a813d49ca6e7797c7940647782e44ad48) - 🧮 **Knowledge uploads listed at once.** Files selected or dropped together into a knowledge base now all appear as Uploading right away in upload order, so each one no longer waits to appear until the file before it has finished processing. [#32992](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32992), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0c684208bbc6e28e31995c0c4788429b38048db0) - 📈 **Analytics by tokens.** The Admin Analytics dashboard now ranks models and users by tokens by default with a switch back to messages, and its usage chart adds volume and activity views, a single model view, a comparison with the previous period, a choice of time steps and Year to date and Last 12 months ranges. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/131ddc32ecaeb06f9aa8740ff004710bacef72e0), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/06c17834ee3168b922f1b8a935b9dd76884b8fda), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/dc2ac86060e583f7374dd86f32f374a0b956bb8b), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3539ad63213d13865a87a4db64a9829fd7d3b1b4), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/08bfaa4be67f6f87878b2209d568a648818506a7), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/06c382e5d223ba1b35dc0b1306c71da489c632b0) - 🗂️ **File browser grid view.** The terminal file browser can now switch between a list and a grid view from its menu, folders show how many items they hold, and on larger screens hovering a file shows a preview, up to 1 MB for text files and 20 MB for images, audio, video and documents. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a76c02e58ec81621cf3c6ca38eb0d09eccee694f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fefa31fae22a8eb9d39aaf17063373c27fd5a61e), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fa50081e67bfa9883bbc92fd47b20d3f3974c927) - 📁 **File browser folder menu.** A folder's menu in the terminal file browser now has New File and New Folder items that create the file or folder inside that folder. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b6b0fff5d6af2fefb84f8858244adeef50462aac), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fbeee59e99f47c9ff4fb0f114f78eb9dc85f2967) - 📬 **First-message terminal uploads.** With Chat Uploads set to Filesystem on a Per chat terminal, a file attached to the first message of a new chat now waits and uploads into that chat's own terminal when the message is sent, so it no longer lands in the shared workspace. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/049d75e176dd1ef3f2f1eef09d63f30c4e988b3c), [#30245](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/30245) - 🪧 **Folder descriptions.** Folders can now have a description in their settings, shown under the folder title on the folder's page. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f625c37cce4ec971e7d86af6da644fee03561b1e), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/72d86c6b13c1ccb567e2ffa574b3a53a853361b3) - 🚚 **Organize chats in shared folders.** The owner of a shared folder can now rename chats other people started in it and drag them to another folder within the same shared folder. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2262073146bbeda1aa4f998bb61e15ec7f9b6491) - 🛰️ **MCP protocol 2026-07-28.** MCP connections now also work with servers that speak the stateless 2026-07-28 protocol version, while older servers still connect through the earlier handshake. [#33087](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33087), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8151826483163877cec837b35ba7c1affdd1fb5a), [#29778](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/29778) - 📧 **LDAP default email domain.** Administrators can now set a default email domain in the LDAP settings or with "LDAP_DEFAULT_EMAIL_DOMAIN", so LDAP users without a mail attribute sign in with their username at that domain as their email address and into any existing account with that address, which is why the domain should be one you control. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/74fb7a8c56bd92e6603e71e74917a99597228d10), [#33001](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33001) - 🔐 **OAuth connections over the API.** Connections set to OAuth authentication now also send the user's OAuth token when the user calls the API with an API key or bearer token, like tool servers already did. [#32826](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32826), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6367a3cc406b3417d1a2eb2bfde9f1b841d43150), [#32820](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32820) - 🫣 **Hidden base models for groups.** Administrators can now turn on the Hidden Models permission under Models Access in the user and group permissions, or set "USER_PERMISSIONS_WORKSPACE_MODELS_HIDDEN", so those users can pick hidden models as the base model of a workspace model while the hidden models stay out of their model picker. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7f1b618727fe76669158bd42e2ce18fbcca63481), [#29806](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/29806), [#29639](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/29639) - 🧾 **Model usage in audit logs.** With audit logging on and "/chat" removed from "AUDIT_EXCLUDED_PATHS", where it is excluded by default, each chat reply and tool call round now writes its own audit log entry with the model and the input, output and total tokens the provider reported, linked to the request that started it. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/52e155019f511846a8066e012ff37f8aac6db2db), [#30098](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/30098) - 📨 **Voice call user headers.** With "ENABLE_FORWARD_USER_INFO_HEADERS" on, Realtime voice calls now also send the user info headers, or the signed user JWT when configured, to the realtime provider, like chat requests do. [#32733](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32733), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c7e159bdd66a1d05331e6a579e627004bc3c51d1) - 🏎️ **Faster action buttons.** An action button now only saves the chat again when the action changed a message, so actions on large chats finish sooner. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7708ee851a1d6745a5ee3c106a0dd9f728bca732), [#32658](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32658), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b4a58be193031827f2fc19b943646136d1e43575) - 👪 **Faster group saves.** Saving, deleting or changing the members or permissions of a group now finishes in well under a second with thousands of users online, where it took several seconds and reloaded the open chat of everyone online. [#32883](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32883), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b7d1de05ab91150726d83ae427f320a014ddeeec), [#32882](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32882), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/bae611e785df5feabf712ab8c8684a78bf90ec96) - 🧳 **Faster folder changes.** Renaming, sharing, moving or deleting a folder and deleting all chats now finish quickly with thousands of users online, where each took several seconds and reloaded the open chat of everyone online. [#32888](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32888), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/e1e31973f0bfd04dc4195c896eff3476678d2005) - 📣 **Faster channel posts.** Posting a message in a channel with many members online now returns in a fraction of a second, where it waited for every online member's login to be checked again first. [#32889](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32889), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a9adb2b78ebccdd51543a47c285309e9b016a71c) - 📊 **Faster analytics dashboard.** The Admin Analytics dashboard now gets its totals, model and user lists and daily charts from fewer and lighter database queries, so it opens faster on instances with many messages. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/97650bbbea43b98c1c403bf74b08736e9ad522d7) - ♻️ **Prompt cache survives restarts.** Tools from tool servers, Open Terminal included, now reach the model in the same form after every restart, so the provider's prompt cache keeps working and long chats are no longer processed again from scratch. [#33000](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33000), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ee7c9ff5c19d7d8c24c991028b08ff6672c6e653) - 👁️ **PDF image OCR stays light.** With PDF Extract Images (OCR) on, reading text from images in PDFs now uses one processor thread per upload by default, so uploads no longer start a thread pool the size of the server's cores that could slow the whole server down, and administrators can pass their own RapidOCR settings with "RAPIDOCR_PARAMS". [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/56c570afc36e6dae528563059e30b1e030628734), [#32844](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32844) - ♿ **Improved UI accessibility.** Screen reader users now hear the Send button, the sidebar's user menu button by the name it shows, Playground's role button by the word it shows, the model editor's Description, Tools, Skills, Filters and Actions rows by what they show, a sidebar folder's options button, the sidebar button on Calendar, Notes, Channels and Home on phones, the on/off switches on Skills, Functions and Automations rows, the suggested prompts on a new chat as buttons, Archived Chats as a table, the sort order on the column headers of Archived Chats and the Evaluations leaderboard, chat input pills and menus, copy, export, merge and artifact buttons, clear search and Cancel buttons, the note editor's Undo, Redo, Record and Note Menu buttons, Playground's System Instructions button and model selects, the code box in the tool and function editors, switches, icon buttons, selects and number inputs in Admin Settings, the Create knowledge base, Create Prompt and Clone Prompt dialogs and the close button of 22 dialogs by name, together with a source's number, the number on a channel's member count button, the choice a Visibility button shows, a resizable panel's width, how long ago each sidebar chat was updated, the info button under a reply with its usage figures, a model's description, notifications such as copy confirmations (copying a prompt in Workspace included), when a reply starts and finishes, call mode's Listening and Thinking status, menu buttons as menus with their open state, the selected search content type, how many archived chats a search found, how many models match a search in the model picker, the Settings window by name, the sidebar folder toggle by name with its open state, the chosen option in Select dropdowns, the search window's list by its visible caption, a heading on each Workspace page, the sidebar section titles and chat date groups as headings, the sidebar's models, folders and chats as lists, the Settings tabs as Personal and Admin tab lists that point to their panels, the items of the input, integrations, user and chat menus as menu items, the Keyboard tab's shortcut categories as headings with a list under each, the chat input as a text field, the sidebar logo link as New Chat, the onboarding logo by the instance name with other branding images hidden as decoration, the model logos on a new chat by model name (hidden when only one model is selected), the model the arrow keys are on in the model picker, the model name and the Suggested label on a new chat as headings, the suggestion list by name and the chat title as the page heading, and no longer hear an open or closed state on the area around the sidebar button in Channels on phones; keyboard users get the model picker's More and Eject buttons that show on focus, filter lists that move focus to their options when opened, tooltips that show on focus and close with Escape, Escape in the model picker's More menu or filter list that closes only that list, focus that returns to the model picker button when the picker closes with Accessibility Mode on, an outline on the model picker row the arrow keys are on, focus that moves to the other sidebar button when the sidebar opens or closes, arrow keys that move through the input, integrations, user, chat and Terminal menus, a Terminal menu that opens on its first terminal, a Tab key that reaches the note editor's Record and Note Menu buttons, Evaluations leaderboard columns they can sort and models they can open from it, focus that stays on a Workspace section link after choosing it, focus that stays in the Archived Chats list after unarchiving a chat, focus on the recording bar when dictation starts and a Skip to main content link that now moves focus to the page content; notification text in the light theme and the chat dates in Archived Chats now meet the 4.5:1 contrast minimum; buttons in the collapsed sidebar, on sidebar folders, on Prompts, Skills, Functions and Automations rows, on Workspace and Notes list rows, on attached files and in the Integrations menu are no longer nested inside other buttons; the Archived Chats and Keyboard lists stay usable at 400% zoom; and with Accessibility Mode on, pinch zoom works on phones, the active Workspace section is underlined, code editor placeholders, the model editor's JSON Preview row and Calendar's days from other months and today's date meet the contrast minimum, and the More and Eject buttons and Set as default in the model picker, the version link and See what's new in Settings > About, the sidebar's Chat Menu button, the Note Menu and Delete buttons in Notes, the More button on file rows, the Create button's menu arrow, Calendar's previous and next month buttons and the name, ID and command fields in the model and prompt editors are at least 24 pixels in size. - 🔄 **General improvements.** Various improvements were implemented across the application to enhance performance, stability, and security. - 🌐 **Translation updates.** Translations for Korean, Cebuano, Thai, German, Japanese, Vietnamese, Urdu, Bengali, Hungarian, Tibetan, Turkmen, Lithuanian, Danish, Basque, Irish, Slovenian, Estonian, Croatian, Faroese, Georgian, Portuguese (Brazil), Spanish (Spain), Hindi, Finnish, Kabyle, Galician, Uyghur, Slovak, Norwegian Bokmål, Serbian, Ukrainian, Polish, Turkish, Bosnian, Dutch, Czech, Bulgarian, Hebrew, Punjabi, Swedish, Romanian, Persian, Greek, Malay, Latvian, Azerbaijani, Indonesian, Tamil, Filipino and Russian were enhanced and expanded.  ### Fixed  - 🛡️ **Security Advisory**: This release contains **security fixes**. We **strongly recommend updating** all deployments **as soon as possible** to have the latest security fixes in place. These fixes are **not listed in this changelog**; we will publish the security advisories for them immediately following CVE assignment. [Advisories](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/security) - 📤 **Chat export permission respected.** The Export item of a folder in the sidebar, the Export option in Archived Chats and the Copy option in the chat menu are now hidden from users without the Allow Chat Export permission, like the export options of single chats. [#32703](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32703), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a21335bdd44892a804d74614567b2c64a19e0a11), [#32702](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32702), [#32684](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32684), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c9936e81ecb8d50187a9c31d2952353da2b538c8), [#32683](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32683), [#32960](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32960), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b17ee7d83925c87688f308e4599dbd320a017fe4) - 🎛️ **Pipe Valves permission respected.** The Valves button of a pipe model in the chat input is now hidden from users without the Allow Chat Valves permission. [#32772](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32772), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/46cdd70d7be82bbc25952a37fd6f647d7ea627cc), [#32771](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32771) - 👥 **One model without Multiple Models.** A new chat now starts with a single model for users without the Multiple Models permission, where a default or a link with several models kept them all selected. [#32668](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32668), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5d75c1905b79a6a0e9dd505cd1fc2db6a5502849), [#32667](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32667) - 🖊️ **Chat Edit permission respected.** The Edit button of a user's own messages is now hidden from users without the Allow Chat Edit permission, where only the Edit button of replies was hidden. [#32841](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32841), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ce7f87a7505c911da1fab5309692a7329bf0c395), [#32832](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32832) - 📅 **Calendar page respects access.** Opening the Calendar page with Calendar turned off or without the Calendar permission now returns to the home page, where it showed an empty calendar and an error. [#32837](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32837), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ffcb24824f6b4afacb4e36147eb3b701b99441db), [#32834](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32834) - 🔒 **Live access after archiving.** People who see a chat through a shared folder now stop receiving its live updates when the owner archives it or archives all chats, and people with a share link stop when the owner unshares all chats, as they already did when a single chat was unshared or deleted. [#32887](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32887), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ed3cf8355346689d790829074aa7d8cd004b7865) - 🧵 **Thread replies deleted too.** Deleting a channel message now also deletes its thread replies, which stayed behind and could still be found in search. [#32666](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32666), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/54fc21d6855ba4c41c9088c758949d6fcc0ff26f), [#32665](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32665) - 🔗 **Shared chat pages load once.** Chats opened under /s/, through a share link or by an administrator viewing another user's chat, no longer reload over and over and jump back to the bottom, which since 0.12.3 made scrolling up impossible. [#32725](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32725), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c4153a6eeaeb6a0c5ec8cc152a1bdf328b848859), [#32657](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32657) - 🧹 **Deleting the open chat.** Deleting the chat you are viewing from the sidebar now opens an empty new chat, where the deleted chat's messages stayed on screen and the next message failed, in Safari with "The string did not match the expected pattern." [#33069](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33069), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c04e408432b24e2a6cbeb2eaf513b8f2840460b6), [#33068](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33068) - 🪡 **More Concise and Add Details.** Choosing More Concise or Add Details or typing a suggestion under a reply now rewrites that reply, where the model got the instruction without the reply and answered it on its own, for example with "OK, I'll keep answers shorter". [#33084](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33084), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c69e5a73c0de43691e502bfaa03285e4e8cec05d), [#33083](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33083) - 📱 **Sign-in page on small screens.** The sign-in page can now be scrolled when it is taller than the screen, such as on phones with the keyboard open, where parts of the form could be out of reach. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ffbe0ac12da8ca5af9da12f8f00c43e382300b4f) - 🖼️ **Note menu image uploads.** Images picked with Upload files in a note's menu now appear in the note, where they were saved with the note's files but never inserted. [#32793](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32793), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4604f2a3bc2f112135eac75c06048916d3ff37df), [#32792](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32792) - 📜 **Prompt history on unchanged saves.** Saving a prompt without changes no longer adds an identical version to its history. [#32709](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32709), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/312d113fd04bc43996cf97282c34b46ba79c26cd), [#32708](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32708) - 🎨 **Images after tool approval.** Images the model generates after you answer its question or approve or reject a tool call now appear in the reply, where they were created but never shown and the model kept retrying with more hidden images. [#32680](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32680), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/93ba628f3a3cf7f275892f3f3e0375d5984b51a7), [#32678](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32678) - 🧬 **Embeddings with Prefix ID.** The embeddings API now works with models from a connection that has a Prefix ID, where the provider rejected the prefixed model name as unknown. [#32724](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32724), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c901bc39d31eb85af289743850f92848529d422b), [#32715](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32715) - 📞 **Realtime calls answer once.** A Realtime call no longer answers the same sentence twice when a short pause splits it into two turns. [#32773](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32773), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/25b115662396a9e09ee115008f4516aaa2eebd1d), [#32764](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32764) - ⬅️ **Right-to-left question cards.** Question cards from Ask User now follow the Chat Direction setting, so Hebrew and Arabic questions and answers are laid out right to left. [#32784](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32784), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/aee4cbf4639281f711018af08e029e0f501fd3c4), [#32778](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32778) - 🛠️ **Same-named tool functions.** When two attached tools have a function with the same name, the model can now call both, where only the first one could be called. [#32670](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32670), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ab6976b5bf6b1f18149a4f6c8ec4e2d90adec643), [#32669](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32669) - 🧷 **Tool server array parameters.** An array query parameter now reaches an OpenAPI tool server as repeated values instead of one text value that looks like a Python list. [#32677](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32677), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fd1e59b25ff68ff015ce84707839084157df6045), [#32676](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32676) - 📖 **Reading files in pages.** The model's file reading tools now return every part of a file or knowledge base file read in pages with line numbers on, where some text between pages was skipped, and asking for only an end line now reads from the first line up to it, where the model got the same opening part back and kept retrying. [#33155](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33155), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a01b36afe80983ca878b2c4dcdc25f8559a25308), [#33099](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33099), [#33154](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33154), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/d378fe81cb7e6bde2e4766cc5be1e0a45a320d3b), [#33100](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33100) - 📚 **Multi-folder knowledge drops.** Dropping several folders on a knowledge base now uploads all of them, where only the first one was uploaded. [#32653](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32653), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/d907b61816018202d2425109fbf9b506107e7182), [#32652](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32652) - 🙈 **Base models after publishing.** Hiding, toggling or editing a base model right after making it public in Admin Settings > Models is now saved. [#32655](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32655), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/d8bef1ef90391647d2d34dffade63484f70e5c83), [#32654](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32654) - 🧩 **Restored skill-only drafts.** A draft that holds only a skill, brought back to the chat input, now sends only that skill, where the names of every skill were sent. [#32690](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32690), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/14956af03f2ba50a154b13ff77166139fdcc0315), [#32689](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32689) - 🐍 **Python code block results.** Running a plotting block again now shows only the new plot, and a block whose last value is a date, decimal number or other object shows that value instead of "Maximum call stack size exceeded". [#32686](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32686), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1a0bdb38a179ace36419aee87688727a9466a25a), [#32685](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32685), [#32682](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32682), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fb5eec56dd2212d07a77dfb4ed4b25d26ccebcb2), [#32681](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32681) - 🪟 **Preview of an earlier artifact.** Preview on an earlier code block now opens that block's artifact when the artifacts pane was closed, where it opened the newest one. [#32688](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32688), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9862a5bd613bb1c98166bb0eff1c8e824fa86723), [#32687](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32687) - 📎 **Attachments in the chat input.** An attached webpage now stays when file upload is not allowed, and a file's Entire Document switch shows that file's own setting after another attachment is removed. [#32701](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32701), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/bb8d49ff31d3503f91dff2d6e60b4376e021d2f7), [#32700](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32700), [#32699](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32699), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a944564c20a3c207c6987e49d10cc01658e50b67), [#32698](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32698) - 🕸️ **Web pages on fresh installs.** Attached web pages and other HTML text extraction return their text again on fresh installs, where a broken update of a library underneath made them come back empty. [#33103](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33103), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f32de07ddead5453658c828cd7e2c3a1c680934c) - 🔎 **Shared Chats search paging.** A search in Shared Chats or in an admin's view of a user's chats now loads more than the first 60 results. [#32675](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32675), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0f2d1af1ce2092b2d6021823a923ddd179682f52), [#32674](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32674) - 📂 **Open folder stays open.** Deleting another folder in the sidebar no longer closes the folder page you have open. [#32697](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32697), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/db9aa8c9c02f841f4d35f65ee061eddd2e4a57f9), [#32696](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32696) - 📝 **Note pins and counts.** The Notes list now shows Pin for a note unpinned from the sidebar, and readers of a note shared as read only see its word and character counts instead of zero. [#32707](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32707), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6a7205e965072a016c9c3046e1be320921a0af5f), [#32706](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32706), [#32711](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32711), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/252338964223cc75b5d0f02ba9e3cb7f6bd9ab90), [#32710](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32710) - 🪞 **Notes open once.** A note that two people open at the same time, such as one imported from a Markdown or text file and not edited since, no longer shows and saves its text twice, also with several workers. [#33004](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33004), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/499b83fa8d47f592bd7d79cab270d5cfc12652be), [#33002](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33002), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1407f83cfc8df4e727f82beabdd4da6e29fc1119), [#32824](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32824) - 🔊 **Voice from Settings > Audio.** Read Aloud and voice mode now use the voice picked in Settings > Audio, which in Firefox fell back to the default voice. [#32705](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32705), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5edbd12bc9ba29eb0753d9bc888165d23171b0d8), [#32704](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32704) - 🔈 **Read Aloud stops on failure.** Read Aloud now stops requesting the remaining sentences once one fails, so an unavailable speech provider shows a single error instead of one for every sentence. [#33028](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33028), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ea615c3a2e89d827e24016d719acb5ee63f409d2), [#33023](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33023) - 🗄️ **Archived chats survive folders.** Archive All Chats now takes the chats out of their folders, so deleting a folder afterwards no longer deletes those archived chats for good. [#32647](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32647), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/97f5c4e973a703b63a2606b054de324db88f706f), [#32646](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32646) - 🔌 **No hangs after closed pages.** Closing a page while it was still loading, such as a chat export or a file's processing status, no longer loses database connections on SQLite, where later requests could hang or fail with 503 once enough were lost. [#32617](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32617), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2355b8a3699826eaf1fe5a344f63e292f9532c48), [#32609](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32609), [#32741](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32741) - 🧱 **Frontend build on Windows.** Running npm run build or npm run dev on Windows works again, where the encryption module check added in 0.12.3 failed on Windows paths and line endings, and the build now runs with a 12 GB memory limit like the Docker build. [#32735](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32735), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7345c6c31e91b473a4a6b49ec14d37d37cbd8b49), [#32656](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32656) - 🪄 **Function input dialogs answered.** Confirming or cancelling a function's input or confirmation dialog now reaches the function when a script event arrived while the dialog was open, where the function kept waiting for an answer. [#32744](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32744), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2d9852c6048774c0d8913c722b5fe661326f1b4d), [#32742](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32742) - ❓ **Clearer Ask User error.** When a model sends the Ask User questions as something other than a list, such as a JSON string, it now gets an error saying they must be an array of question objects instead of "ask_user requires 1-3 questions", which made models repeat the same call. [#32727](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32727), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/794e57cf7ed29e0bf00a66a825c1d0b9682845b4), [#32662](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32662) - 🛑 **Stop sequences with percent.** A stop sequence containing % no longer stops every message from sending. [#32649](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32649), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fdb304be2f5250c9282bacd3ff91757e55b89976), [#32648](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32648) - 🗜️ **Channel image compression by default.** With image compression on, images in channels are now compressed when Compress Images in Channels is left at its default, where they were sent uncompressed. [#32693](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32693), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/70fb5462eab6b71e258c9fbd9ce5714bd173bcfb), [#32692](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32692) - 🎙️ **Microphone off when leaving.** Leaving a chat while dictating now turns the microphone off. [#32640](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32640), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/636b631ab7cf2f4053c43466468cd4bb119dd250), [#32638](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32638), [#32639](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32639) - ✏️ **Chat titles of only spaces.** Renaming a chat to only spaces now keeps its title and shows an error instead of saving a blank title. [#32770](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32770), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9cbd27011b1d1842f52cbf91b4b5764b5820b52b), [#32769](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32769) - 💬 **Prompts from / in channels.** Picking a prompt from the / menu in a channel now inserts the prompt text instead of a command chip. [#32713](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32713), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1d0b78271910cb5a20e121fd586389906b87dbbe), [#32712](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32712) - 🔤 **No stray formatting spaces.** Chat messages no longer show an extra space at the start of each paragraph and before italic, bold, struck-through or linked text, which appeared in 0.12.3 and left a gap after an em dash. [#32743](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32743), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2bebbb44e1a04b220f831aced950b0e334529988), [#32739](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32739) - 🔁 **FastMCP list results sent once.** List results from tools on servers built with the standalone FastMCP package now reach the model once instead of twice, as text and as structured data. [#32726](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32726), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/217766ed0f53bde167cb80b115f7eb79664ffe5a), [#32719](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32719) - 🪢 **MCP tools with alternative inputs.** MCP tools that describe their input as alternatives, such as a page by ID or by path, now receive the arguments the model sends, where they were called with none and the server rejected the call. [#32903](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32903), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f3f9af594f3c9a20b1f46f846eb8393630733f6c), [#32898](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/32898) - 🩺 **Readable MCP tool errors.** When an MCP tool returns an error, the model now gets the server's error text as written instead of an unreadable dump of the whole result, so it can correct the call where it tended to retry the same broken one. [#32942](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32942), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/37af63b6496d545f6f2d4e8067ea01de209e218b), [#32918](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32918) - 📞 **Ended Realtime calls named correctly.** When the voice provider ends a Realtime call, the call now shows "Voice provider connection closed" instead of sometimes showing "Voice connection failed". [#32730](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32730), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/cd74e6d1266c5a623f9c143d7efa6cb4d6f3f0c9), [#32729](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32729) - 🧭 **Scrolling while a reply streams.** On slow or busy devices a streaming reply now keeps the view at the bottom as its content grows, where it stopped following partway through, and scrolling up while it streams is no longer pulled back down. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5a46aad0f0702536c98c9a26ae7d04482cdac784), [#32728](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32728) - ⚡ **Early updates in new chats.** The first reply of a new chat no longer stays empty with the Stop button showing when its error or content arrives before the page knows the new chat, a message added while a chat is still loading no longer stays missing until a reload, and a message queued during the first reply of a new chat is sent once that reply finishes. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/cbd06d2aad0d64c2a50b4989c4290762de2cd3f0), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/d5038fec44d2f37e81798a6c7f4340a8d1a62f10), [#32550](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32550), [#32761](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32761) - 🧠 **Reasoning replay with Azure.** Reasoning models on Azure OpenAI through the Responses API no longer fail with an "Unknown parameter" error on the request after they think and then call a tool. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/d1705c10788242b4d1a49042318a577877042469), [#32805](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32805) - 📋 **Drafts with variables kept.** Reloading the page with a chat input draft that contains {{ }} placeholders, or editing a queued message that contains them, now keeps the text as it was, where it asked for input variables and dropped the text. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6dbd375c267dd0dafc83c480ec23e3b1a7156406), [#32650](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32650) - 🔑 **OAuth 2.1 static MCP secrets.** MCP connections using OAuth 2.1 (Static) now send the client secret through HTTP Basic authentication when the server does not take it as a form field, where servers such as X Ads that list the public client method first got no secret and rejected the sign-in. [#32818](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32818), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/21f770fb731ff24245bc9ca8c3c82bb4171e24cf), [#32813](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32813) - 🌍 **Web Search settings save.** Admins can now save Web Search settings such as the web loader while web search is turned off, where empty search engine fields blocked saving. [#32840](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32840), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/cc6de63bc876f3c3d8116d5d2858e7887b1997b9), [#32831](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32831) - 🖌️ **Images settings save.** Admins can now save Images settings while Image Generation or Image Edit is turned off, where a missing API key or URL for that feature's engine, such as the OpenAI key on a fresh install, blocked every change on the page. [#32949](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32949), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6b9237f1a1ef28045d36c07eb452e7b76524c19d), [#32835](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32835) - 🌄 **WebP image editing.** Editing a WebP image with Image Edit now works in the Docker image, where the image reached the provider without an image type and was rejected. [#32902](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32902), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f6f42b0111054123d6811270feca35bf9ce6d19d), [#32899](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32899) - 🍴 **No fork in temporary chats.** The Fork chat option is now hidden in temporary chats and the /fork command answers that there is no chat to fork, where forking failed with an error. [#32839](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32839), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1a492207971a5118dde2fe6b523edf7021f38cc9), [#32833](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32833) - 🚀 **First message after loading.** A message sent right after the page loads is no longer wiped by the new chat setup still running, where it disappeared and its reply stayed empty until a reload. [#32829](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32829), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4ed1af6879ee632a3130771b4a960ccf37c1c305), [#32819](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32819) - 📑 **MinerU key in local mode.** Document extraction with MinerU in local mode now sends the configured API key to MinerU 4.x servers that require one, where every document failed. [#32838](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32838), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3e0564d11a06052d8042fffc95b138dcbde5df6b), [#32836](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32836) - 🚫 **Model import reports taken IDs.** Importing models on the Workspace Models page now stops with an error at a model whose ID belongs to another user's model the importer cannot edit or, for non-admins, to a connection model, where that model was skipped without a message. [#32866](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32866), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/cb7d7e851d160bfc866aca9385c8a8013dd10192) - 🎞️ **Left-aligned PowerPoint titles.** The PowerPoint preview now keeps titles and subtitles that the file aligns left on the left, where it centered them. [#32876](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32876), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8fa77c8282b10e7de36863c4df6583a62a55354c), [#32873](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32873) - 🫥 **Multi-model hover previews.** Sidebar hover previews of a chat with replies from several models now show one reply at full width, where every reply was squeezed into the card. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/35b51e09eab0942470cb9dce8f97fb00b9b96f2c), [#32858](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32858) - 🐚 **Launcher help in zsh.** The examples in the Docker Compose launcher help now quote options with square brackets, so they also work when pasted into zsh. [#32881](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32881), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/52f51ea4f3044072d49eefd4feedb859bb66b80c), [#32878](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32878) - 🌡️ **Context gauge uses model threshold.** The context usage gauge now measures against the compaction threshold set on the model or in the default model parameters, the same one compaction uses, where it ignored a lower threshold on the model and showed a chat as far from compaction. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c9415a688ea469144ab2a7796d5eef71947af0b8), [#32821](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32821) - 🏷️ **Tooltips show text as written.** Tooltips now show names, paths and descriptions that contain angle brackets as written, where they came out mangled or partly missing. [#32953](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32953), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3299a097530847ec53da01a5964cf7ef8592dee0) - 📇 **Knowledge file text edits.** The Preview of a text file in a knowledge base now shows its indexed text and refreshes after you save an edit, where it kept showing the original upload, and saving empty content is now refused. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/82d6f230b4e701e85863c01b1992a25a767696bf) - 📡 **Websockets after Redis reconnects.** With the Redis websocket manager and "retry_on_timeout" in "WEBSOCKET_REDIS_OPTIONS", an instance now accepts new browser connections again once Redis is back after its connection dropped, where since 0.12.3 every new connection could hang until the instance was restarted. [#33009](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33009), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0d17660535dd2ad9d6f0b75a2a2f5855066cd0a0), [#33008](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33008) - 🥞 **One model More menu open.** Clicking the More button of another model in the model picker, or clicking another model, now closes the More menu that was open, where the menus stayed open and piled up over the picker. [#33020](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33020), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fa629e5ca5015f6bc3e3be1f7a4a76c5ccd57e18), [#33019](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33019)  ### Changed  - 🔧 **Direct Integrations off disables connections.** While Direct Integrations is off, as it is by default, the tool servers and Open Terminal connections that users and admins saved in their Integrations settings are no longer used in chats, where turning it off only hid the settings tab, so instances that never turned it on lose those connections until an admin turns on Direct Integrations in Admin Settings > Connections or sets "ENABLE_DIRECT_INTEGRATIONS". [#32996](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32996), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3e9d6cb9508a1ba4ec18017b3c80afaf6ac59a89) - 🔀 **Sharing changed only in Access.** Saving a model, prompt, tool, skill, knowledge base, note, channel or calendar no longer changes who it is shared with, which is now changed only in the item's Access settings, saved right away in the model editor, channel settings and external knowledge sources, or through the access endpoints that channels and calendars now also have. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5f95008abdfbbbfbe5c080de0347deb70eb507b6), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c7e34e0fa9bdd34f798ab197e1e21aca2234ed2b) - 🐳 **Python 3.12 Docker image.** The Docker image now runs Python 3.12 on Debian 13 instead of Python 3.11 on Debian 12, so custom functions, tools and packages installed with pip inside the container need versions that support Python 3.12. [#32861](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32861), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a915df8ce12b32109b7b929cb2cad6046293aef6) - 🤝 **Sharing permissions enforced everywhere.** Sharing permissions such as Models Sharing, Knowledge Sharing and Folders Sharing now also apply to sharing through the API, and a change in an item's Access settings that a user is not allowed to make is now refused with an error instead of being quietly left out. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5f95008abdfbbbfbe5c080de0347deb70eb507b6) - 🗄️ **Manage Files shows only yours.** Manage Files in Settings > Data Controls now lists only your own files, also for administrators, who saw every user's files there while "BYPASS_ADMIN_ACCESS_CONTROL" was on, as it is by default. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2a86805648f45c36c479c6f9a0127cae799ee14f) - 🕹️ **Model controls for all users.** Anyone with access to a model can now pick its model control options, such as a Thinking level, and parameters saved to an account are no longer filtered by the Allow Chat Controls and Allow Chat Params permissions, which now only hide the Chat Controls and Advanced Parameters panels. [#32860](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/32860), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/db5b8749448c5cb2a1e42f1bd0c01213bc2c31d1), [#32626](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32626) - 🚦 **Stricter MCP timeouts and redirects.** "MCP_INITIALIZE_TIMEOUT", 10 seconds by default, now also limits listing tools and reading resources on MCP servers, so slow servers may need a higher value, and MCP connections now only follow redirects that stay on the same address. [#33087](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33087), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8151826483163877cec837b35ba7c1affdd1fb5a) - ⌨️ **Shift quick actions removed.** Holding Shift over a sidebar chat no longer shows the quick Archive and Delete buttons, since Shift-click now selects chats, which are then archived or deleted together, and deleting asks for confirmation first. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/acc231774d00d8856feefd2e7a3ef4b503a8224e), [#32543](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32543) - ✉️ **No emails on hover.** Hovering an owner in the workspace lists and admin Functions, or a person in access settings, no longer shows their email address, since the profile card that replaces the tooltip does not include it. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/02974f41e9b7c9d34f2cd7c664c2a04fd6ac12ae), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/72cc79b8f336a753b6e75e0f3415630601b14759), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ba34b1c47c1d054d2f9c498d0aed862ec06da41a) 

### 2026-10-27T17:33:02Z [**gitbutlerapp/gitbutler**](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler)

```
## Work in any branch without `gitbutler/workspace`  GitButler now works in any Git repo, without the need to run a setup or to be on `gitbutler/workspace` ref. All app operations work as normal. GitButler switches to `gitbutler/workspace` only when you apply a second parallel branch and moves back when you return to one.  ## Worktree support  You can now work with linked Git worktrees directly in GitButler. Worktrees created by other apps show up in the CLI status or as lanes in the GUI and support most GitButler operations. For example you (or your agent) can move individual changes, commits or branches to and from worktrees as needed. Parallel branches are light & fast while worktrees offer full code isolation so each tool can be used situationally. Worktrees that already exist when you upgrade start archived. Bring them in from the new Worktrees page in Project settings or with the `but` CLI. Discarding changes in a worktree can’t be undone yet.   ## Changes  - Projects now use a local target branch, so GitButler can be used on repos without a remote - Faster refreshes in Git LFS repositories and after undo, plus faster workspace updates in repositories with many pack files - Commit signing now honors more Git settings, including X.509 signing and `gpg.ssh.defaultKeyCommand` - SHA-256 repositories now support commit signing, rewording, undo and push - Workspace update previews no longer sign temporary commits or disable signing when a preview signature fails - GitButler no longer installs its own `pre-commit` and `post-checkout` hooks - Branches can now be dragged below the last branch in a stack  ## CLI  - `zz` is now `@` for uncommitted changes - `but land` is now `but merge`, and `land` still works as an alias - `but setup` is gone because `but` works in any repository. `but init` replaces `but setup --init` - `but review`, `but branch new --anchor` and ID ranges such as `g0-h2` are removed. Use `but pr`, `--above` and single IDs instead  ### Worktrees  - `but status` lists worktrees with their own IDs and marks ones behind the target - `but worktree` creates, lists, archives, restores and removes worktrees. `but worktree new` can start at a commit with `--above` or on an existing branch outside the workspace - `but pull --update <workspace|worktrees>` picks what to update. A worktree whose branch merged upstream moves to a new branch at the latest target commit - `but move <branch> --above <branch>` moves a branch between a worktree and the workspace  ### New in this release:  - `but split` moves files or hunks out of a commit into a new commit above it - `but diff <commit>` shows hunk IDs, so `but squash`, `but move`, `but uncommit` and `but discard` can work on single hunks of a commit - `but merge` and `but pull` work without a remote, using the local target branch - `but merge --ff-only` refuses merges that can't fast-forward - `but commit`, `but squash`, `but move` and `but split` read the message from a file or stdin with `-F/--file` - `but status` marks the checked-out branch with `[HEAD]` - `b` is a default alias for `but branch`  ## TUI  - You can squash several marked commits into one - You can archive (`a`) and unarchive (`u`) worktrees - Mark committed hunks in the details view to discard, squash or move them - Move a commit's files to another commit, a branch or a new branch - In move mode, `b` moves commits onto a new branch above the selected one - Undo (`u`) works from details mode - The status view scrolls with the mouse wheel, and clicking a row selects it - `but tui` starts faster, especially on Linux - Windows: `?` and `:` now respond  ## Fixes  - Fixes the diff preview jumping to another file when the file list changes - Fixes workspace loading failures after commits made outside GitButler or a concurrent fetch - Failed workspace updates now show the error and a retry button; unrelated target or remote history now comes with recovery guidance - Fixes partial commits leaving Git's index out of sync, and empty commits being created when all selected changes are rejected - Fixes "Create branch below" moving existing commits into the new branch - Fixes undo leaving stray branch names, failing to restore deleted branches, or showing files outside a sparse checkout as deleted - Fixes deleted files reappearing as changes after pulling or applying a branch that also deletes them - Fixes pushing a branch based on `gitbutler/workspace` accidentally publishing unrelated applied branches - GitHub and GitLab: Authentication, access and rate-limit errors now explain how to recover and stop repeated background failures - GitHub: Fixes merging native stacked pull requests and loading CI checks for pull requests from forks - Fixes self-hosted forge detection and repository URLs containing `.git` within their names - Network operations now time out when the remote stops responding; failed Git hooks now show their error output - Git changes now appear promptly while builds or dev servers keep writing files - Windows: Commits and amendments now preserve executable permissions - macOS: Newly created symlinks, hard links and APFS clones now appear promptly  ## New Contributors * @bzbetty made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16136 * @juanibiapina made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16324 * @RaphaelFakhri made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16406 * @ZainnQureshii made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16041  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/compare/release/0.22.3...release/0.23.0  ## Downloads  Download bins from https://gitbutler.com/ 
```

```
## What's Changed  ### ✨ Features - **UDP Mode:** Add native `ControlMaster` multiplexing support in UDP mode by @soupdiver in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/284 - **Zmodem:** Support Zmodem auto-rename and transfer summaries. - **Auto-Upload:** Support allowed auto-upload directories for `trzsz`. - **UI:** Add `PromptSearchPointer` configuration option to customize the search cursor style (#272).  ### 🐛 Bug Fixes - **Configuration:**   - Fix `ssh -G` effective config loading and caching bugs (#285).   - Fix Windows path parsing for file configuration options (#285).   - Fallback to canonical destination when `HostName` is empty (#273). - **Known Hosts:**   - Automatically create missing user `known_hosts` file and directory (#285, #286).   - Ignore malformed `known_hosts` entries by @eejd in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/274 - **Session & Execution:**   - Fix command pipe handling.   - Fix session exit status and suppress normal exit warnings.   - Handle shell EOF gracefully to align with OpenSSH behavior (#275).   - Fix control master option passed to `ssh` (#279). - **SSH & Networking:**   - Fix: support `@` in SSH usernames by @SomSamantray in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/288   - Keep `%C` hash consistent with OpenSSH when a proxy jump is used by @Mrkinggggg in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/291   - Add the root KSK-2024 trust anchor (key tag 38696) for DNSSEC validation by @CyberNativeAI in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/290 - **UDP Preview:**   - Correctly calculate display width and clip text containing ANSI styles in attach previews.  ### 🚀 Maintenance & Improvements - **Go Upgrade:** Upgrade CI, release builds, and dependencies to **Go 1.26**. - **Token Alignment:** Align `%j` and `%C` token expansion with modern OpenSSH versions. - **Power Management:** Pause UDP reconnects when macOS graphics are inactive (Dark Wake support).  ## New Contributors * @eejd made their first contribution in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/274 * @SomSamantray made their first contribution in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/288 * @CyberNativeAI made their first contribution in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/290 * @Mrkinggggg made their first contribution in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/291 * @soupdiver made their first contribution in https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/pull/284  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/trzsz/trzsz-ssh/compare/v0.1.26...v0.1.27
```
